# System Risk Register

Status: Active

Owner role: tracked queue for material security, data, PIT, schema, execution, and cross-LLM process risks that are not fixed immediately in the same reviewed slice.

This file exists to prevent audit findings from living only in chat. `docs/SESSION_LOG.md` records reasoning and review verdicts; this register records the durable open-risk queue. Do not use it as authorization to batch-fix everything at once. Each fix still follows `docs/AI_REVIEW_PROTOCOL.md`.

## Enforcement Rules

- Every material review or audit finding that affects data integrity, PIT safety, schema contract, execution simulation, security, ship-gate evidence, or cross-LLM continuity must be either fixed in the same reviewed slice or entered here before the round ends.
- `执行` must check this register before choosing the next task. Open P0 items outrank normal roadmap work unless the user explicitly approves a narrower override.
- `审查` must verify that new material findings were either fixed or registered here, and that resolved entries include concrete verification.
- `修复` may update this register only for approved Required fixes or Optional dispositions being handled in that repair round.
- A risk entry is not closed by intent. Closure requires file / test evidence and a reviewed commit or an explicit accepted-risk decision from the user.

## Status And Severity

Severity:
- P0: blocks the next unsafe execution path or can contaminate evidence / official outputs.
- P1: high risk before broader implementation, provider work, long-lane work, or production-like use.
- P2: medium risk; must be queued and fixed before the affected subsystem is promoted.
- P3: low risk or documentation hygiene.

Status:
- `open`: accepted for tracking; not fixed.
- `in_progress`: current reviewed change is addressing it.
- `blocked`: needs user decision or external dependency.
- `resolved`: fixed and verified.
- `superseded`: replaced by more specific open / resolved entries; the underlying risk is not fixed merely because the parent summary was split.
- `accepted_risk`: user explicitly accepted the residual risk.
- `needs_revalidation`: audit claim is plausible enough to track, but line-level repo validation is still required before implementation.

Remaining material loopholes: (1) `R-ASHORT-GAP42-FORWARD-EVENTS-ROW-LANDING-GUARD-GAP` still includes a validator/landing gap beyond the manual-review-only holding case: a weekly artifact can contain a checked event for a `reports[]` code, skip `_attach_forward_event_impacts()`, and still pass `validate_weekly_report()` with `machine.operation_impact` absent. This means row no-dangling is done by the current `main()` order, not enforced by the consumer validator. Required repair: make `validate_weekly_report()` or an equivalent write-time guard reject checked `upcoming_events` events that have no explicit landing in the matching report row or manual-review holding surface; keep global calendar as an index, not the only landing. (2) `R-ASHORT-GAP42-FORWARD-EVENTS-PARTIAL-UNKNOWN-GAP`: `_upcoming_events()` returns `status="checked", events=[]` when one code successfully returns `[]` and another code fails/returns `None`. Required repair: add per-code coverage to the schema/render (for example `unchecked_codes` / `coverage_status=partial`) or fail the whole object to `unknown_or_unavailable` whenever any required code fails; add tests for all-fail, partial-fail-with-events, and partial-fail-empty so the report never says all checked when some codes failed. (3) `R-ASHORT-GAP42-FORWARD-EVENTS-SOURCE-GUARD-GAP`: `validate_operation_impact_no_dangling()` has semantic-source isolation but no forward-event-source isolation. A generated `forward_event_limit_unlock` impact can be mutated to `veto_class="production_hard_veto"` and `production_effect_enabled=true` while keeping `new_entry_effect="manual_review"` and `操作="建仓"`; `validate_weekly_report()` still PASSes. Materiality: 4.2 explicitly says forward_events first version is `production_effect_enabled=false` and cannot become hard veto/rescue. Required repair: add source-specific guard for `source_field=="forward_event_limit_unlock"` (or a generic nonproduction source allowlist) enforcing `field_class=="structured"`, `production_effect_enabled is False`, `veto_class=="none"`, `new_entry_effect!="hard_veto"`, `holding_effect in {"none","hold_watch"}`, and no production evidence/write path; add mutation tests. Optional optimizations (non-blocking after Required): carry `float_ratio/float_share` from `share_float` so small unlocks can be labeled differently from large unlocks; de-duplicate `code_names` before provider calls; later add financial-report appointment date as a separate slice after feasibility, not in this repair. Finding 1 (row landing / no-dangling gap): desktop `4.2.md` lines 276-281, 345, 741, and 770 require forward events to be analysis-only but still land in M6.7 as candidate/holding row impact: candidates can get `manual_review` / `priority_down` / `sizing_down` / `observe_required`; holdings can get `hold_watch` / `blocked_add_required` / `reduce_review` text; M6.7 landing includes `upcoming_events`, `风控触发`, `操作建议`, and when applicable `股数`. The implementation only sets `weekly["upcoming_events"]` as a global weekly object (`runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` around `_exdiv_codes` / `_upcoming_events`) and renders one global Markdown section (`runners/a_short_m67_render.py` upcoming-events block). It does not attach the event to the affected `reports[]` row, does not create row-level `operation_impact`, and does not update the affected row's user-visible `风控触发` / `操作建议`. Independent Codex probe injected a valid `limit_unlock` for `600000.SH`; `validate_weekly_report()` accepted the weekly report, but the 600000.SH row had `row_operation_impact_sources=[]`, no `upcoming_events` / `limit_unlock` / `解禁` text in the row, `风控触发=="无"`, and `操作=="建仓"`. Materiality: the calendar is visible as a side table but is disconnected from the exact M6.7 decision row the user reads, so the field is dangling for the row-level risk/action contract.

Required repair for Finding 1: preserve the global `upcoming_events` index if useful, but also consume each checked event into the affected candidate/holding row's M6.7 surface. Minimum acceptable behavior: candidate rows with near `limit_unlock` show a user-visible event warning and an analysis-only row impact (`manual_review` / `observe_required` / `priority_down` / `sizing_down` as the reviewed mapping chooses) without becoming a production hard veto; holding rows show holding-scope warning text such as `hold_watch` / `blocked_add_required` / `reduce_review` text only, with no automatic sell/reduce action before S3b. The repair must keep `production_effect_enabled=false`, must not change EGS selection, TopN, production `result/a_short`, historical backtest, broker/order behavior, or price formulas. Add adversarial tests proving a valid event is visible on the exact candidate row and on the exact holding row, not only in a global section.

Codex review finding for A-short 4.2 Round 2 `exclusion_summary` contract (2026-06-17; status `resolved` @2d2ac28; severity `P2`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND2-EXCLUSION-SUMMARY-CONTRACT-GAP`; scope reviewed: modified `docs/SESSION_LOG.md`, `runners/a_short_m67_render.py`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; review-record edits also touch `docs/system_risk_register.md`; excluded untracked byproduct `research/results/a_short/iv_feed_20260605/iv_feed.json`; PIT label `A-short 4.2 Round 2 / batch_exclusion / exclusion_summary / evidence-artifact lineage + no-dangling completeness`): review verdict FAIL. Materiality: desktop `4.2.md` requires batch exclusions to land in weekly `exclusion_summary` with count, reason, stage, `m67_text`, privacy routing, and evidence / artifact reference; this is a schema contract and PIT traceability surface for risks that have no M6.7 row. The current weekly schema requires only `as_of`, `total_excluded`, `by_reason`, and `m67_text`; `source` is optional free text. `_build_exclusion_summary()` emits only `source="analysis_input.universe_summary.excluded_counts"`, not a parseable artifact / lineage ref, and `validate_weekly_report()` does not check any evidence or artifact reference. Codex probe built a weekly object with no `source`, no `artifact_ref`, and no `evidence_ref`; both `jsonschema.validate(...a_short_weekly_report...)` and `validate_weekly_report()` passed (`MISSING_EVIDENCE_ACCEPTED`). Same defect class: `analysis_input.schema.json` allows arbitrary `excluded_counts` keys, while desktop `4.2.md` scopes Round 2 to L0/L2/Stage3 and other upstream production hard risks. `_build_exclusion_summary()` maps only four current L0 keys and silently ignores any other nonzero count. Codex probe `_build_exclusion_summary({"unlock": 1, "stage3_policy_veto": 4}, "20260609")` returned `total_excluded=1` and only `share_float_unlock`, so an unmapped upstream reason can disappear while the report still validates. Privacy note: the current emitted content is counts-only and does not by itself leak account holdings; this finding is the missing evidence/artifact guard and completeness/no-dangling gap, not a secret leak. Required repair: add a parseable evidence/artifact contract and guard for `exclusion_summary` (for example top-level `artifact_ref`/`evidence_ref` tied to `weekly.run_lineage.analysis_input`, or per-reason evidence refs); schema must require it, and `validate_weekly_report()` must reject missing/empty/unparseable refs and stale/mismatched lineage. Add a completeness guard for `excluded_counts`: either map every present nonzero key to a reason record with reviewed `stage`/`veto_class`/`pit_basis`/`privacy_class`, or fail closed on unknown nonzero keys until EGS emits reviewed metadata. If Round 2 is intentionally limited to the current four L0 keys, make that boundary explicit in schema/validator/docs and reject or route unknown nonzero keys instead of dropping them. Add regression tests for missing evidence/artifact, empty/bad lineage, and unknown nonzero count. Boundary: do not fetch data, do not implement Round 3 semantic, S3b, new providers, production scoring/selection, broker/order behavior, or commit; keep `research/results/a_short/iv_feed_20260605/iv_feed.json` excluded. Verification performed by Codex before FAIL: weekly suite 139 OK with local `tushare` stub; doc-governance+route-doc 30 OK; two mutation probes reproduced the guard/completeness gaps; `git diff --check` clean (CRLF warnings only). Closure requires Claude repair, Codex re-`审查` PASS, and reviewed commit. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-17)**: 判定 Required 两面全对,逐修。(1) **完整性 fail-closed**:`_build_exclusion_summary` 先扫 `excluded_counts` 全键,任何 `count>0` 的未映射键(不在 `_EXCL_REASON_META` 的 4 个 L0 键)→ `raise ValueError`(绝不静默丢);Round2 的 4-键边界即此映射表,新上游原因须先映射 stage/veto_class/pit_basis 才能进摘要;`count==0` 的未知键无害放行。(2) **evidence/lineage**:weekly schema `exclusion_summary.evidence_ref` 入 `required` + 定形状(`kind`∈`lineage_key|artifact_path`、`value` `minLength 1`、`as_of` `^[0-9]{8}$`),去掉旧 free-text `source`;builder 产 `evidence_ref={kind:lineage_key, value:"analysis_input.universe_summary.excluded_counts", as_of}`;`validate_weekly_report` 加:evidence_ref 缺失/坏 kind/空 value → raise、`evidence_ref.as_of != 周报 as_of`(陈旧)→ raise、`run_lineage.analysis_input` 空(无源 artifact lineage)→ raise。对抗测试:fail-closed 未知非零键 raise(+ count0 无害)、schema+validator 缺 evidence_ref 拒、evidence as_of 漂移拒、缺 lineage 拒;旧测试经 `_weekly_excl` helper 设源 lineage。Scope `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`、`runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`、`tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`。Verify: weekly 套件 **144 OK**(139+5);全量 **2192 OK**(零回归);doc-governance+route-doc 30 OK;py_compile OK;无 BOM;`git diff --check` 干净。反向:正常发射(4 键、as_of/evidence 对齐、有源 lineage)仍过(`test_schema_and_validator_accept`),未误拒合法态。Boundary held:未碰取数/Round3/S3b/价格/选股/券商;`iv_feed_20260605` 仍排除。Closure on Codex re-`审查` PASS + 用户 `提交`。

Codex review finding for A-short 4.2 Round 1 gap-data registry + `reduce_deduct` operation_impact (2026-06-17; status `resolved` @c5b2ce81 — folded into RESOLVED summary at top of Hot Queue; severity `P2`; parent Required ID `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-OPERATION-IMPACT-GUARDS`; Required IDs `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-HOLDING-SCOPE-DRIFT`, `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-EVIDENCE-REF-GUARD-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-VISIBILITY-SHAPE-GUARD-GAP`; scope reviewed: `git status --short --untracked-files=all` showed modified `docs/README.md`, `docs/SESSION_LOG.md`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `schemas/a_short_m67_report.schema.json`; untracked intended files `presets/a_short_gap_data_operation_impact_governance_20260617.json`, `schemas/a_short_gap_data_field_registry.schema.json`, `schemas/a_short_gap_data_operation_impact_governance.schema.json`, `schemas/examples/a_short_gap_data_field_registry.example.json`, `tests/test_a_short_gap_data_registry.py`; untracked excluded byproduct `research/results/a_short/iv_feed_20260605/iv_feed.json`; PIT label `A-short 4.2 Round 1 / operation_impact / field-level traceability / no-dangling guard`): review verdict FAIL. (1) `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-HOLDING-SCOPE-DRIFT`: Round 1 is scoped in desktop `4.2.md` to the candidate-row `negative_event` path only, with holding-effect fields explicitly text-only/future-S3b. But `build_m67_report()` emits the new `holder_reduction_deduct_30d` `operation_impact` for held positions too, setting `visibility_shape="holding_row_impact"`, `impact_scope="existing_holding"`, `new_entry_effect="hard_veto"`, `holding_effect="none"`, `terminal_surface_target="already_structured"`, and `pending_successor_slice=null`. Codex probe using `_good_input(stateful_risk=_held_state(), event.holder_reduction_active=true)` returned `position_state=held`, `table.操作=否决`, the above impact, and `validate_m67_consistency()` PASS. This overclaims that holding impact is already structurally landed, even though 4.2 says `holding_management_signal` / `持仓处置` / reduce-clear prices belong to S3b. Required repair: keep Round 1 emission to non-held candidate-row impacts only, or encode any held-position trace as current text-only/future-S3b with the correct holding fields and successor slice; add a held-position regression so this cannot pass silently. (2) `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-EVIDENCE-REF-GUARD-GAP`: 4.2 §10.2 and §15.1 require every operation impact to carry parseable evidence, and this slice's stated purpose is field-level traceability. The current m67 schema does not require `evidence_ref`, and `validate_operation_impact_no_dangling()` does not check it. Codex probe deleted `machine.operation_impact[0].evidence_ref`; both `jsonschema.validate(...a_short_m67_report...)` and `validate_m67_consistency()` still passed. Required repair: require `evidence_ref` in the m67 operation-impact schema and runtime guard; at minimum enforce `kind` in `artifact_path|lineage_key|source_id`, non-empty `value`, and matching `as_of` or an explicitly justified no-date form; add a deletion/empty-value adversarial test. (3) `R-ASHORT-GAP42-ROUND1-VISIBILITY-SHAPE-GUARD-GAP`: `operation_impact` is the row-level path; 4.2 sends upstream filtered/no-row risks to weekly `exclusion_summary`. The m67 schema currently allows `visibility_shape` values `batch_exclusion`, `summary_only`, and `out_of_scope` inside row-level `machine.operation_impact`, and the runtime guard does not reject them. Codex probe changed the emitted row impact to `visibility_shape="batch_exclusion"` with weekly landing/terminal surfaces; both schema and guard still passed. Codex second re-review correction for `R-ASHORT-S3A-HOLDING-LEVELS-MISSING-MANUAL-STOP-ADVICE` (2026-06-16; status remains `open`; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::validate_m67_consistency`, `tests/test_a_short_phase5_engine.py`; PIT label `A-short M6.7 / S3a holding advice honesty guard / state-bound validator gap`): Claude's second repair adds a validator check for the exact phrase pair `无手填参考止损` + `请按手填参考止损`, plus a planted regression for that phrase pair. This is progress but still not the Required guard. The validator is not bound to the actual machine state (`action=="持有"`, `plan is None`, account/manual reference absent) and it still does not enforce the design-required executable-stop wording for plan-present/manual-fallback paths. Codex adversarial probes show two live false negatives: (1) a no-system-level/no-manual-stop report can use `系统止损未算出...请按手填参考止损盘中无条件手动执行。` while simply omitting `无手填参考止损`, and `validate_m67_consistency` passes; (2) a plan-present hold report can say `系统跟踪止损 69.38` while omitting both `无条件` and `盘中`, and `validate_m67_consistency` passes. Materiality: a future refactor can still output an instruction to execute a nonexistent manual stop, or strip the unconditional/manual execution caveat from a real stop, while M6.7 claims consistency. Required repair: implement the state-bound guard, not a fixed-string pair only. Minimum required behavior: for `action=="持有"` and `plan is None`, if the account/manual reference is absent, reject any advice that tells the user to execute a hand-filled reference stop and require an explicit no-executable-stop phrase such as `无可执行止损位`; if a manual fallback exists, allow the manual-fallback execution wording but do not also claim there is no manual reference. For `plan is not None`, require advice to contain `止损` and at least one execution caveat token (`无条件` or `盘中`). Add adversarial regressions for the two false negatives above; the existing exact-phrase regression can remain but is not sufficient. Codex finding for A-short holdings S3a implementation review (2026-06-16; status `resolved` (committed `d79174e` local master 2026-06-16, Codex re-`审查 PASS` + user `提交`); severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::build_m67_report`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::build_holding_report`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::validate_m67_consistency`; PIT label `A-short M6.7 / S3a holding system levels / missing data no manual stop`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-S3A-HOLDING-LEVELS-MISSING-MANUAL-STOP-ADVICE`): S3a correctly tries to compute holding stop/take-profit from system data, but when `holding_levels` returns `plan is None` and the account position has no manual `stop_loss`, both report paths still say to execute the hand-filled reference stop (`请按...手填参考止损盘中无条件手动执行`) while the same advice also says there is no hand-filled reference. Strengthen `validate_m67_consistency` for hold rows so missing-level/no-manual-stop advice cannot pass, and so normal hold advice preserves the required unconditional/manual stop wording. Add regressions for `plan is None` with and without manual `stop_loss`. Boundary: no S3b active reduce/add/sell, no broker/order automation, no data fetch, no EGS/selection change.

Codex finding for A-short M6.7 price-calculation roadmap proposal design review (2026-06-16; status `resolved` (design-gate cleared; local master 2026-06-16, Codex re-`审查 PASS` + user `提交`; design complete, implementation = follow-up slices); severity `P2`; scope `docs/a_short_m67_price_calc_optimization_proposal.md`, `docs/a_short_holdings_s3_system_levels_design.md`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::exit_and_size`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::validate_m67_consistency`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `A-short M6.7 / price execution levels / side-aware tick / entry range / cash allocation / validator contract`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-M67-PRICE-ROADMAP-DESIGN-EXECUTABLE-CONTRACT-GAPS`): Codex design-layer review verdict = FAIL. The roadmap direction is correct (system-level permanent price logic, 0.01 side-aware tick, worst-case entry price, global cash allocation, no-dangling), but three design gaps must be closed before implementation. (1) Post-tick invariants are not explicit. The proposal correctly says stop rounds up and take-profit / buy-high round down, but it does not require revalidating the rounded execution prices before emitting a buildable plan. Required repair: state that all build decisions and RR checks use the final ticked execution prices, then fail/observe if any post-tick invariant breaks (`stop < entry_for_risk`, `t1 > entry_for_risk`, `t2 >= t1`, finite positive risk, `rr_at_entry_high >= rr_floor`). Add adversarial tests where a raw-valid plan becomes invalid only after tick rounding. For S3a holdings, rounded stop crossing current price must become the documented breached/无法精算 path rather than a normal take-profit plan. (2) The low-buy entry range is under-specified: `entry_high 取上沿(用 max 口径修退化)` is not a deterministic formula. Required repair: write the exact low-buy `entry_high` formula, its raw fallback, and its post-tick fallback when `entry_low > entry_high`; tests must lock the formula. (3) Cash-allocation zero-share transition is incomplete. The proposal says `allocated_shares==0 → table 转观察`, but existing validator requires `m67.table.操作 == machine.entry_exit_size_star.action` and non-build table fields null. Required repair: specify the full state transition: if allocation turns a raw build into observe, update `machine.entry_exit_size_star.action` and `m67.table.操作` together, clear/null user execution fields unless a new explicit observation-reference schema is added, preserve raw plan only as diagnostic/audit data, and add tests proving `validate_m67_consistency` passes and no `建仓` row has null/0 shares. Boundary: design/doc only; no data fetch, no S3b active actions, no broker/order integration, no automatic add/reduce/sell; keep `research/results/a_short/em_probe_smoke_20260614/*` excluded. **Claude 设计 `修复` (2026-06-16)**: judged all 3 Required correct (design-doc only). (1) post-tick invariants — §2.1 added: tick = final execution price; all build/RR checks use ROUNDED prices; after side-aware tick, revalidate `risk>0 finite / stop<entry_for_risk / t1>entry_for_risk / t2>=t1 / rr_at_entry_high>=rr_floor`, any break → 建仓 转观察, S3a holding rounded-stop>=close → breached path; adversarial test (raw-valid, tick-invalid) required. (2) low-buy entry_high — §3 now exact: `entry_ref=close, entry_low=max(support, close−0.5×ATR), entry_high=close` (range [entry_low, close], non-degenerate while support<=close) + raw fallback (support>close → single point) + post-tick fallback (entry_low>entry_high → single point); tests lock it. (3) zero-share transition — §4 now full: set machine.action AND table.操作=观察 together, null 入/损/盈/股数 per validator observe rule, keep raw only in machine.plan.diagnostic_raw (not execution price), advice explains, never a 建仓 row with null/0 shares; test proves validate_m67_consistency passes. Boundary: design/doc only, no code/S3b/data/broker, em_probe excluded. Verify: doc-governance+route-doc 30 OK; git diff --check clean. **Codex re-`审查` PASS (2026-06-16)**: independently verified the three design gaps are closed in the proposal: post-tick execution prices are revalidated and raw-valid/tick-invalid cases must observe or breach; low-buy `entry_high` is deterministic (`close`) with raw and post-tick fallback; zero-share cash allocation updates machine action and table action together, nulls execution fields, and keeps raw plan diagnostic-only. Verification: proposal/design/code-contract review; doc-governance+route-doc 30 OK; `git diff --check` clean (CRLF warning only). Closure now requires Claude `提交`; `em_probe_smoke_20260614` stays excluded.

Claude self-discovered finding for A-short account-bearing weekly M6.7 output privacy leak vector (2026-06-16; status `resolved` (committed `155e3ad` local master 2026-06-16, Codex re-`审查 PASS` + user `提交`); severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `.gitignore`, `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py`, `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md`; PIT label `A-short holdings-in-M6.7 / privacy / account output routing / git-leak surface`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-HOLDINGS-WEEKLY-ACCOUNT-OUTPUT-PRIVATE-ROUTING`): surfaced while running S1 with the user's real account. `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` hardcodes the M6.7 output to `research/results/a_short/<as_of>/weekly_m67.json`, a **git-tracked** lane (only `research/results/a_short/m67_validation_*/` is gitignored). When run with `-Account`, the weekly report embeds real holdings (`ts_code`/`avg_cost`/`shares`/`stop_loss`), so every account-bearing weekly run lands the user's private holdings in a committable file; a single `git add` would leak them. A direct `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py --account ... --out <tracked path>` call (exactly the shape used in the S1 validation run) had the same exposure with no guard. Materiality: project privacy posture requires real holdings to never be committed (memory + AGENTS.md git-privacy); this is a standing leak surface that triggers on **normal** weekly use, not an edge case. Fix (this slice, 固化): (1) `.gitignore` add `state/*/weekly_private/` (scheme covers a_short/a_long/us_short/us_long); (2) `weekly_screening.ps1` routes `-Account` output to gitignored `state/a_short/weekly_private/<as_of>/`, observation-only stays at the standard research lane (committable evidence, no holdings); (3) pipeline hard guard `_reject_nonprivate_account_output_path` — `--account` + `--out` **inside repo and not git-ignored** → fail-fast `SystemExit` before any fetch/write; repo-external paths (temp/external drive) allowed (git can't commit them); `--allow-nonprivate-account-out` explicit override; (4) tests + design doc §8. Boundary: no change to `egs_main`/top-N/EGS scoring/engine action/semantic/observation-only routing/existing `_reject_production_output_path`. Verify: holdings + weekly pipeline tests **128 OK** (the ~25 existing `--account`+tmp-`--out` tests still pass because temp dirs are repo-external); guard unit tests cover inside-nonprivate-refuse / private-ok / outside-ok / no-account-ok / override-ok / main-fail-fast. Closure requires Codex `审查` PASS + reviewed commit; flip to `resolved` with hash on commit. **Codex `审查 FAIL` (2026-06-16)**: ps1 routing correct, but the pipeline guard used a path-name heuristic (`'/weekly_private/' in out.lower()`) that does not equal gitignore truth — bypassable by (a) an in-repo **fake** `weekly_private` not covered by `state/*/weekly_private/` (e.g. `research/.../weekly_private/`, nested `state/<x>/sub/weekly_private/`) and (b) case variants; probe confirmed both were allowed. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-16)**: judged correct. Replaced the substring heuristic with **git ground truth** — new `_is_account_output_git_ignored()` runs `git -C <repo_root> check-ignore -q -- <out>`; guard refuses iff `--account` and out path is inside repo AND `git check-ignore` does NOT match (git unavailable/error → fail-closed = treat as not-ignored). Repo-external paths still early-return (allowed). Net: guard-accepted ⟺ (outside repo) OR (actually git-ignored), so fake/nested/case-variant `weekly_private` follow git's real per-platform decision. Regressions: `test_fake_weekly_private_under_research_refused` (research fake → not ignored → refuse), `test_nested_fake_weekly_private_under_state_refused` (nested → refuse), `test_case_variant_guard_matches_git` (guard verdict == `git check-ignore` per platform). Verify: PrivacyGuard 9 OK; holdings+weekly+render+phase5+doc-governance+route-doc **226 OK**; py_compile OK; `git diff --check` clean (CRLF only); design §8 updated to "git check-ignore truth, not path-name heuristic". **Codex re-`审查` FAIL (2026-06-16)**: runtime guard is now correct on the reproduced paths (true private allowed; fake research/fake nested/tracked case-variant rejected), but two active text surfaces still contradict the repaired invariant. (1) `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py::PrivacyGuardTests` docstring still says the criterion is "仓库内 且 路径不含 weekly_private → 拒", which teaches the exact path-name heuristic this repair is meant to retire; required repair: update that docstring to "仓库内 且 git 未忽略 → 拒; git 真忽略/仓库外/无 account/override → 放行". (2) the standalone previous Codex FAIL paragraph below still says "current fix is incomplete" and describes the old substring code as current, after the main entry has already recorded the git-ground-truth repair; required repair: mark that paragraph as historical/superseded-by-Claude-repair or fold it into the main entry so the register no longer contains a live-current contradiction. No runtime behavior change is required unless the text cleanup exposes a code mismatch. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-16, doc residue)**: judged correct (text-only; the runtime guard is already git-`check-ignore` ground truth and is unchanged). (1) `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py::PrivacyGuardTests` docstring updated to the git-truth criterion ("仓库内 且 git 未忽略 → 拒; git 真忽略/仓库外/无 account/override → 放行"). (2) the standalone first-`审查 FAIL` paragraph below is now prefixed **[SUPERSEDED / 历史]**, framing its "current/currently substring" description as then-current and superseded by the git-truth repair above (audit detail retained, no live contradiction). Repo-wide re-grep for stale substring判据 = 0 LIVE residue; the only other hit is the `docs/SESSION_LOG.md` `起草` entry's "不是'路径必须叫 weekly_private',而是'仓库内 且 非私密 → 拒'", which is spirit-accurate under the git-truth model (非私密 = git-ignored) and is an append-only historical entry, deliberately left. Verify: holdings+weekly+render+phase5+doc-governance+route-doc **226 OK**; py_compile OK; `git diff --check` clean (CRLF only). No runtime change. Re-`审查` pending. **Codex re-`审查` PASS (2026-06-16)**: independently verified runtime guard and doc-residue cleanup; probe matrix true_private allowed / fake_research rejected / fake_nested rejected / case_private allowed / case_nonprivate rejected; PrivacyGuard/holdings 27 OK; render+phase5 65 OK; weekly guard 9 OK; doc-governance+route-doc 30 OK; py_compile/PSParser OK; `git diff --check` clean (CRLF only). Full weekly pipeline still 103 OK + 1 environment gap (`tushare` missing). Closure now requires Claude `提交`, then flip this entry to `resolved` with commit hash; untracked `research/results/a_short/em_probe_smoke_20260614/*` must stay excluded. **Resolved at `155e3ad`** (local master 2026-06-16, Codex re-`审查 PASS` + user `提交`; 7-file slice). NB: the slice commit message frames it as a user override of "one more pure-doc re-审查" — that wording predated this register's Codex re-`审查 PASS` note (a stale SESSION_LOG read); the commit is in fact post-PASS. `em_probe_smoke_20260614` stayed excluded.

**[SUPERSEDED / 历史 — 已被主条目(上方)记录的 Claude `修复`(护栏改用 `git check-ignore` 真值)取代;保留供审计,下文 "current/currently" 为当时态、描述的是当时的 substring 实现,非当前实现]** **Codex `审查` FAIL (2026-06-16,历史首次)**: current fix is incomplete. The pipeline guard currently authorizes any in-repo path whose normalized string contains `/weekly_private/`, but `.gitignore` only protects `state/*/weekly_private/`. Repro: `_reject_nonprivate_account_output_path(r"D:\cnhea\Stock\research\results\a_short\weekly_private\20260612\weekly_m67.json", True)` returns without `SystemExit`, while `git check-ignore -v research/results/a_short/weekly_private/20260612/weekly_m67.json` exits non-zero, so that account-bearing artifact would still be committable. A second probe with a case-variant repo path (`d:\CNHEA\Stock\research\results\a_short\20260612\weekly_m67.json`) also returns without `SystemExit` because `inside_repo` uses a case-sensitive string prefix comparison on Windows paths. Required repair: make the private predicate prove that the account-bearing output is under the canonical gitignored route `repo_root/state/<system>/weekly_private/<as_of>/` (or an equivalently verified ignored private route) using case-insensitive/canonical path comparison on Windows; reject fake `weekly_private` directories anywhere else inside the repo; add regression tests for a fake `research/results/.../weekly_private/...` path and a case-variant/noncanonical repo path; keep the explicit `--allow-nonprivate-account-out` override and observation-only research route unchanged. Verification gap recorded: current PrivacyGuard tests pass because they only cover the true private route, not the committable fake-private route.

Codex review finding for A-short holdings-always-in-M6.7 S1 coverage contract doc/schema drift after Tier-2 repair (2026-06-16; status `resolved` (committed `3e81200` local master 2026-06-16, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`); severity `P2`; scope `schemas/a_short_m67_report.schema.json`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md`, `docs/README.md`, current repair for `R-ASHORT-HOLDINGS-S1-TIER2-EGSFULL-CLOSE-PRICE-CLOCK-DRIFT` and `R-ASHORT-HOLDINGS-S1-TIER2-SEMANTIC-UNCHECKED-MISRENDERED-CLEAR`; PIT label `A-short holdings-in-M6.7 / coverage contract / schema-description / route-doc drift`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-HOLDINGS-S1-COVERAGE-CONTRACT-DOC-DRIFT`): the two functional Tier-2 repairs are correct on code path review and targeted tests: `_build_holdings()` now overrides injected holding current price from the accepted price provider latest bar, and Tier-2 holding render now keeps real egs_full-derived EGS fields while visibly warning `语义/新闻未核查(S1)`. However, several contract surfaces still describe the old model. `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` `_build_holdings()` docstring still says Tier-2 `coverage=full`; `schemas/a_short_m67_report.schema.json` still defines `coverage_status.full` as "top-N 或 egs_full" and `partial` as "Tier-3 粗筛未覆盖"; and `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md` §3 still says the S1 architecture routes injected holdings through `build_m67_report` and that render overrides EGS fields by `coverage_status=partial`, while the actual implementation uses `build_holding_report` for Tier-3 and keys the EGS-field override on `row_source=="account_position_only"`. Materiality: `coverage_status` is a schema contract consumed by future agents/tools; leaving it to say Tier-2 is full or partial means only Tier-3 can reintroduce the exact safety bug just fixed, because a later change can trust the schema text and treat a Tier-2 injected holding as fully checked. Required repair: align all contract text with the actual S1 model: `coverage_status=full` only for top-N candidate rows (including held top-N); every injected non-top-N holding is `coverage_status=partial` because S1 does not run semantic/news for holdings; `row_source` distinguishes Tier-2 (`account_position_egs_full`, EGS scored but semantic/news unrun) from Tier-3 (`account_position_only`, EGS and semantic/news unrun); EGS-field "未核查" override is row_source-based for Tier-3 only; Tier-3 reports use `build_holding_report`, while Tier-2 uses `build_m67_report` with explicit semantic/news caveat at render. Update schema descriptions, `_build_holdings` docstring, `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md`, and the README route row if needed. Do not change runtime behavior unless a doc-alignment check exposes a true code mismatch. Add a narrow grep/doc guard or at least rerun `rg` to prove stale phrases such as `coverage=full` for Tier-2 and `full=完整(top-N 或 egs_full)` are gone. Closure requires doc-governance/route-doc guards, targeted holdings tests, py_compile, `git diff --check`, and continued exclusion of `research/results/a_short/em_probe_smoke_20260614/*`. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-16)**: judged correct — pure doc/schema/comment alignment to the actual S1 model, no runtime change. Aligned all four surfaces: (1) `schemas/a_short_m67_report.schema.json` `coverage_status` description now reads `full`=**only** top-N candidate rows (incl held top-N, full candidate chain w/ semantic), `partial`=**every** non-top-N injected holding (S1 runs no semantic/news for holdings), Tier-2(`account_position_egs_full`, EGS reused/semantic unrun) vs Tier-3(`account_position_only`, EGS+semantic unrun) distinguished by `row_source`, EGS-未核查 render override Tier-3-only; (2) `_build_holdings` docstring fixed (Tier-2 no longer "coverage=full"; coverage 一律 partial; current price from price-provider bar); (3) `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md` §3 fixed (Tier-3 via `build_holding_report`, Tier-2 via `build_m67_report`; EGS override keyed on `row_source`; semantic未核查 line for every holding; price from price clock); (4) README route row updated (all holdings partial, price clock, semantic-未核查-for-all). B-ripple grep: stale `top-N 或 egs_full` and Tier-2 `coverage=full` = **0 residual** (remaining `coverage_status=full` hits are the legitimate Tier-1 candidate case). Verify: holdings + weekly + doc-governance + route-doc **152 OK**; schema JSON parses; py_compile OK; no-BOM; `git diff --check` clean. No runtime behavior changed (docstrings/descriptions/docs only). **Codex re-`审查` PASS (2026-06-16)**: independently verified code/schema/doc alignment; targeted holdings test with local jsonschema stub 18 OK; render+doc-governance+route-doc 51 OK; schema JSON parse OK; py_compile OK; stale-contract grep OK (only legal Tier-1 `coverage_status=full` wording remains); `git diff --check` clean (CRLF warning only). Full weekly/phase5 unittest imports remain blocked in this Codex environment because bundled Python lacks real `jsonschema`; Claude recorded jsonschema-present broader runs. **Resolved at `3e81200`** (local master 2026-06-16, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`).

Codex review finding for A-short holdings-always-in-M6.7 S1 Tier-2 price clock drift (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (committed `3e81200` local master 2026-06-16, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`); severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_egs_full_adapter.py`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py`, current S1 design `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md`; PIT label `A-short holdings-in-M6.7 / account_position_egs_full / price clock / current price`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-HOLDINGS-S1-TIER2-EGSFULL-CLOSE-PRICE-CLOCK-DRIFT`): the Tier-2 route correctly reuses the current `egs_full_<as_of>.csv` scoring row for an account holding that is outside top-N, but it also maps `egs_full.close` into `candidate.quote.close` and then `normalize_candidate()` uses that candidate quote as the M6.7 `close`, even though the S1 design requires actual M6.7 price to come from the weekly pipeline price clock. Independent probe reproduced the drift: with a Tier-2 holding whose `egs_full.close` was `999.0` and whose fetched/latest price series close was `10.25`, `_build_holdings()` produced normalized `close=999.0` while `price_series[-1].close=10.25`. Materiality: the row can display or calculate holding action/prices from a stale or mismatched CSV close while the lineage says the weekly price clock is fresh; this directly contaminates the final M6.7 holding row. Required repair: for `account_position_egs_full` rows, keep `egs_full` as score/risk lineage, but override the candidate/current close from the accepted `price_provider` latest bar (or override normalized `close`) before M6.7 report construction; do not let `egs_full.close` be the current-price authority. Add a regression where `egs_full.close` deliberately differs from the fetched price-series close and assert the normalized/report/table current price uses the weekly price-provider close. Boundary: do not change `egs_main`, top-N admission, EGS score semantics, semantic providers, broker/order behavior, or the S1 no-active-stop/no-active-add boundary. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-16)**: judged correct. `_build_holdings` now overrides `cand["quote"]["close"]` with the accepted `price_provider` latest bar (`series[-1]["close"]`) for **all** injected holdings before `normalize_candidate`, so `egs_full.close` is never the current-price authority; egs_full stays only as EGS score/risk lineage. Regression `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py::BuildHoldingsTests::test_tier2_close_uses_price_clock_not_egsfull` (egs_full.close=999.0, fetched series close=10.25) asserts normalized `close==10.25` while `egs_score` still reuses egs_full (71.5). Verify: holdings+render+weekly+phase5 **187 OK**; no `egs_main`/top-N/semantic-provider/boundary change. **Codex re-`审查` PASS (2026-06-16)**: confirmed the working tree keeps current price on injected holdings from the accepted price-provider bar and not `egs_full.close`; targeted holdings test with local jsonschema stub 18 OK; py_compile OK. **Resolved at `3e81200`** (local master 2026-06-16, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`).

Codex review finding for A-short holdings-always-in-M6.7 S1 Tier-2 semantic coverage misrendered as clear (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (committed `3e81200` local master 2026-06-16, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`); severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `runners/a_short_m67_render.py`, `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py`, current S1 design `docs/a_short_holdings_in_m67_design.md`; PIT label `A-short holdings-in-M6.7 / account_position_egs_full / semantic coverage / M6.7 safety display`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-HOLDINGS-S1-TIER2-SEMANTIC-UNCHECKED-MISRENDERED-CLEAR`): S1 explicitly does not expand official/web semantic-news analysis to injected account holdings, but Tier-2 holdings are sent through `build_m67_report()` with `semantic=None` and `coverage_status=full`. The renderer only overrides unsafe "无" displays when `coverage_status == "partial"`, and the new test even locks Tier-2 behavior as "full shows normally no override". Therefore a Tier-2 account holding can show `否决审查触发:无` / normal full coverage even though semantic/news/regulatory review was not run for that injected holding. Materiality: M6.7 is the user's final landing table; hiding an unrun semantic layer as "无风险" is worse than a missing field because it looks cleared. Required repair: make S1 coverage honest for every injected holding, including Tier-2. Either split coverage into EGS coverage and semantic coverage, or mark Tier-2 holding rows with an explicit `semantic/news 未核查(S1)` caveat in the M6.7 holding section; in all cases missing semantic input must not render as "无/clear". Add a regression proving a Tier-2 `account_position_egs_full` row without semantic evidence renders the unrun semantic/news layer visibly and does not show it as cleared, while still reusing `egs_full` scoring. Boundary: keep candidate/top-N rows unchanged; do not expand live semantic fetching to holdings in S1 unless separately approved; do not change hard-veto/star/sizing/business semantics beyond honest coverage display. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-16)**: judged correct. Made S1 coverage honest for **every** injected holding: (1) `_build_holdings` now sets `coverage_status="partial"` for **all** holdings (Tier-2 included) since semantic/news is not run for holdings in S1 — `full` is no longer claimed; Tier-2 vs Tier-3 distinction moved entirely to `row_source`. (2) render adds an explicit `语义/新闻未核查(S1)` caveat line to **every** holding card (Tier-2 + Tier-3), so an unrun semantic layer is visible, never shown as 无/clear. (3) the EGS-derived `否决审查触发`/`板块资金事件` 未核查 override now keys on `row_source=="account_position_only"` (Tier-3, EGS uncovered) — Tier-2 keeps its **real** egs_full-derived EGS fields (EGS was genuinely scored) while still flagging semantic uncovered; `_coverage_label` shows `复用egs_full·语义未核查` / `EGS未覆盖(粗筛)·语义未核查`. No live semantic fetch added to holdings (still S2). Regression `tests/test_a_short_holdings_in_m67.py::RenderHoldingsSectionTests::test_tier2_egs_shown_but_semantic_unchecked`: Tier-2 `account_position_egs_full` row shows real `否决审查触发:无`(EGS reused) + visible `语义/新闻未核查(S1)` + no `EGS 未覆盖`, not cleared. Candidate/top-N rows unchanged. Verify: **187 OK**. **Codex re-`审查` PASS (2026-06-16)**: confirmed Tier-2 keeps real egs_full-derived EGS display while every injected holding visibly carries `语义/新闻未核查(S1)` and `coverage_status=partial`; targeted holdings test with local jsonschema stub 18 OK; render tests 21 OK. **Resolved at `3e81200`** (local master 2026-06-16, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`).

Codex review finding for A-short 4.3-C M6.7 holding/cooldown render state hiding overlapping Rule12+Rule13 tags (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-15; committed `6d3e30d` local master 2026-06-15 + user `提交`); severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_m67_render.py`, `tests/test_a_short_m67_render.py`, `docs/a_short_account_state_manual_tables_4_3.md`, `docs/README.md`, desktop design `C:\Users\cnhea\Desktop\4.3.md §16.2`; PIT label `A-short 4.3-C / M6.7 render-only / stateful_risk display / overlapping Rule12+Rule13`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-43C-HOLDING-STATE-MULTILABEL-DROP`): the render-only direction is correct and the new column is useful, but the implementation drops part of the state when a stock has both a per-stock Rule13 state and a portfolio-level Rule12 state. The authoritative desktop design §16.2 explicitly says the implementation must allow multiple tags and, if the same stock hits both Rule12 and Rule13, must not show only one status; example expected display: `Rule12冷静 + Rule13待复核`. Current `_holding_state()` instead uses a single `if/elif` chain and intentionally gives Rule13 precedence over Rule12, returning only `Rule13待复核` / `Rule13冷静` / `Rule13可再入` when any Rule13 status exists. The in-repo design doc then repeats that precedence rule (`docs/a_short_account_state_manual_tables_4_3.md §10`), so the implementation and repo doc have drifted away from the user-approved desktop 4.3 design. Materiality: M6.7 is the user's final landing surface. Hiding Rule12 while Rule13 is shown can make the table look like only a single-stock cooldown is active when the whole portfolio is also in cooldown/recovery; that weakens the purpose of 4.3-C, which is to make holding/cooldown context plainly visible. Required repair: change the render logic to build a multi-label status from all applicable states instead of selecting only one. Existing position may remain a single dominant `已持仓` label if that is the chosen design, but for flat candidates the label must include both applicable Rule12 and Rule13 tags, e.g. `Rule12冷静 + Rule13待复核` or `Rule12恢复 + Rule13可再入`. Keep all reasons in the per-stock explanation. Update `docs/a_short_account_state_manual_tables_4_3.md` and `docs/README.md` to remove the "Rule13 priority hides Rule12" wording and state the multi-label rule. Add a regression test that fails on the current behavior: when `stateful_risk.rule12.status=active_cooldown` and `stateful_risk.rule13.status=pending_recheck`, the overview row and per-stock line must show both tags. Boundary: render/docs/tests only; do not change `account_state` schema, lineage schema, Phase 5 engine action/star/sizing/hard-veto, weekly pipeline, EGS, broker/order behavior, or 4.3-A/B converter semantics. Closure requires targeted render tests, route-doc/doc-governance guards, py_compile, `git diff --check`, and continued exclusion of unrelated `research/results/a_short/em_probe_smoke_20260614/*`. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-15)**: judged correct (the single `if/elif` gave Rule13 precedence and hid an overlapping portfolio Rule12). `_holding_state` now builds a **multi-label** for flat candidates — portfolio Rule12 (`Rule12冷静`/`Rule12恢复`) first + per-stock Rule13 (`Rule13冷静`/`Rule13待复核`/`Rule13可再入`) second, joined by ` + ` (e.g. `Rule12冷静 + Rule13待复核`, `Rule12恢复 + Rule13可再入`); `空仓` only when none apply; held remains a single dominant `已持仓` (register-permitted) with the Rule12 reason still surfaced in the per-stock card line (no info lost). Repo docs updated to remove the "Rule13 priority hides Rule12" wording and state the multi-label rule (`docs/a_short_account_state_manual_tables_4_3.md §10`, `docs/README.md` 4.3 row). Regression tests added: `tests/test_a_short_m67_render.py::HoldingStateTests::test_overlapping_rule12_rule13_shows_both_tags` (rule12 `active_cooldown` + rule13 `pending_recheck` → BOTH the overview row and the per-stock line must show `Rule12冷静 + Rule13待复核`, both reasons kept) + `test_overlapping_recovery_and_cleared_shows_both_tags` + `test_held_stays_single_label_but_rule12_reason_in_card`; the prior single-label precedence test was replaced. Verify: `tests.test_a_short_m67_render` + weekly + phase5 + route-doc + doc-governance **199 OK**; py_compile OK; no-BOM; `git diff --check` clean (CRLF only). Boundary held (render/docs/tests only; no `account_state`/lineage schema, Phase 5 action/star/sizing/hard-veto, weekly pipeline, EGS, or broker change; `em_probe_smoke_20260614` still excluded). **Codex re-`审查` PASS (2026-06-15)**: verified by Codex with render 21 OK, doc-governance+route-doc 30 OK, py_compile OK, overlap probe OK, no-BOM/FFFD OK, and `git diff --check` clean (CRLF warnings only). **Resolved at `6d3e30d`** (local master 2026-06-15, Codex `审查 PASS` + user `提交`); `em_probe_smoke_20260614` stayed excluded.

Codex review finding for the A-short V14.3 regime Stage-5 weekly_screening integration route-doc drift (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (committed `e478969` local master 2026-06-15, Codex re-`审查` PASS); severity `P2`; scope `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/phase6/test_weekly_screening_guardrails.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `runners/README.md`, contextual read of `docs/README.md`, `docs/CURRENT.md`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `A-short V14.3 regime comparison-only / weekly_screening live sidecar / active route-doc synchronization`; Required ID `R-V143-WEEKLYSCREENING-ROUTEDOC-STAGE5-DRIFT`): the runtime direction is coherent enough to review further — `weekly_screening.ps1` now adds a Stage 5 that runs `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py` only on live same-day runs, supports `-SkipRegime`, chooses `--bootstrap` when `research/results/a_short/regime_daily_ledger.json` is absent, passes the IV feed when present, and treats failures as non-blocking comparison-only sidecar failures. However, the active user/operator entry doc `runners/README.md` still describes `weekly_screening.ps1` as running `egs_main.py` + `data_canary.py` + `forward_tracker.py` + M6.7 advisory only, and it does not mention the newly added V14.3 regime comparison sidecar, `-SkipRegime`, live-only behavior, bootstrap-or-increment behavior, research-lane outputs, non-blocking semantics, or the comparison-only / no-production boundary. This is the same route-doc/entrypoint drift class previously guarded in this repo: active entry docs must match real wrapper behavior before commit. Boundary: documentation/guard synchronization only unless a script help/comment is internally inconsistent; do not change regime semantics, ledger schema, Tushare fetch scope, IV math, V14.2, EGS ranking/scoring, M6.7 actions, production-switch gate, or broker/order behavior. (2) Added `tests/phase6/test_weekly_screening_guardrails.py::test_runner_readme_documents_regime_stage`: when `weekly_screening.ps1` invokes `a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py` and exposes `$SkipRegime`, `runners/README.md` must contain the runner filename + `-SkipRegime` + `--bootstrap` + `comparison-only` + `非阻断` (route-doc drift goes red otherwise). Codex review finding for the A-short M6.7 Monday-intraday weekend-news window (2026-06-15; status `accepted_risk` (user explicitly accepted Monday-news inclusion on 2026-06-15; no code repair required for this boundary); severity `P2` documented operational semantics; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`, `runners/a_short_m67_render.py`, `docs/a_short_weekly_pipeline_design_20260610.md`, current 20260612 weekly artifact diff, and exclusion review of untracked `research/results/a_short/em_probe_smoke_20260614/*`; PIT label `A-short M6.7 advisory / Monday before-close run / weekend-plus-Monday semantic-news clock`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-M67-WEEKEND-NEWS-WINDOW-GAP`): the current repair makes the M6.7 price clock explicit, while the semantic/news clock remains inclusive through Monday `as_of`. In `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, both auto semantic providers are constructed with `args.as_of`: `_build_cninfo_semantic_provider(..., args.as_of, args.cninfo_lookback_days)` and `_build_deepseek_web_llm_provider(..., args.as_of, args.web_news_lookback_days)`. The downstream fetch windows are inclusive through `as_of`: `fetch_cninfo` sends `[as_of-lookback, as_of]`, and `_em_news_in_window` returns true for any `published_at` date `<= as_of`. A no-network AST probe against `_em_news_in_window` confirmed that with `as_of=20260615`, Saturday 2026-06-13, Sunday 2026-06-14, and Monday 2026-06-15 all return `True`. `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` also documents the live path as "新闻窗 as_of=周一 -> 判到周六/周日(及任何周一早间)", which now matches the accepted boundary after the user's 2026-06-15 follow-up. Accepted-risk disposition: no repair is required while Monday news is accepted. The operational meaning is now: Monday-before-close M6.7 can use Friday/prior-settled selection and price features while semantic/news evidence includes Saturday, Sunday, and any available Monday-before-run items. If the user later wants strict weekend-only semantics, introduce an explicit semantic/news clock separate from `as_of` and `price_freshness` (for example schema-validated `run_lineage.semantic_news_window` with start/end dates, plus CLI args such as `--news-window-start`/`--news-window-end` or a reviewed `--semantic-news-through` contract), apply it to EM/web_llm and cninfo official_structured, render it in `weekly_m67.md`, and add no-network Sat/Sun/Monday inclusion/exclusion tests. Boundary: this accepted-risk disposition does not change EGS ranking/scoring, V14.2 rules, price freshness semantics already repaired in `R-ASHORT-M67-INTRADAY-PRICE-FRESHNESS-LINEAGE-GAP`, IV math, risk keyword severity rules, provider expansion, production `result/a_short` write guard, or broker/order behavior. Commit boundary: this disposition is docs-only; continue excluding unrelated EM smoke byproducts unless separately authorized.

Codex review finding for the A-short M6.7 reviewed intraday price tolerance (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (committed `8ad531c` local master 2026-06-15; closed on Claude `审查 PASS` self/adversarial review + user-authorized `提交`; Codex independent re-`审查` not run this round); severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`, `runners/a_short_m67_render.py`, contextual read of `A-EGS/egs_main.py` and current 20260612 artifacts; PIT label `A-short M6.7 advisory / intraday prior-settled price tolerance / output lineage`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-M67-INTRADAY-PRICE-FRESHNESS-LINEAGE-GAP`): the tolerance direction is valid for the user's Monday-before-close workflow, but the current implementation makes the tolerance too implicit and leaves the resulting artifact unauditable. `weekly_screening.ps1` always passes `--run-date $RunDate`, and `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py::main` enables `accept_prior_settled_date` whenever `--run-date == --as-of`; that equality is only a date comparison, not proof that the run is before close or that EGS actually used prior-settled data. If the wrapper is run after close, or if Tushare endpoint families update unevenly, the pipeline can accept a prior-trading-day price series for a same-day `as_of` while the selection artifact may already reflect same-day EOD inputs. Separately, even in the intended Monday-before-close case, the accepted price date is not recorded: `_fetch_price_series()` strips `trade_date`, `run_lineage` only records analysis_input / IV / account fields, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json` forbids extra lineage fields, and the Markdown lineage banner cannot show whether M6.7 price features are through `as_of` or through the prior settled trading day. Materiality: a user-facing M6.7 report can say `as_of=Monday` while its technical price features are through Friday, and neither JSON nor Markdown makes that distinction machine-readable or visible to the user. This is the same artifact-honesty class as the earlier no-account sizing banner: the recommendation may be acceptable, but the output must say exactly what data clock it used. Required repair: make the price freshness mode explicit and auditable. At minimum, add schema-validated lineage fields such as `run_date`, `price_freshness_mode` (`strict_as_of` vs `intraday_prior_settled`), `accepted_prior_settled_date`, and the actual `price_data_through` / latest price date used; render the same clock in `weekly_m67.md`. Do not infer intraday tolerance solely from `run-date == as-of`; require an explicit reviewed mode/flag or a machine-verifiable before-close / prior-settled condition, and fail closed on mixed or older price latest dates unless deliberately documented and tested. Add tests for: strict mode still requires latest `== as_of`; intended intraday mode accepts exactly prior-settled and records it; stale older dates still abort; future dates still abort; same-day after-close/default path cannot silently accept prior-settled; schema rejects missing/invalid price-lineage fields; Markdown displays the price clock. Boundary: do not change EGS ranking/scoring, V14.2 rules, semantic provider scope, IV math, official/web risk semantics, production `result/a_short` write guard, or broker/order behavior. Closure requires the targeted weekly pipeline tests in a `jsonschema`-present environment, schema/render tests, PowerShell ParseFile, `py_compile`, `git diff --check`, and a no-network fixture showing a Monday-before-close artifact visibly states that price data is through the prior settled trading day. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-15)**: judged the finding correct (the original draft relaxed the gate but left the price clock implicit/unauditable) and repaired both parts. (1) Explicit mode: added `--price-freshness-mode {strict_as_of(default)|intraday_prior_settled}`; intraday is rejected up front unless `--run-date == --as-of` (no longer inferred from a bare date equality), and `weekly_screening.ps1` only passes intraday on a same-day live run (`as_of == run_date`). (2) Auditable lineage: `_fetch_price_series` now returns `(series, latest_trade_date)`; `main` records `run_lineage.price_freshness = {mode, run_date, accepted_prior_settled_date, price_data_through}` (schema-required + typed, enum-validated), the Markdown shows the price clock (and a banner when features are through the prior settled day, not `as_of`), and candidates with differing latest price dates (mixed/uneven endpoint clock) FATAL without writing. Tests added/updated: mode controls tolerance; intraday requires `run-date==as-of`; strict & intraday lineage both recorded; intraday no-network fixture renders the clock in JSON+`.md`; mixed dates abort; schema rejects missing/invalid `price_freshness`; gate returns the actual latest date; injected list-providers handled via `_price_provider_result` (no churn). Verify: weekly_pipeline 104 OK + broad cluster 211 OK (render/phase5/contract-docs/ps1-guard/doc-governance/route-doc); schema Draft7 OK; ps1 ParseFile OK; `py_compile`/no-BOM/`git diff --check` clean. The doc↔schema guard is satisfied (`price_freshness` documented in `docs/a_short_weekly_pipeline_design_20260610.md`); the stored 20260612 `weekly_m67.{json,md}` (made schema-stale by the new required field) was retrofitted with its factual clock (strict_as_of, through 20260612) and re-rendered (md diff = one price-clock line). Boundary held (no EGS/V14.2/semantic/IV-math/production-write-guard/order change). **Resolved at `8ad531c`** (local master 2026-06-15) on **Claude `审查 PASS`** (self/adversarial: full-surface read of pipeline/schema/render/ps1/tests/retrofit + try-to-break — empty series / mixed dates / after-close EOD / render-missing-pf / historical run_date all caught by the coverage gate or fail-closed; one cosmetic optional `_cands`/`cands` redundant read, non-blocking) **+ user-authorized `提交`**. Codex independent re-`审查` was NOT run this round (available if an independent gate is wanted; a future Codex review may reopen). Commit `8ad531c` included pipeline/render/ps1/schema/design-doc/tests + the retrofitted 20260612 `weekly_m67.{json,md}` + SESSION_LOG; this register flip is a separate follow-up commit; the untracked EM smoke byproduct stayed excluded.

Codex review finding for the A-short V14.3 regime compute performance optimization (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (committed `9d170818` local master 2026-06-15, Codex re-`审查` PASS); severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/_regime_features_reference_frozen.py`, `docs/SESSION_LOG.md`; PIT label `A-short V14.3 regime comparison-only / compute perf optimization / PIT future-row ignore`; Required ID `R-V143-REGIME-PIT-FUTURE-DUP-STKLIMIT-REGRESSION`): the vectorized rewrite is directionally correct and the frozen-reference equality tests cover ordinary future rows, but `_incomplete_by_date` now builds a left merge over the full `stk_limit` panel before applying any `as_of` PIT cap. A future-date duplicate usable `stk_limit` row (date > current `as_of`) duplicates rows in `mer`, making `fully` longer than the original daily date array and causing `pd.DataFrame({"_d": dd, "ok": fully})` to raise `ValueError: All arrays must be of the same length`. The old pre-optimization implementation ignored that future duplicate for the current `as_of` because `_trade_dates` and all per-day filters were capped to dates `<= as_of`. Independent probe: with `as_of=20240120`, a valid PIT daily/limit panel plus a duplicate future `20240121` `stk_limit` row returns OK under the frozen reference but the optimized implementation raises the length error. Materiality: the function contract explicitly says inputs are PIT-restricted internally and rows `> as_of` are ignored; a bootstrap/provider that reuses a later-date panel across earlier `as_of` values must not let future duplicates or future-source defects break a current row. Required repair: cap the `daily`/`stk_limit` working frames (and their reused date strings) to `<= as_of` before vectorized merge/groupby passes, or otherwise make `_incomplete_by_date` and `_limit_events` merge-cardinality invariant so rows after `as_of` cannot alter counts or raise. Add a regression test proving a future duplicate `stk_limit` row after `as_of` is ignored and optimized output still equals the frozen reference; keep the existing ordinary future-row PIT test. Boundary: do not change regime semantics, emitted fields, schema, classifier/ledger contracts, provider fetch behavior, V14.2, EGS selection, M6.7 decisions, or broker/order behavior. Closure requires the new regression, the existing optimized-vs-frozen equality tests, regime-related unit tests in a `jsonschema`-present environment, `py_compile`, `git diff --check`, and inclusion of `tests/_regime_features_reference_frozen.py` in the eventual commit while still excluding unrelated `research/results/a_short/em_probe_smoke_20260614/*` byproducts. **Claude `修复` (2026-06-15)**: reproduced the finding (frozen reference returns `limit_up_count=1`, optimized raised `ValueError: All arrays must be of the same length`), then PIT-capped the working `daily`/`stk_limit` frames + their reused date-string series to `<= as_of` immediately after the integrity checks and before every vectorized merge/groupby — the whole-class fix (any future row, duplicate or otherwise, is structurally dropped, honoring the "rows > as_of ignored" contract; canonical-date and `<= as_of` uniqueness checks still run on the uncapped frames first, so an at/<= as_of duplicate still raises). Added `test_future_dated_duplicate_stk_limit_is_ignored_pit` (future-dup ignored, optimized == frozen reference, `limit_up_count==1`); kept the ordinary future-row PIT test. Verify: regime suite **205 OK** (`jsonschema` present); real-scale 1.36M-row probe still byte-identical at last AND early as_of (early as_of now 0.60s vs 2.10s — the cap drops future days, so the bootstrap is faster too); `py_compile`/no-BOM/`git diff --check` clean. Claude `执行` finding — weekly_screening Stage-4 IV feed build sys.path failure (2026-06-15; status `resolved` (committed local master 2026-06-15, Codex `审查` PASS); severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_iv_feed_build.py`, optional `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`; PIT label `A-short weekly_screening / M6.7 advisory / IV build ModuleNotFoundError`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-IVBUILD-SYSPATH-MODULENOTFOUND`): the 周五实盘 `执行` (as_of `20260612`, L3 `neutralize`) completed with EGS selection + canary + tracker OK (overall exit 0), but Stage 4 IV feed build FAILED: `python runners\a_short_iv_feed_build.py --as-of 20260612 ... --confirm-fetch-authorized` raised `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'runners'` at `a_short_iv_feed_build.py::main()` line ~247 (`from runners.a_short_iv_feed_probe import init_tushare_pro, fetch_probe_inputs, filter_50etf_options, _is_valid_yyyymmdd`). Root cause: `a_short_iv_feed_build.py` does NOT add the project root to `sys.path` at module top (it only computes SCHEMA_PATH via os.path; there is no `ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]; sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))` like other runners e.g. `a_short_em_news_probe.py`). When run directly as `python runners\a_short_iv_feed_build.py`, `sys.path[0]` is `runners/`, so the `runners` package (whose parent is the project root) is not importable. Effect: `weekly_screening.ps1` Stage 4 (line ~211) always fails on the IV build → the entire M6.7 advisory (market IV feed → `a_short_weekly_pipeline` with semantic cninfo + EM→DeepSeek web_llm inline) is skipped as an advisory sidecar (no `research/results/a_short/20260612/` artifact produced), so the EM→DeepSeek end-to-end was NOT exercised this run despite `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` being set. This is not a data problem; it is an import-path bug that breaks the M6.7 stage on any machine running it directly. Materiality: M6.7 advisory is a first-class weekly_screening output and is currently unrunnable via the one-click entry. Required repair: add the standard `ROOT`/`sys.path.insert` block at the top of `a_short_iv_feed_build.py` (mirror the other runners) so `from runners.*` resolves when run directly; do NOT change the IV inversion / interpolation / percentile logic, schema, or any business behavior. Boundary: sys.path-only fix; do not touch IV math, production EGS / V14.2, M6.7 semantics, or broker/order. The pre-Codex class-not-instance check found `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` (Stage 4 step 2) had the SAME defect — no top sys.path + `main()` does `from runners.a_short_iv_feed_probe import` / `from engine.data.analysis_input_contract import` BEFORE argparse — so fixing only `a_short_iv_feed_build.py` would have re-failed on the Stage-4 rerun (a second round). Scope of the class = weekly_screening direct-run runners: `egs_main` (Stage 1 succeeded this run), `data_canary`, `forward_tracker` already carry sys.path (grep-confirmed), so only these two needed it. `a_short_deepseek_semantic_adapter.py` has a top-level `from runners./engine.` but is only imported by `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` (not a direct-run entry), so it resolves via the caller's now-fixed path; left unchanged on purpose (class boundary = direct-run runners). No business logic / IV math / schema / M6.7 semantics / signatures changed — top-of-file sys.path only. Verify: `py_compile` OK for both; `python runners\a_short_iv_feed_build.py --help` exit 0 and `python runners\a_short_weekly_pipeline.py --help` exit 0 — both now pass the argparse-preceding `from runners.*` import in the exact direct-run scenario (`sys.path[0]=runners/`) that previously raised `ModuleNotFoundError`; `git diff --check` clean (LF/CRLF only); no BOM. Codex review finding for the A-short EM news-feed feasibility probe draft (2026-06-14; status `resolved` (committed local master 2026-06-14 `ecf2b5d`, Codex `审查` PASS); severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_em_news_probe.py`, `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_em_news_probe_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_em_news_probe.py`, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_contract.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk / EM web_llm source-feasibility probe / fetcher-filter audit gap`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-EM-PROBE-FETCHER-FILTER-AUDIT-GAP`): the new EM probe is the right shape for replacing the deleted `_diag_web_sources.py` ad-hoc diagnostic with a tracked, reviewable source-feasibility artifact: it is probe-only, gated by `--confirm-fetch-authorized`, refuses `result/a_short`, keeps `backtest_evidence_capable=false`, and its pure classifier/summary consistency tests cover future-date, bad-date, bad-shape, quiet, transport-failure, forged-reachable, and forged-feasible cases. However the current real-fetch path overclaims source-quality auditing. The active contract / README / schema / runner state that the EM probe "re-validates every returned item itself", "does NOT trust `fetch_em_news`' own window filter", partitions `n_recent` / `n_future` / `n_stale` / `n_bad_date` / `n_bad_shape`, and that `future_dated_rejection` proves zero future leak. In the actual CLI path, `runners/a_short_em_news_probe.py::main()` calls the existing `fetch_em_news`; that function normalizes the EM payload and filters it to `recent = [n for n in norm if _em_news_in_window(...)]` before returning `items` to the probe. Future-dated, stale, and malformed/unparseable items can therefore be dropped before `classify_em_code()` ever sees them. The tests inject raw future / bad-shape items directly into `classify_em_code()` / `assess_em_feasibility()`, so they prove the classifier rejects visible defects, but they do not prove the real fetcher-to-probe path preserves those defects for counting. Materiality: a real EM response containing future-dated or malformed rows can be silently filtered by the shared weekly fetcher, while the probe summary still records `n_future_leak_codes=0`, `n_bad_date_codes=0`, `future_dated_rejection=true`, and possibly `feasible=true`; this reintroduces a source-evidence overclaim exactly in the artifact meant to close the EM audit gap. Required repair: either add a probe-specific EM fetch path / mode that returns the normalized unfiltered EM item stream plus enough shape/date metadata for `classify_em_code()` to count future / stale / bad-date / bad-shape rows, or downgrade the contract/schema/README/runner language and fields so the tracked probe claims only reachability and recent-news coverage over the already-filtered `fetch_em_news` output, not future-leak / bad-shape auditing. Add regression coverage that exercises the real fetcher-to-probe integration (mock EM HTTP/session or injected fetcher at the same abstraction boundary) with future-dated and malformed EM rows and proves they either fail the probe gates or are no longer claimed as audited. Boundary: source-feasibility probe, schema, docs, and tests only; do not change DeepSeek judgment semantics, M6.7 advisory behavior, official_structured semantics, production EGS scoring/ranking, V14.2, historical backtests, broker/order behavior, or run real web fetches in tests. Closure requires the repaired targeted EM probe tests, contract/README/schema drift guard if claims are changed, `py_compile`, schema parse, `git diff --check`, and no ad-hoc `_diag_web_sources.py` / validation byproduct staged. **Addressed (Claude `修复` 2026-06-14, option A = preserve auditing, not downgrade)**: added a probe-specific unfiltered fetcher `fetch_em_news_unfiltered` in `runners/a_short_em_news_probe.py` — same em endpoint/request as production `fetch_em_news` (reuses the endpoint constants + `_strip_jsonp`), but returns the normalized-but-UNFILTERED item stream (NO `_em_news_in_window` PIT/recency filter, no sort/cap; em `date`→`published_at` kept even when empty; non-dict rows kept) so `classify_em_code` actually sees future / stale / bad-date / bad-shape rows; `main()` now defaults to it (`news_fetcher or fetch_em_news_unfiltered`, fetcher signature `(codes, names)`). Production weekly still uses the FILTERED `fetch_em_news` (untouched — boundary held). Added integration regression class `FetchEmNewsUnfiltered` (5 tests) exercising the REAL fetcher→probe path: a future-dated + a bad-shape raw EM row are PRESERVED (5 items, not pre-filtered) and counted (`n_future`/`n_bad_shape`) → code `unknown`; a 15-code pool with one future-leak code → `n_future_leak_codes=1`, `future_dated_rejection=false`, `feasible=false`; clean-recent → reachable; non-dict → bad_shape; no-name / non-200 fail-closed. Updated the now-accurate claims on every active surface: runner docstring + `classify` section comment, schema `description`, contract §web_llm EM-owner bullet (probe 走 unfiltered；weekly 生产用过滤版 `fetch_em_news`，probe 绝不复用), README owner row (UNFILTERED probe fetch + filtered-prod-not-reused). Verify: `tests.test_a_short_em_news_probe` 41 OK; with guards 139 OK (em probe + contract-docs drift guard + doc governance + route-doc ledger + existing semantic probe — contract/README/schema edits trip no EM-drift / run-path / governance guard); schema Draft7 OK; `py_compile` OK; 5 changed files no-BOM; `git diff --check` clean (only LF/CRLF warnings); `git status` = 4 docs M (README/contract/SESSION_LOG/register) + 3 new (runner/schema/test), no `_diag_web_sources.py` / `_em_slice.patch` / byproduct staged. Pending Codex re-`审查`.

Claude `审查` findings for the A-short account-state slice (Rule12/Rule13 stateful risk + `持有` action) finalization (2026-06-14; status `resolved` (committed local master 2026-06-14 `3ef40497`, Codex 审查 PASS); severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_m67_render.py`, `tests/test_a_short_m67_render.py`, `tests/test_a_short_phase5_engine.py`, optional `schemas/a_short_account_state.schema.json`; PIT label `A-short V14.2 gap 4.2 / stateful Rule12-13 + 持有`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-ACCOUNT-STATE-RULE12-13-FINALIZE`): independent review found the slice's RUNTIME LOGIC correct — held/veto precedence right (`if hard:` precedes `elif has_position:`, so a held stock with a real hard_veto → `否决` + "已有持仓也不得加仓、手动执行止损/清仓", never `持有`); Rule12/Rule13 flat-block vetoes via the `hard = [... for f in RISK_FAMILIES ...]` aggregation that now includes `stateful_risk`; `size_multiplier` clamps to (0,1] in both `stateful_risk_for_candidate` and `exit_and_size`/`build_m67_report`; the account schema is strict (`available_cash` exclusiveMinimum 0, multiplier∈(0,1], PIT dates ≤ as_of, position dedup, `manual_order_only`/`broker_connection_allowed` const); `validate_m67_consistency` accepts `持有`; `build_weekly_report` default lineage + main emit the new required `account_ref`.
