Codex re-review correction for `R-ASHORT-WEEKLYSCREENING-M67-MISSING-ACCOUNT` / `R-ASHORT-WEEKLYSCREENING-M67-BUNDLE-CONTRACT-DRIFT` (2026-06-14; status `resolved` (committed local master 2026-06-14 `4de3fe29` (Slice 3b-2, Codex 审查 PASS)); severity `P1`; scope `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_run_bundle_convention_20260611.md`, `engine/a_short_run_paths.py`, `runners/README.md`, `tests/phase6/test_weekly_screening_guardrails.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `A-short Slice 3b-2 / weekly M6.7 account + lineage closure gap`): the latest repair partially addresses the prior finding but overclaims closure. Account side: `weekly_screening.ps1` now passes `--account` only when `$Account -and Test-Path $Account`; if the user supplies a bad path, the script falls into the same "no -Account" branch and still runs M6.7 without sizing. If no account is supplied, the warning is terminal-only. A Codex no-network probe using existing weekly-pipeline fixtures showed the durable `weekly.json` / `weekly.md` artifact still has no no-sizing marker (`has_no_sizing_text=false`) while the same buildable candidate changes from `建仓` with account to `观察` / `可建股数/金额不足(放弃)` without account. Therefore the conclusion-changing no-account path is not closed merely by printing a terminal warning. Required repair: distinguish "no account requested" from "user supplied an invalid account path"; invalid supplied path must not silently run sizing-less M6.7. Either require a valid account for the weekly M6.7 stage, or if no-account observation-only is deliberately allowed, write a durable `sizing_mode` / `account_status` / warning into `weekly_m67.json` and the markdown banner so later readers cannot mistake sizing-artifact `观察` for a real avoid signal. Validate a provided account file enough to reject missing/non-numeric `available_cash` before producing normal sizing conclusions. Add behavioral tests, not only static string tests: no-account or bad-account path must either skip/refuse M6.7 or produce an artifact-level no-sizing label, and with-account must still produce real sizing. Bundle side: the docs now choose a HYBRID and claim `weekly_m67.json` records `analysis_input/iv_feed` lineage, but the actual weekly schema/report only has `iv_feed_ref` as a basename and no `analysis_input_ref`, `selection_bucket`, `account_ref`, or `sizing_mode`; `build_weekly_report` emits only `iv_feed_ref` / `reports`, and `main` passes `os.path.basename(args.iv_feed)`. This leaves selection in `result/a_short/<as_of>` and M6.7 in `research/results/a_short/<as_of>` without a durable machine-readable pointer tying them together, which preserves the user's "results scattered" pain under a new label. Required repair: either co-locate/copy the consumed selection/account/lineage into the M6.7 research run folder, or add a schema-validated `run_lineage` / manifest field to `weekly_m67.json` (and markdown) containing at minimum the consumed `analysis_input` path/ref, selection bucket, IV feed path/ref, account ref/status, and sizing mode. Update docs/tests to match the actual artifact, not aspirational prose. Submission hygiene: delete the untracked root `_fix_bundle_docs.py` temp helper before commit, and explicitly decide whether the untracked `research/results/a_short/m67_validation_20260605/` evidence artifacts are intended to be tracked; do not let a blanket add commit temp scripts or accidental validation byproducts. Boundary: account/lineage artifact contract, weekly-screening orchestration, docs/tests, and commit hygiene only; do not change production EGS ranking/scoring, V14.2, DeepSeek judgment semantics, provider fetch scope, historical backtest, or broker/order behavior. Closure requires behavioral account tests, schema/report lineage tests, route/run-bundle doc tests, PowerShell ParseFile, `git diff --check`, and `git status --short` showing no accidental root temp helper. **Addressed (Claude `修复` R2 2026-06-14)**: (account) `weekly_screening.ps1` now distinguishes three cases — valid `-Account` → `--account` (real sizing); `-Account` given but path missing → SKIP M6.7 (`$RunM67=$false` + "-Account path not found", no silent sizing-less run); `-Account` omitted → observation-only, with the pipeline writing a DURABLE `run_lineage.sizing_mode=observation_only_no_account` + `account_status=absent` into `weekly_m67.json` plus a no-sizing banner in the `.md`, so a reader of the ARTIFACT (not just the terminal) can't mistake a sizing-artifact `观察` for a real avoid. `a_short_weekly_pipeline.main` rejects an `--account` file with missing/non-numeric/≤0 `available_cash` (FATAL). Behavioral tests: valid account → sized + `建仓`; no account → observation_only + `观察` + `.md` no-sizing banner; bad-cash → SystemExit (`tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`); render banner tests (`tests/test_a_short_m67_render.py`); guardrails extended for bad-path skip + observation-only label. (lineage) added a schema-validated `run_lineage` object to `a_short_weekly_report` (required + enum-typed) = {analysis_input, selection_bucket, iv_feed, account_status, sizing_mode}; `build_weekly_report` emits it (default absent/observation_only so direct builders/tests stay valid), `main` populates real relpaths, `validate_weekly_report` enforces account_status↔sizing_mode consistency, `.md` renders a lineage line; `docs/a_short_run_bundle_convention_20260611.md` documents the actual `run_lineage` fields. A-short weekly_screening M6.7 one-click wiring omits account sizing input and drifts from the run-bundle contract (status `resolved` (committed local master 2026-06-14 `4de3fe29` (Slice 3b-2, Codex 审查 PASS)); severity `P1`; scope `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `docs/a_short_run_bundle_convention_20260611.md`, `engine/a_short_run_paths.py`, `runners/README.md`, `tests/phase6/test_weekly_screening_guardrails.py`; PIT label `A-short Slice 3b-2 / weekly one-click M6.7 / account + bundle contract`; Required IDs `R-ASHORT-WEEKLYSCREENING-M67-MISSING-ACCOUNT` and `R-ASHORT-WEEKLYSCREENING-M67-BUNDLE-CONTRACT-DRIFT`): the Slice 3b-2 direction is right (retire the standalone semantic summary CLI and run semantics through the M6.7 weekly report), but the current one-click implementation is not safe to submit. First, `weekly_screening.ps1` builds `M67Args` without `--account`, while `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` documents `available_cash` as a required user account input and otherwise leaves it absent. Codex reproduced the behavioral impact with existing unit-test fixtures: the same candidate is `建仓` with an account file but becomes `观察` with trigger `可建股数/金额不足(放弃)` when no account is passed. This is a conclusion-changing path, not a cosmetic omission. Required repair for `R-ASHORT-WEEKLYSCREENING-M67-MISSING-ACCOUNT`: add a reviewed account input path/parameter for `weekly_screening.ps1` and pass it to `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, or explicitly refuse/label the run as no-sizing observation-only if the account file is missing; the preferred fix is to pass a real reviewed account file so one weekly run can produce the intended M6.7 conclusion. Doc-governance B ripple-grep strengthening is directionally correct but not fully self-enforcing (status `resolved` — Codex `审查 PASS`, committed master 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `AGENTS.md`, `docs/pre_codex_self_review_checklist.md`, `tests/test_doc_governance_guard.py`, `docs/SESSION_LOG.md`; PIT label `cross-LLM review protocol / B ripple-grep anti-recurrence / proof and checklist guard`; Required ID `R-DOCGOV-B-RIPPLE-GREP-PROOF-AND-CHECKLIST-GUARD-GAP`): the latest protocol change correctly names the root cause of the repeated evidence-full repair loop: B ripple-grep was executed too narrowly and missed emitted runtime strings plus test docstrings/comments. However the new standard is not yet implemented to its own bar. First, `AGENTS.md` now requires any behavior/symbol/mechanism/rule-wording change to paste the actual `rg` command plus `0 hits` zero-residual evidence into proof-of-use, but the latest `SESSION_LOG.md` handoff for this rule-wording change only says "grep 确认 B 规则仅此两处权威" and does not provide the command or 0-hit proof. This weakens the exact habit the rule is meant to force. Second, the new guard `test_agents_b_ripple_grep_covers_emit_strings_and_test_surfaces` extracts and checks only the `AGENTS.md` B block. It does not read or pin `docs/pre_codex_self_review_checklist.md`, even though `AGENTS.md` routes implementers to that checklist for the detailed pre-Codex gate and the latest handoff claims both AGENTS and checklist were synchronized. A future edit can remove `emit 字符串字面量`, `test_*` docstring/comment coverage, `零残留`, or `全仓 guard` from the checklist while the tests still pass, recreating a docs-contract drift surface inside the very checklist implementers are told to follow. A-short weekly M6.7 cninfo semantic provider bypasses the reviewed semantic-summary safety gates (status `resolved` — Codex `审查 PASS`, committed master 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk into M6.7 / Slice 1b cninfo provider / top15 and batch-quality contract`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-M67-CNINFO-PROVIDER-BYPASSES-SEMANTIC-SUMMARY-GATES`): the new direct `_build_cninfo_semantic_provider` is directionally useful, but it re-implements the semantic layer instead of reusing the already reviewed summary contract. Codex probes show it fetches every `weekly_candidates` item, not the semantic layer's main-board Top15 watch pool, so non-mainboard / outside-cap symbols can be requested and fed into M6.7 despite the established bounded semantic-risk boundary. It also converts a batch of 15 `ok=True` / empty-announcement cninfo rows into per-stock `clear`, bypassing `build_summary_from_fetches`' batch-empty / batch-unhealthy gate that downgrades mass-empty cninfo windows to `unknown` rather than "checked clear". A negative lookback is accepted and passed to the fetcher, which can invert the date window and produce the same false-clear signature. Malformed rows lacking `ts_code` are inserted under a `"None"` key instead of being treated as provider-quality failure for the requested candidate set. This creates the exact old risk class: a provider outage, anti-scrape empty batch, bad lookback, or malformed response can become `semantic_risk.status=clear` inside M6.7 instead of `unknown`, silently removing a semantic warning path. Required repair: centralize the weekly cninfo provider through the existing semantic summary contract or faithfully reuse its gates. Minimum: derive the semantic fetch universe with `main_board_top15(weekly_candidates)` (or the same shared helper) and do not fetch/feed non-mainboard or outside-Top15 names; validate `--cninfo-lookback-days` as a positive integer before any fetch; treat missing/malformed rows for requested codes as `unknown`, not clear, and never create a `"None"` code mapping; apply the same batch-empty / batch-unhealthy rule used by `build_summary_from_fetches` so a 15-name ok-empty batch cannot produce all-clear M6.7 semantic state. Add adversarial tests for mass-empty Top15 -> unknown, partial/missing requested code -> unknown, non-main/outside-Top15 not requested/fed, zero/negative lookback rejected or routed to unknown without clear, malformed raw row no `"None"` mapping, and happy-path official risk still feeds M6.7. Boundary: weekly semantic provider integration + tests/docs only; no DeepSeek, no production EGS ranking/scoring, no historical backtest, no real network call in tests, no V14.2 spec change. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `_build_cninfo_semantic_provider` rewritten to REUSE `build_summary_from_fetches` (the reviewed contract) instead of a thin re-impl — it now derives the universe via `main_board_top15(codes)` (non-mainboard/outside-Top15 neither fetched nor fed), drops malformed/no-`ts_code` rows (no `"None"` mapping; requested-code-missing → `not_fetched` → `unknown`), and inherits the batch-empty/unhealthy gate (mass ok-empty → `unknown`, never all-clear). `--cninfo-lookback-days` validated as positive int before any fetch (else `None`, no fetch, no false-clear). Adversarial tests added (mass-empty→unknown, missing code→unknown, ChiNext not-fetched/not-fed, zero/negative/non-int lookback rejected without fetch, malformed-row no `"None"`, genuine official risk still feeds). Resolved on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

Add adversarial tests for blank `title`, blank `category`, blank `url_or_pdf`, whitespace-only strings, and keep valid high/medium/low/clear/unknown/None green. If a future provider review proves cninfo can legitimately omit `adjunctUrl`, do not silently allow blank URL to drive `high -> 否决`; instead formally revise the contract/schema and route such events to a non-veto pending/unknown path with an explicit "missing URL/PDF" reason. Boundary: consumer validation + tests only; do not wire real cninfo provider, DeepSeek, production EGS scoring, historical backtest, deterministic promotion, or V14.2 spec changes in this repair. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `_validate_semantic_official` event-field loop now requires every `_SEM_EVENT_REQUIRED` field to be a **trimmed non-empty string** (present-but-empty / whitespace-only rejected), not just present; the `source=="cninfo"` / severity-enum / canonical-PIT-date / had_pit_announcements gates are kept. So a high event with blank `title`/`category`/`url_or_pdf` can no longer become an M6.7 advisory `否决`. Adversarial tests added (blank title, blank category, blank url_or_pdf, whitespace-only title) all fail-closed; valid PIT cninfo cases green. **Slice 1b carry-forward (per Codex escape hatch)**: `build_official_structured` emits `url_or_pdf=""` when `adjunctUrl` is absent (verified) — when the real cninfo provider is wired in Slice 1b, it MUST either guarantee a non-empty URL/PDF or route empty-URL events to a non-veto pending/unknown path (contract/schema revision), else a legitimate empty-URL event would abort the weekly; this consumer non-empty gate is intentionally strict and that reconciliation is owned by Slice 1b. Status flip to `resolved` on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

A-short semantic-official M6.7 validator accepts evidence-less or future-dated official events as advisory veto (status `resolved` 2026-06-13 commit `908f95f`(review-passed,Slice 1)Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_contract.md` if wording is adjusted; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk into M6.7 / official_structured PIT evidence contract / advisory veto`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-M67-SEMANTIC-OFFICIAL-EVIDENCE-SHAPE-GAP`): the previous input-consistency repair correctly rejects status/events contradictions and non-dict/non-list malformed shapes, but `_validate_semantic_official` currently validates only `status`, `events` list shape, and `severity`. The stable contract now says validated `official_structured` high severity may produce an advisory M6.7 `否决`, and the upstream `a_short_semantic_risk_summary` schema requires every official event to carry `source`, `title`, `category`, `disclosure_date`, `url_or_pdf`, `risk_type`, and `severity`, with `validate_summary_consistency` enforcing `risk_type/source` plus canonical PIT disclosure date `<= as_of`. Codex probes against the current repair show all of these are accepted and hard-vetoed in M6.7: `{"status":"risk","events":[{"severity":"high"}]}`, `{"severity":"high","risk_type":"x"}`, `{"severity":"high","risk_type":"x","disclosure_date":"20990101"}`, and `{"severity":"high","risk_type":"x","source":"manual"}`. That means a malformed provider stub, future-dated event, or source-less event can produce user-facing advisory `否决`, contradicting the "validated official_structured" and PIT-evidence boundary. Required repair: strengthen `_validate_semantic_official` to validate the actual official_structured event evidence shape before any M6.7 impact is derived. Minimum: require `had_pit_announcements` boolean and, for `status=="risk"`, require it true; require each event to contain non-empty `source`, `title`, `category`, `disclosure_date`, `url_or_pdf`, `risk_type`, `severity`; require `source == "cninfo"` (or formally document/test any broader allowed official source); require `disclosure_date` to be canonical YYYYMMDD and `<= as_of` (so the validator needs `as_of`, or an equivalent PIT-aware call); keep severity enum and status/events consistency. Invalid evidence must raise `ValueError` before writing. Add adversarial tests for missing required event fields, blank `risk_type`/`source`, future `disclosure_date`, malformed date, `had_pit_announcements=false` with events, and non-cninfo/manual source; keep valid official events green. Boundary: semantic official M6.7 consumer validation + tests only; do not wire real cninfo provider, DeepSeek, production EGS scoring, historical backtest, deterministic promotion, or V14.2 spec changes. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `_validate_semantic_official(sem, as_of)` now enforces the full official_structured PIT evidence contract before any M6.7 impact is derived: each event must carry non-empty `source`/`title`/`category`/`disclosure_date`/`url_or_pdf`/`risk_type`/`severity`, `source == "cninfo"`, `risk_type` non-blank, `severity` enum, `disclosure_date` canonical YYYYMMDD AND `<= as_of` (PIT — validator now takes `as_of`); plus `had_pit_announcements` must be bool and True when `status=="risk"`; clear/unknown carry no events. Invalid/fabricated/future-dated/source-less/non-PIT → `ValueError` before any build/write, so residual or non-cninfo evidence can never drive an M6.7 advisory 否决. Adversarial tests added (severity-only, missing any field, blank risk_type, non-cninfo/manual source, future date, malformed date, had_pit_announcements false/missing) all fail-closed; valid PIT cninfo high/medium/clear/unknown/None stay green. Status flip to `resolved` on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

A-short semantic-official M6.7 consumer accepts inconsistent or malformed semantic input (status `resolved` 2026-06-13 commit `908f95f`(review-passed,Slice 1)Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk into M6.7 / official_structured consumer contract / advisory M6.7 scoring`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-M67-SEMANTIC-OFFICIAL-INPUT-CONSISTENCY-GAP`): the new `semantic_provider`/`normalize_candidate(semantic=...)` path is directionally correct, but the Phase 5 engine consumes the dict without validating the official-structured contract. `classify_risk_families` only hard-vetoes when `semantic.status == "risk"` and a high event exists, while `build_m67_report` derives `machine.layer.semantic_risk.impact = "veto"` from any high event regardless of status. Codex probe showed `{"status":"clear","events":[{"severity":"high"}]}` and `{"status":"unknown","events":[{"severity":"high"}]}` produce action `建仓` but machine trace `impact=veto` / `severity_max=high` / no hard_veto, an internal contradiction for downstream consumers. A missing status with a high event has the same impact trace contradiction, while malformed events such as `events="abc"` or `events=["x"]` raise `AttributeError` mid-run rather than a controlled contract error. Existing tests cover the happy shapes only (`risk/high`, `risk/medium`, `clear`, `unknown`, `None`) and do not guard adversarial/inconsistent shapes. Required repair: add a single semantic-official input normalizer/validator before any Phase 5 consumption (acceptable locations: `a_short_phase5_engine` helper called by both `classify_risk_families` and `build_m67_report`, or a weekly normalizer plus an engine guard). It must accept only `None` or a dict with `status in {"risk","clear","unknown"}`, `events` as a list of dicts, severity in `{"high","medium","low"}`, and a consistent relation such that `clear`/`unknown` cannot carry events and `risk` must carry at least one valid event; all `impact`, `severity_max`, family action, and trace must be derived from the same sanitized object. Invalid provider output should fail closed with a clear `ValueError/SystemExit` before writing, not silently convert to conflicting machine state. Add adversarial tests for clear-with-high, unknown-with-high, missing-status-with-high, risk-with-empty-events, invalid severity, non-list events, and non-dict events; keep valid high/medium/clear/unknown/None behavior green. Boundary: semantic official input contract + tests only; do not wire real cninfo provider, DeepSeek, panel retirement, production EGS scoring, historical backtest, or V14.2 spec changes in this repair. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: added `_validate_semantic_official` (fail-closed) in `a_short_phase5_engine.py`; the semantic family / `impact` / `severity_max` / trace are now ALL derived from the single validated object (no status↔events contradiction). Contract enforced: None or dict with `status∈{risk,clear,unknown}`, `events` list-of-dicts, `severity∈{high,medium,low}`, clear/unknown carry no events, risk carries ≥1 event; invalid → `ValueError` before any build/write. severity gating moved inside `status=="risk"`, so clear/unknown can never yield `impact=veto`. Adversarial tests added (clear-with-high, unknown-with-high, missing-status, risk-empty-events, invalid-severity, non-list events, non-dict event, non-dict semantic) all fail-closed; valid high/medium/clear/unknown/None stay green. Status flip to `resolved` on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

Additional closure blocker for `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-AUX-ARTIFACT-CANDIDATE-SET-MISMATCH` (status `resolved`(已闭 commit `6709055`,review-passed 2026-06-13) Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `A-short weekly M6.7 / overlay auxiliary artifact consumer-validation / duplicate candidate lineage`): the working-tree repair correctly rejects missing overlay rows and wrong same-size overlay sets, but still accepts a schema+consistency-valid overlay artifact containing duplicate `ts_code` rows. Root cause: `_load_validated_overlay` collapses `candidates` into `{ts_code: row}` before the new lineage check, and `main` compares `set(overlay)` to `set(weekly_candidates)`. A duplicate-row artifact such as candidates `[600000.SH, 000001.SZ, 000001.SZ]` with `candidate_count=3` and contiguous `overlay_rank=1..3` validates, then dict/set collapse hides the duplicate; Codex probe confirmed `main --overlay ov_dup.json` accepted it and wrote both `weekly.json` and `weekly.md`. If the later duplicate row carries different `eligible`/`crowding_hit`, the overwrite can silently alter the consumed M6.7 overlay flags while still looking like an exact candidate set. Required repair: preserve the raw overlay candidate `ts_code` list before dict collapse and require duplicate-free exact coverage against the current weekly candidates (minimum: `len(raw_codes) == len(weekly_candidates)`, no duplicate raw codes, and `set(raw_codes) == set(weekly_candidates)`; stricter list equality is acceptable if same-run order is declared). Add a main-level adversarial test where an internally-valid overlay with a duplicate current candidate row aborts before any write, and tighten the existing overlay abort helper so it asserts `weekly.json` and `weekly.md` are each absent separately rather than only asserting they are not both present. Keep the already-fixed semantic wrong-pool, overlay missing-row, overlay wrong-set, and positive matched-overlay tests. Boundary: weekly pipeline overlay consumer-validation + tests only; do not change EGS scoring, overlay scoring formula, semantic-risk summary schema, Phase5 decisions, hard veto behavior, live web execution, production paths, or V14.2 frozen semantics. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `_load_validated_overlay` now detects duplicate `ts_code` rows on the RAW candidate list BEFORE the `{ts_code: row}` dict collapse and aborts (`SystemExit`), so a 3-row `[600000.SH,000001.SZ,000001.SZ]` overlay no longer survives the `set()` comparison. Added `test_main_overlay_duplicate_candidate_writes_no_file` (the dup overlay is schema+consistency-valid — probed — so it genuinely reaches the new dup gate). Also fixed the test-helper weakness Codex flagged: `_assert_main_overlay_aborts_no_file` now asserts `weekly.json` and `weekly.md` are each absent SEPARATELY (the old `not (a and b)` would have passed a partial write). Semantic side is already dup-safe (it compares the ordered `candidates` list, not a collapsed set). weekly suite 62 OK; five-suite 152 OK. Status flip to `resolved` on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

(1) `--semantic-risk-summary`: a schema+consistency-valid summary with the same `as_of` but `universe.main_board_top15 = [600000.SH,600001.SH,600002.SH,600003.SH]` is accepted and appended to a weekly report whose `analysis_input.candidates` are `[600000.SH,000001.SZ]`; the command writes both JSON and md. This contradicts the semantic-risk design: the artifact is for the EGS weekly `watch_n=15` main-board Top15 observation pool, not any arbitrary same-date summary. (2) `--overlay`: a schema+consistency-valid overlay with the same `as_of` but missing weekly candidate `000001.SZ` is accepted; `normalize_candidate(... overlay.get(ts_code))` silently defaults the missing row to `eligible=false/crowding=false`, so a wrong or incomplete overlay can alter M6.7 stars without a failure. For semantic risk, derive the expected main-board Top15 watch pool from the `analysis_input.candidates` order using the same main-board filter/cap semantics as the semantic summary builder, and require `summary.universe.main_board_top15` / `summary.candidates[].ts_code` to match that expected pool exactly (or document and test a stricter reviewed equivalent). For overlay, require the supplied overlay artifact to cover the weekly analysis candidates it is being consumed for (preferred: exact candidate set equality with the current analysis_input pool if the EGS side-output is same-run; acceptable minimum: no missing weekly candidate rows, with any looser extra-row allowance explicitly documented/tested). Add adversarial main-level tests for both same-date-but-wrong semantic summary and same-date-but-missing/wrong overlay aborting before any output write, plus positive tests using candidate-matched fixtures. Keep the existing partial-write repairs: invalid auxiliary artifact inputs must leave no `weekly.json` or `.md`. Boundary: weekly pipeline consumer-validation + tests only; do not change EGS scoring, overlay scoring formula, semantic-risk summary schema, Phase5 decisions, hard veto behavior, live web execution, production paths, or V14.2 frozen semantics. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `main` now binds both auxiliary artifacts to the weekly EGS candidate set BEFORE any write. (1) Semantic: expected pool = `main_board_top15([c.ts_code for c in analysis_input.candidates])`; require `summary.universe.main_board_top15` AND `[c.ts_code for c in summary.candidates]` to equal it exactly, else `ValueError` (no file). (2) Overlay: require `set(overlay) == set(weekly_candidates)` exactly (no missing/extra), else `SystemExit` (no file) — closes the silent `eligible/crowding=false` default on a missing row. Added adversarial main-level tests: semantic wrong-pool aborts-no-file; overlay missing-candidate and overlay wrong-set (both internally-valid overlays, so MY lineage check fires, not overlay-internal consistency) abort-no-file; plus positive candidate-matched semantic + overlay paths still write. Tamper/as_of/missing tests refit to matching-pool bases so the intended schema/as_of failure is what triggers. weekly suite 61 OK; five-suite 151 OK. Project doc-governance minimal-template guard skips PASS-only review headers (status `resolved`(已闭 commit `9918d84`,review-passed 2026-06-13) Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-13; severity `P3`; scope `tests/test_doc_governance_guard.py`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/AI_REVIEW_PROTOCOL.md`; PIT label `cross-LLM continuity / SESSION_LOG minimal review-cycle entries / anti-double-write`; Required ID `R-DOCGOV-MINIMAL-GUARD-PASS-HEADER-GAP`): the latest structural allowlist repair correctly catches same-day missing register pointers, Chinese free-form duplicated detail, `Finding-1`-style duplicated sections, and `修复` entries missing proof-of-use. However `_review_cycle_offenders` only treats an entry as review-cycle when the header contains `审查` or `修复`. The protocol text explicitly says "A clean Pass still needs a minimal PASS-only `docs/SESSION_LOG.md` entry" and "EVERY review-cycle ... including ... PASS", while the AGENTS template says `审查 PASS`. A future compliant-looking but PASS-only header such as `## 2026-06-14 — Codex PASS (R-TEST-FOO)` can cite a Required ID, include an extra copied problem/finding section, and be skipped completely by the guard because the header lacks `审查`/`修复`. Codex independent probe reproduced this: `pass_without_review_keyword` returned `[]` offenders while the same body under `Codex 审查 FAIL` is caught. Required repair: make the compliant-zone guard cover PASS-only review headers as well as `审查`/`修复` headers. Acceptable repair: classify as review-cycle if the header contains `审查`, `修复`, or `PASS`/`Pass` (or, more robustly, any compliant-zone entry citing an `R-*` ID unless explicitly exempted). Add a planted-failure test for a `Codex PASS (R-ID)` entry with an extra disallowed label/free-form detail, and keep the existing compliant PASS/repair cases green. Project doc-governance anti-recurrence guard is too weak for entry-format drift (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P3`; scope `tests/test_doc_governance_guard.py`, `AGENTS.md`; PIT label `startup-read-burden / docs-only / cross-LLM continuity`; Required ID `R-DOCGOV-GUARD-ENTRY-FORMAT-SCOPE-WEAK`): the latest repair correctly fixed the three concrete doc-governance findings and added a narrow guard, but `tests/test_doc_governance_guard.py::test_session_log_entry_rule_teaches_archive_pointer_exception` checks `self.assertIn("归档指针", text)` across the entire `AGENTS.md` instead of the `### Entry 格式` section. Keep the guard narrow: do not scan historical `SESSION_LOG` or archive entry bodies, and do not turn this into a full-text style linter. Closure on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

Project doc-governance simplification comprehensive review blockers (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P3`; scope `AGENTS.md`, `docs/SESSION_LOG.md`, `docs/archive/session_log/session_log_archive_2026-05-25_to_2026-06-12.md`, `docs/handoff/README.md`; PIT label `startup-read-burden / docs-only / cross-LLM continuity`; Required IDs `R-DOCGOV-SESSIONLOG-INSERT-RULE-CONFLICT`, `R-DOCGOV-ARCHIVE-HEADER-COUNT-DRIFT`, `R-DOCGOV-AGENTS-DUPLICATE-HANDOFF-INDEX`): the archive itself is structurally sound, but the broader doc-simplification contract is not yet commit-ready. `R-DOCGOV-SESSIONLOG-INSERT-RULE-CONFLICT`: `AGENTS.md` adds an archive pointer after the `SESSION_LOG` H1 intro, but the entry-format rule still says new entries go immediately after the H1 header; that stale instruction can recreate the pointer/order drift. Required repair: replace that lower handoff block with a single pointer to `docs/handoff/README.md` or make the root doc clearly not a second index. Do not delete handoff originals, undo the archive, or restore the giant active `SESSION_LOG`. Closure on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13, all 3)**: `R-DOCGOV-SESSIONLOG-INSERT-RULE-CONFLICT` — `AGENTS.md §Session log discipline → Entry 格式` rewritten: the archive pointer (if present) is the fixed meta right after the H1 intro, new dated entries go immediately after that pointer (else after the H1 intro), entries reverse-chronological. `R-DOCGOV-ARCHIVE-HEADER-COUNT-DRIFT` — archive file header line 3 reworded to a past-tense creation fact ("created after retaining the pre-archive latest 30 entries … later entries accumulate until the next archive"), no live count. `R-DOCGOV-AGENTS-DUPLICATE-HANDOFF-INDEX` — the 12-item handoff mini-index in `AGENTS.md §文件参考` (which was missing the Phase 6a handoff) replaced by a single pointer to `docs/handoff/README.md`; ripple-grep confirms no remaining `^- docs/handoff/…_handoff.md` list item in AGENTS and no other drifting "30" count / second index. route-doc guard 14/14; `git diff --check` clean; BOM/FFFD=0. **Anti-recurrence guard added (Codex round-N required, scope += `tests/test_doc_governance_guard.py`)**: a narrow doc-governance drift test pins the CURRENT rule regions so this same drift class fails automatically next time — (a) AGENTS SESSION_LOG entry-format rule must contain the archive-pointer insertion exception and must NOT contain the old "新 entry 永远 prepend …紧跟 H1" rule; (b) any `docs/archive/session_log/*.md` HEADER (split before the first dated entry, so archived bodies are never scanned — avoids false-positives on historical review records) must not restate a drifting live active-log count ("keeps the most recent 30 entries" / "只保留最近 30 条"); (c) AGENTS must keep no `^- docs/handoff/…_handoff.md` mini-index list item, only the README pointer; (d) the handoff index must reach every `*_handoff.md` (so the single index can't go stale-incomplete). 4 guard tests pass. **Guard-#1 strengthened (Codex follow-up: v1 too weak)**: check (a) no longer does a whole-file `assertIn("归档指针")` (which would stay green via §归档 even if the Entry-format rule regressed); it now extracts the `### Entry 格式` section and asserts WITHIN it `归档指针` + `指针之后` + `无指针` + absence of the old bare `新 entry 永远 prepend 到文件顶部`. Still narrow (section-scoped; no SESSION_LOG/archive body scan). Status flip to `resolved` on Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

A-short semantic-risk route/schema docs omit `unknown ⇒ action no_action` after code fix (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P3`; scope `docs/README.md`, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_web_llm_patch.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_contract_docs.py`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-2b-ii-A / web_llm doc-contract drift / advisory-only`; Required ID `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-UNKNOWN-ACTION-DOC-DRIFT`): the code-level repair for `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-UNKNOWN-ACTION-WITHOUT-EVIDENCE` is correct, and the stable contract says no-evidence web must be `unknown/unknown/no_action`; however active route/schema summaries still restate the weaker old invariant. `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md` says `unknown ⇒ risk_level unknown` and then only gives the action enum; `docs/README.md` says unknown may have empty sources and must keep risk_level unknown; the web patch schema description says unknown may have empty sources and any non-unknown needs sources. A-short semantic-risk web_llm patch accepts no-evidence `unknown` with action (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_contract.md`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_web_llm_patch.schema.json`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-2b-ii-A / web_llm evidence-action contract / advisory-only`; Required ID `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-UNKNOWN-ACTION-WITHOUT-EVIDENCE`): the current evidence-contract repair correctly rejects empty-source `clear_light` and `tailwind`, but `_web_llm_consistency_error` returns success for `status="unknown"` as soon as `risk_level=="unknown"` and does not enforce `action=="no_action"`. Independent probe: `validate_web_llm_patch` accepts `web_llm.status=unknown, risk_level=unknown, action=downgrade, sources=[]`. This conflicts with the stable contract line that未检索/检索失败/证据缺失时 web must remain `unknown/unknown/no_action`, and reopens an evidence hole: a no-evidence/no-search candidate can still carry a soft downgrade/manual-review action into M6.7. Required repair: enforce `unknown ⇒ risk_level unknown AND action no_action` in the shared `_web_llm_consistency_error`, and add regression tests in both the patch-validator path and summary-consistency path for `unknown/unknown/downgrade` (or `manual_review_required`) being rejected while `unknown/unknown/no_action` with empty sources remains accepted. Do not broaden this fix into a full status/action matrix unless separately designed; clear/tailwind/risk action semantics beyond the explicit unknown triple are a lower-priority design decision. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `_web_llm_consistency_error` (shared by summary + patch validators) now enforces `unknown ⇒ risk_level unknown AND action no_action`, so `unknown/unknown/downgrade|manual_review_required` is rejected at every web exit; `unknown/unknown/no_action` with empty sources still accepted. Regression tests both sides: patch validator (`test_a_short_semantic_risk_contract_docs.py::test_behavior_anchor_unknown_requires_no_action`) and summary consistency (`test_a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py::test_web_unknown_with_soft_action_raises`); contract drift-guard anchors the `unknown/unknown/no_action` triple in the contract doc. Scope honored — did NOT broaden to the full status/action matrix (Codex de-scoped; contract only locks the unknown triple); the non-blocking clear/tailwind/risk-action observation is acknowledged + deferred. 127 tests pass; py_compile OK. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13.

A-short semantic-risk web_llm patch can mark clear/tailwind without evidence + coverage 48h overclaim (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_web_llm_patch.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-2b-ii-A / web_llm patch evidence contract / advisory-only`; Required IDs `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-CLEAR-WITHOUT-COVERAGE`, `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-TAILWIND-WITHOUT-COVERAGE`, `R-SEMANTIC-COVERAGE-48H-OVERCLAIM`, `R-SEMANTIC-COVERAGE-WEB-INVARIANT-STALE`): the prior stale-summary and schema-name mismatch gaps are repaired in the working tree, but the patch evidence contract still violates the semantic-risk unknown-not-clear principle. Independent probes show `validate_web_llm_patch` accepts `web_llm.status=clear_light, risk_level=none, action=no_action, sources=[]` and also accepts `web_llm.status=tailwind, risk_level=none, action=observe, sources=[]`. This conflicts with the design assertions "未检索/失败→unknown,绝不伪装 clear", "无命中但检索成功→clear_light(须带 source coverage / checked_at / scope)", and the Slice-2 requirement that sources/date/confidence/action be present for web/LLM judgments. Required repair: encode a positive evidence/coverage invariant before any non-unknown web status can be merged. Recommended narrow repair is to require `sources` (or an explicit per-candidate `checked_scope` / coverage object) for `clear_light` and `tailwind` as well as risk statuses; if no source/coverage check exists, the status must remain `unknown/unknown/no_action`. Add regression tests rejecting empty-source `clear_light` and empty-source `tailwind`, and update existing happy/stale-summary tests that currently use empty-source clear patches. Documentation repair: `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md` must not say exact "监管 48h" is structurally built by the cninfo layer, because the runner default is a 90-day cninfo lookback and only enforces `disclosure_date <= as_of`; rewrite as broader PIT official-announcement evidence over the configured lookback, with exact 48h freshness / media-negative judgment left to the 2b-ii-B skill/prompt or a future recency field. No production/EGS/Phase5 behavior, data fetch, hard veto, or historical-backtest claim is authorized by this repair. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-CLEAR-WITHOUT-COVERAGE` + `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-TAILWIND-WITHOUT-COVERAGE` — `_web_llm_consistency_error` (shared by summary + patch validators) now requires `sources` for ANY non-`unknown` status (clear_light / tailwind / risk_candidate / risk / headwind); only `unknown` may have empty sources, so "未检索→unknown,不伪装 clear" holds at every web exit. Headless summaries (web_llm always unknown) are unaffected. Regression tests added (`test_clear_light_without_coverage_raises`, `test_tailwind_without_coverage_raises`, `test_unknown_may_have_empty_sources`); existing patch tests updated so clear/tailwind patches carry evidence (`_pc` default sources now non-empty). `R-SEMANTIC-COVERAGE-48H-OVERCLAIM` — `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md` row reworded: official_structured provides broader PIT official-announcement evidence over the configured cninfo lookback (default 90d, disclosure_date ≤ as_of), NOT an exact 48h window; exact 48h / media-negative judgment left to 2b-ii-B skill or a future recency field. 55 summary tests pass; py_compile OK. **Codex re-`审查` FAIL residual `R-SEMANTIC-COVERAGE-WEB-INVARIANT-STALE` (2026-06-13)**: runner behavior was fixed, and the current working tree also repairs the residual doc drift: `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_contract.md` is the stable contract anchor; `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_coverage.md` states that any non-unknown/evaluated web status requires `sources` and only `unknown` may have empty `sources`; `docs/README.md` routes to the stable contract and no longer contains the old `web risk-status ⇒ sources required` wording; `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_web_llm_patch.schema.json` points to the validator as cross-field authority; `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_contract_docs.py` adds behavior + doc-drift guards for empty-source clear/tailwind, unknown empty-source, stale README/coverage wording, and exact-48h overclaim. Status flip to `resolved` only after Codex re-`审查` PASS + `提交`.

A-short semantic-risk web_llm patch replacement/schema-name contract gaps (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_web_llm_patch.schema.json`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-2b-ii-A / web_llm patch merge contract / advisory-only`; Required IDs `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-STALE-SUMMARY`, `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-SCHEMA-NAME-MISMATCH`): Slice 2b-ii-A tests pass, but independent probes found two contract gaps. `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-STALE-SUMMARY`: `apply_web_llm_patch` advertises replacement semantics for `web_llm/sources/confidence/summary`, but only writes `summary` when the patch candidate includes it. If a prior enriched candidate had `summary="old risk summary"` and a later replacement patch sets the same candidate to `clear_light` without a `summary`, the old risk summary remains while `web_llm.status=clear_light` and `sources=[]`. Required repair: define replacement semantics for optional summary; recommended behavior is to set `c["summary"] = pc.get("summary", "")` (or another explicit neutral value) whenever a candidate is patched, and add a regression test that a risk patch with summary followed by a clear patch without summary clears the stale text. `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-SCHEMA-NAME-MISMATCH`: the patch schema carries `target.summary_schema_name`, but `apply_web_llm_patch` checks only target `as_of` and `summary_schema_version`; a summary object with `schema_name="wrong_schema_name"` and version `1.0.0` is accepted and returned unchanged. Required repair: check `patch["target"]["summary_schema_name"] == summary["schema_name"] == "a_short_semantic_risk_summary"` (or equivalent) before merge, and add a regression test. No production/EGS/Phase5 behavior, data fetch, hard veto, or historical-backtest claim is authorized by this repair. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-13)**: `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-STALE-SUMMARY` — `apply_web_llm_patch` now ALWAYS sets `c["summary"]` on every patched candidate: the patch's `summary` if provided, else a regenerated `官方结构化: <official_status>; web/LLM: <status>/<risk_level>/<action>` reflecting the CURRENT merged state (never leaves stale text); regression test `test_no_stale_summary_after_clear_overwrite` (risk+summary → clear-without-summary clears the old text + reflects clear_light). `R-SEMANTIC-WEBPATCH-SCHEMA-NAME-MISMATCH` — apply now enforces `target.summary_schema_name == summary.schema_name == SCHEMA_NAME` before merge; regression test `test_summary_schema_name_mismatch_raises`. 52 summary tests pass; py_compile OK. A-short semantic-risk advisory summary — Slice 2b-i keyword calibration + panel render (status `resolved` Claude `起草`+`修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-13; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-2b-i / headless keyword calibration + M6.7 panel / advisory-only`): Slice 2b split into 2b-i (headless keyword calibration + panel, fully testable) + 2b-ii (skill enrichment contract for web_llm + coverage doc, skill-in-loop). Driven by the Slice-2a 执行 finding (2026-06-12): broad keyword `资金占用` matched the bank annual-season routine "…非经营性资金占用…情况专项说明/汇总表" — coarse headless matching can't reliably disambiguate routine-vs-real, so substance goes to the 2b-ii web/LLM skill.

CURRENT MECHANISM (final — narrowest strategy):
- `RISK_KEYWORD_MAP` carries `severity` (high 立案/处罚/ST · medium 监管函件/关注/资金占用/担保 · low 诉讼/仲裁); official_structured events carry `severity` (schema enum high/medium/low).
- `_is_routine_occupation_report` suppresses a fund_occupation match ONLY when the title is a routine disclosure form (`ROUTINE_OCCUPATION_FORMS` = 专项说明/专项审核/汇总表) AND carries an explicit no-occupation negation (`NO_OCCUPATION_NEGATIONS` = 不存在/未发生/未形成/无新增/无占用/…). high severity is never suppressed.
- Everything else → risk for the 2b-ii skill to downgrade: a bare routine report WITHOUT an explicit negation (incl. the exact 浦发 专项说明 / 关联资金往来情况汇总表) surfaces as `risk[medium]`; explicit/suspected occupation (存在/发生/被…占用) and 整改/违规担保/问询/立案/处罚 → risk. **Property: residual errors are false-positives only (skill downgrades), never 漏报** (a missed negation word merely shows one no-occupation report as risk — harmless). Tested both ways (`test_only_routine_form_WITH_negation_is_suppressed` clears 不存在/未发生/无新增/不存在被…占用; `test_bare_routine_without_negation_now_surfaces_as_risk` + `test_explicit_or_suspected_occupation_surfaces_as_risk`).
- `render_semantic_risk_panel(summary)`: pure M6.7/weekly-visible markdown block (advisory/non-reproducible labels, coverage, lists only flagged risk/unknown/needs-review candidates with official severity+latest-date + web_llm cell + 需人工复核, clear summarized; never mixed into deterministic reports). Panel WIRING into the weekly pipeline deferred (render function only here).

SUPERSEDED interim (do NOT re-introduce): rounds 1-3 used a blanket `NEGATIVE_PATTERNS` then an `_has_adverse_marker`/`ESCALATION_MARKERS` override — both DELETED; they caused 漏报 on negated forms (未发生/无新增/不存在被…占用) across a 5-round whack-a-mole, hence the narrowest inversion above. The pre-calibration artifact `research/results/a_short/semantic_risk_summary_20260612.json` (old shape, no `severity`) was DELETED.

A-short semantic-risk advisory summary — Slice 2a headless backbone (status `resolved` Claude `起草`+`修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`, `tests/test_semantic_risk_slice3_guard.py`, `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py` (Sina hardening), `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-2a / headless structured advisory / production-isolated`): second build slice of the semantic-risk layer (Slice 2 split into 2a headless backbone + 2b skill/panel to keep each reviewable, given the Slice-1 4-round PIT saga). Ships the anti-forget CI guard `test_semantic_risk_slice3_guard.py` (advisory-layer-exists ⇒ register Slice-3 reconciliation tracker must remain present; **tracker-present anti-forget, NOT fail-until-Slice3-done** — a literal fail-until-done would block the staged plan the same design mandates; **Codex Finding-2 fix: design §8 + this deferred-open tracker + the test docstring are now all aligned on this reading**). 82 tests pass (33 summary/guard + 49 probe); schema meta + py_compile OK. A-short semantic-risk probe cninfo fetch fix — orgId resolution (status `resolved` Claude `起草` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / cninfo provider request-shape fix / fetch-layer only`): the first real `执行` 2026-06-12 (sample 15 main-board codes) found cninfo `hisAnnouncement/query` with the egs-stage3 bare `stock`="code,sh/sz" shape returns HTTP-200-but-empty for ALL codes (`n_ok=15`, `n_announced=0`, `failure_categories={}`) — not a transport failure, a request-shape/soft-anti-scrape issue; liquid names like 600519/601318 cannot truly have zero 90-day announcements. Probe behaved correctly (reported not-feasible, did NOT fabricate clear). Fetch-layer fix (pure assess/validate core UNCHANGED): `fetch_cninfo_orgid_map` resolves {6-digit code: orgId} from cninfo's `sse_stock.json`/`szse_stock.json` (env-overridable `CNINFO_ORGID_URLS`, defensive parse); `fetch_cninfo` now sends `stock`="code,orgId" with layered failure semantics (orgId list unfetchable → all `orgid_map_failed`; list ok but code missing → `no_orgid`; HTTP 403/429 → `anti_scrape`) + a small inter-request delay (default 0.3s) to mitigate soft-blocking. 5 new no-network fetch-layer tests (FakeSession: orgId map parse, map-fetch-failure, orgId actually used in the POST `stock` param, no_orgid, orgid_map_failed → all codes + failure tally) on top of the 44 pure tests = 49. Closure on Codex PASS + `提交`; then re-`执行` to empirically confirm orgId returns real announcements (the hypothesis this draft implements).

A-short semantic-risk provider probe README status-invariant wording drift (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / route-doc status invariant / cross-LLM continuity`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-README-STATUS-INVARIANT-DRIFT`): the code-level repair for `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-BAD-ROW-CLEAR-STATUS` is correct, and the active README row correctly says provider-quality defects map to `unknown`; however the same row still summarizes `validate_probe_summary_consistency` as `failure⇔unknown invariant`. That phrase describes the pre-repair invariant and is now wrong: the implementation intentionally uses `not ok ⇒ unknown` plus `clear/risk ⇒ ok + quality-clean`; cninfo may have `ok=True` and `status='unknown'` when provider quality is invalid (future / unparseable / non-dict / missing-field / mapping-mismatch). Because `docs/README.md` is the active durable routing table, this stale phrase can mislead a future agent into restoring the old `ok⇔not-unknown` invariant and reintroducing the just-fixed status bug. Required repair: replace `failure⇔unknown invariant` in the semantic-risk provider probe row with wording aligned to implementation, e.g. `status invariant [not ok⇒unknown; clear/risk⇒ok+quality-clean]`. No code/schema/provider fetch/production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12)**: the README semantic-risk provider probe row's `validate_probe_summary_consistency` description now reads `status invariant [not ok⇒unknown; clear/risk⇒ok+quality-clean]`. Repo-wide scan confirms remaining `failure⇔unknown` occurrences are only in append-only historical SESSION_LOG entries and in this/the prior finding's narrative (describing the defect), not in any active contract claim; the active register Slice-1 entry already carries the corrected wording. Docs-only. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short semantic-risk provider probe reports provider-quality-invalid cninfo rows as `clear_light` (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_probe_summary.schema.json` if status enum is extended, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / per-code status semantics / unknown-not-clear`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-BAD-ROW-CLEAR-STATUS`): the P1 feasibility bypass is repaired, but `classify_cninfo_code` still sets `status="clear_light"` whenever `ok=True` and there is no PIT-valid announcement, even if the provider returned future-dated rows, unparseable disclosure dates, or non-dict malformed rows. Codex synthetic no-network probes after the latest repair show `future`, `bad_date`, and `bad_shape` samples now correctly produce `feasible=false` and reject forged `feasible=true`, but the affected per-code object still reports `status='clear_light'` with `dates_pit_ok=false` and one of `n_future_dated` / `n_unparseable_dates` / `n_bad_shape` > 0. That is not "successfully checked and clear"; it is an unknown / provider-quality-invalid state and contradicts the design/route wording that failure/unknown must not be disguised as clear. The same semantic hole can exist for PIT rows whose required fields or `secCode` mapping fail: feasibility blocks, but the per-code status can still read `clear_light`. Required repair: define and enforce status semantics so `clear_light` is allowed only for a genuinely successful clear result (empty successful window, or PIT-clean non-risk rows with required fields and mapping valid). Any code with future rows, unparseable dates, malformed rows, missing required fields, or code-mapping mismatch must be `unknown` or a formally added invalid-status enum. If using `unknown`, update `_check_provider_counts` so `ok=True` provider-quality failures may be `unknown`, while transport failures still must be `unknown`. Add adversarial tests for future-only, unparseable-only, non-dict-only, missing-field, and mapping-mismatch rows not being `clear_light`, plus a consistency test that a summary containing such an invalid row labelled `clear_light` raises. No real provider fetch or production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, reused `unknown`)**: `classify_cninfo_code` now computes `quality_ok = (no future/unparseable/non-dict defects AND n_pit>0 AND required_fields_ok AND code_mapping_ok)` and assigns `status` as: not ok → `unknown`; ok + empty window → `clear_light`; ok + not quality_ok → `unknown`; ok + quality_ok + risk-keyword → `risk_candidate`; else `clear_light`. So future/unparseable/non-dict/missing-field/mapping-mismatch codes now read `unknown`, never `clear`. `_check_provider_counts` relaxed: the strict `ok ⇔ not-unknown` invariant became `not ok ⇒ unknown` (transport failure) for both providers + a cninfo per-code status-quality invariant (clear_light/risk_candidate require ok + clean + valid fields/mapping; risk_candidate requires PIT rows) while sina keeps `ok ⇒ not unknown`. Adversarial tests added (future / unparseable / non-dict / missing-field / mapping-mismatch → status `unknown`; + consistency test that a defective row labelled `clear_light` raises). Status enum unchanged (reused `unknown`); 44 tests pass; schema meta + py_compile OK. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short semantic-risk provider probe ignores unparseable / malformed cninfo returned rows in feasibility gate (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_probe_summary.schema.json` if fields are added; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / cninfo provider-shape and disclosure-date gate`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-BAD-DATE-SHAPE-BYPASS`): the future-date PIT leak was repaired, but returned rows with bad shape can still bypass feasibility. `classify_cninfo_code` filters `announcements` to dict rows before counting `n_returned`, so non-dict provider rows vanish completely. Rows with unparseable `announcementTime` set per-code `dates_pit_ok=False`, but because they yield `n_announcements=0`, they are excluded from the aggregate `announced` set and do not affect `n_dates_pit_ok`; a sample with 4 clean PIT-announced codes plus one unparseable-date returned row still produced `feasible=True`, no blocking reasons, and that bad-date code status `clear_light`. A sample with 4 clean PIT-announced codes plus one non-dict announcement row also produced `feasible=True` after silently treating the malformed row as no return. This violates the Slice-1 goal of proving cninfo field/date shape before Slice 2. Required repair: count raw returned items before filtering, expose and gate invalid returned rows (`n_unparseable_date_codes` / `n_bad_shape_codes` or equivalent), and ensure non-dict rows or unparseable disclosure dates cannot be reported as clean and cannot coexist with `feasible=True`. Add adversarial tests for unparseable dates, non-dict announcement rows, and mixed clean+bad-shape samples; keep the future-date PIT tests passing. No real provider fetch or production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12)**: `classify_cninfo_code` now counts `n_returned` on the RAW provider list BEFORE any `isinstance` filter, and partitions every returned row exactly into `n_announcements`(PIT dict+canonical+≤as_of) + `n_future_dated` + `n_unparseable_dates`(dict, bad/missing date) + `n_bad_shape`(non-dict), with a consistency assert that the four sum to `n_returned`; non-dict rows are no longer silently dropped. Three provider quality gates (`n_future_dated_codes==0`, `n_unparseable_date_codes==0`, `n_bad_shape_codes==0`) are now feasibility AND consistency conditions, so a malformed/unparseable/future row can neither be reported clean (`dates_pit_ok` == "all returned rows clean") nor coexist with `feasible=true`. Adversarial tests added: unparseable-date row counted-not-dropped, non-dict row counted-not-dropped, partition-sums-to-n_returned, and the exact Codex repros (4 clean + 1 unparseable-date code → feasible=false + reason; 4 clean + 1 non-dict-row code → feasible=false + reason). 43 tests pass; schema + consistency updated. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short semantic-risk provider probe accepts future-dated cninfo disclosures as PIT-valid (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_probe_summary.schema.json` if fields are added; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / cninfo PIT disclosure-date gate`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-FUTURE-DATE-PIT-LEAK`): `classify_cninfo_code(raw, as_of)` receives `as_of` but only checks that `announcementTime` is parseable; it does not verify parsed disclosure dates are `<= as_of`, and `fetch_cninfo` passes provider announcements through without a PIT cap. Codex synthetic probe with four future `2099-01-01` announcement rows and `as_of=20260630` returned `feasible=True`, `n_announced=4`, `n_dates_parse_ok=4`, and no blocking reasons. This violates the probe's own claim that cninfo disclosure dates are PIT-capable and can let future provider rows shape the official structured layer for Slice 2. Required repair: make cninfo date validation PIT-aware: parsed `announcementTime` must be canonical and `<= as_of`; future-dated rows must not count toward `n_announced`, `n_dates_parse_ok`, `n_code_mapping_ok`, `n_risk_candidate`, or feasibility. Either expose a future-row counter and fail the provider PIT gate, or mark the affected code date gate invalid so `feasible=false`. Add adversarial tests for future canonical strings, future epoch-ms, and mixed past/future rows, including a keyword future row that must not produce `risk_candidate`. No real provider fetch or production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12)**: `classify_cninfo_code` now PIT-caps inside the pure function (does not trust the fetch window) — a row counts as evidence only if its parsed disclosure date is canonical AND ≤ as_of; future-dated rows are excluded from `n_announcements`, `risk_candidate`, fields/mapping judgments, and feasibility, and are tallied in a per-code `n_future_dated` + aggregate `n_future_dated_codes`; the provider feasibility gate now requires `n_future_dated_codes == 0`; `dates_parse_ok` was renamed to `dates_pit_ok` (canonical AND ≤ as_of, over ALL returned rows) and `n_returned` added per code. Adversarial tests added: future canonical string, future epoch-ms, future regulator-keyword row (cannot be `risk_candidate`), mixed past/future, and the exact Codex 4×future-only repro (now `feasible=false`, `n_announced=0`, `n_future_dated_codes=4`). 38 tests pass; schema + consistency updated. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short semantic-risk provider feasibility probe — Slice 1 (status `resolved` Claude `起草`+`修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_probe_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 provider feasibility / probe-only / production-isolated`): first build slice of the semantic-risk layer. Boundary: no hard-veto / no EGS-scoring change / no Phase5-decision change / no historical-backtest evidence / no production-path write / no fetch without 执行; V14.2 frozen; production stage3 untouched. Closure on Codex PASS + `提交`; then real-fetch 执行 (user-authorized) → evaluate → Slice 2.

A-short semantic-risk layer ↔ production reconciliation — DEFERRED, DO NOT CLOSE until done (status `deferred-open` 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `A-EGS/egs_main.py::stage3_ai_clearing`, the future semantic-risk advisory layer, `docs/a_short_semantic_risk_top15_enrichment_design_20260612.md` §1/§8 Slice 3; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk production integration / legacy-conflict / anti-forget`): the semantic-risk Top15 advisory layer is deliberately **isolated** from production `egs_main` for now (de-risk production, isolate scrape-grade providers, avoid touching the legacy LLM hard-veto, cheap iteration). Isolation is **staged, not permanent**. Two debts remain OPEN and must NOT be silently dropped: **(1) DeepSeek `POL-RISK-VETO` legacy-conflict** — production stage3 already does an LLM(DeepSeek)+web → industry hard-veto, which contradicts the new "web/LLM advisory-only, never hard-veto" principle; it is left untouched for now and must be explicitly reconciled (migrate to advisory, or re-govern as a reviewed deterministic veto). **(2) cninfo duplication / divergence** — stage3 has its own cninfo regulatory check (4 keywords, `cninfo_flag`) while the new layer will run an extended cninfo PIT check; two parallel cninfo paths can drift in keywords/caliber. Closure condition: ONLY after the advisory layer (Slice 2) is built + validated, run **Slice 3 (deterministic promotion)** — reconcile/unify the cninfo paths and decide the DeepSeek path's fate via a reviewed analyzer + governance + schema + PIT/forward-evidence slice. Until Slice 3 lands, this entry stays open as the standing anti-forget tracker. **CI-guard (LANDED with Slice 2a, `tests/test_semantic_risk_slice3_guard.py`)**: a **tracker-present anti-forget guard** — once advisory-layer code exists (`runners/a_short_semantic_risk_summary.py`), the test asserts THIS reconciliation tracker is still present in the register (carries the `DEFERRED, DO NOT CLOSE` marker AND still names Slice 3 / POL-RISK-VETO / cninfo); it FAILS only if the tracker is silently deleted/degraded. **Deliberate interpretation (NOT the literal "fail until reconciliation closed")**: a fail-until-closed guard would build-block the very staged delivery this same design mandates (Slice 3 comes AFTER the advisory layer is built+validated). So the guard ensures Slice 3 is not FORGOTTEN, not that it is done first; it does NOT block Slice 2a/2b commits. When Slice 3 actually lands it flips this tracker to `resolved` and updates/retires the guard. (Design §8 + this entry + the test docstring are aligned on this reading.) No production code change yet; production stage3 + V14.2 untouched.

A-short V14.3 strict main-board helper accepts malformed accepted-prefix codes (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py`, `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_share_board_scope.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / strict A-share main-board universe integrity before bootstrap`; Required ID `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-MALFORMED-LEAK`): the new inclusion-based `is_a_share_main_board` correctly rejects normal B-share / ChiNext / STAR / BSE codes, but it only checks `symbol.startswith(...)`. Independent probe showed malformed accepted-prefix values such as `600ABC.SH`, `60000.SH`, `6000000.SH`, `00000A.SZ`, and `002.SZ` return `True`. This contradicts the helper comment / runner docstring claim that unknown or malformed codes are rejected, and leaves a non-canonical code path able to enter the regime breadth universe. Required repair: require canonical six-digit Tushare-style symbols before applying the exact prefix whitelist (e.g. `^\d{6}\.(SH|SZ)$` plus SSE `600/601/603/605`, SZSE `000/001/002/003`), and add adversarial tests for non-six-digit and non-numeric accepted-prefix symbols. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `is_a_share_main_board` now requires `len(symbol)==6` and all-ASCII-digit BEFORE the prefix whitelist, so `600ABC.SH`/`60000.SH`/`6000000.SH`/`00000A.SZ`/`002.SZ`/`600000.BJ` are rejected; canonical 6-digit accepted prefixes still pass. Tests extended with those malformed-under-accepted-prefix negatives. Verification 218/218 OK.
