A-short V14.3 pre-bootstrap main-board filter B-share leak (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_share_board_scope.py`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.schema.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / main-board A-share universe integrity before bootstrap`; Required ID `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-FILTER-BSHARE-LEAK`): the current repair correctly tries to scope V14.3 regime breadth to the user's A-share main-board universe, and the governance/design now declare the exact universe as SSE `600/601/603/605` plus SZSE `000/001/002/003`, excluding ChiNext/STAR/BSE. However the implemented `main_board_only()` calls the shared `is_main_board_ts_code`, whose current logic is exclusion-based and treats any code not starting with `300/301/688/689/920/8/4` and not ending `.BJ` as `main`. Independent probe showed `main_board_only(["600000.SH","000001.SZ","002001.SZ","003001.SZ","200001.SZ","900901.SH","300750.SZ","688981.SH","920083.BJ"])` keeps `200001.SZ` and `900901.SH`, which are outside the declared A-share main-board prefix set. This can either fail the first bootstrap again if such symbols lack usable `stk_limit`, or contaminate the comparison ledger's breadth metrics with non-target symbols. Required repair: enforce the exact governance universe in the runner or tighten the shared board helper if that is the intended project-wide contract; reject B-share prefixes such as `200*` / `900*`, unknown/malformed codes, and existing non-main boards; add adversarial tests for those negatives plus the positive `600/601/603/605/000/001/002/003` cases; keep docs/governance/schema aligned to one source. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added an INCLUSION-based `is_a_share_main_board` to the shared `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py` (accepts ONLY `.SH` 600/601/603/605 + `.SZ` 000/001/002/003; rejects B-shares 200*/900*, ChiNext/STAR/BSE, and unknown/malformed) — added as a NEW function so the many existing exclusion-based `is_main_board_ts_code` callers (A-long) are untouched. `run_regime_step.main_board_only` now uses `is_a_share_main_board`. Tests: shared-helper inclusion/rejection (incl. 200001.SZ/900901.SH/garbage/None) + runner `main_board_only` drops B-shares + the `_init_pro` delegation test (Optional). The Optional register/external-memory-reference wording was also dropped (docs state the main-board rule directly). Verification: full V14.3 + board-scope + route-doc 218/218 OK.

A-short V14.3 regime runner pre-bootstrap probe fixes — Tushare init + main-board universe (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / real-data de-risk before bootstrap`): a throwaway pre-bootstrap real-Tushare fetch probe (over the real latest data date 20260611; wrote nothing) found two issues that would have broken the first authorized bootstrap of the just-committed ②b-2 runner (`707d5d8`). **(1) Tushare init silent-empty**: `_init_pro` used `ts.set_token` + `ts.pro_api`, which hits Tushare's silent-empty-DataFrame failure (trade_cal/daily returned 0 rows) — a bootstrap would have silently fetched nothing and could persist an empty/garbage ledger as "success". Fixed to use the repo's sanctioned `runners.a_short_iv_feed_probe.init_tushare_pro(token)` (pins base URL, no set_token). **(2) Universe not scoped → fail-closed trip**: the full-market fetch included a BSE stock (`920083.BJ`) lacking a usable stk_limit, so the as_of fail-closed gate raised (1/5511 uncovered). Per the user's standing directive (A-shares are operated main-board only), `run_regime_step` now filters daily/stk_limit to main board via the strict inclusion-based `engine.data.a_share_board_scope.is_a_share_main_board` (see the `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-FILTER-BSHARE-LEAK` entry for the B-share-leak follow-up), and the frozen governance `universe` const + design §2 are updated from generic "A-share universe" to "MAIN-BOARD A-share universe (SSE 600/601/603/605 + SZSE 000/001/002/003; excludes ChiNext/STAR/BSE)" so spec=impl. Re-probe after fixes: main-board as_of = 3190 stocks, 0 uncovered, `compute_regime_daily_features(20260611)` succeeds (limit_up=65/down=53/net=12/streak=3/promo=0.159/failed=0.387/pct_ma20=13.8/csi300=-0.55%/csi1000_below=True), row passes daily schema + ledger semantics. Tests added: `main_board_only` filters ChiNext/STAR/BSE; a .BJ-without-stk_limit frame fails closed unfiltered but computes after main-board filtering. Verification: full V14.3 + route-doc 214/214 OK. Boundary unchanged (comparison-only, V14.2 frozen, egs_main untouched); the bootstrap RUN remains a separate user-authorized `执行` + TUSHARE_TOKEN.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner short-calendar append failure (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / persisted ledger append calendar coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-SHORT-CALENDAR-BLOCKS-WEEKLY-APPEND`): normal post-bootstrap weekly execution fetches only a short recent calendar window (`45` days) and passes that same calendar into `save_ledger` / `validate_ledger`, but the persisted ledger already contains older bootstrap rows and the sanctioned ledger validator requires the supplied trade calendar to cover the existing coverage. Independent probe with an existing 252-row ledger plus a recent 45-day calendar failed with `ValueError: invalid ledger: ledger contains dates not on the trade calendar`, so the first real steady weekly append after bootstrap is likely blocked even when new data are valid. Required repair: for existing ledgers, validate/plan against a calendar covering persisted ledger coverage plus the new run date (or fetch calendar from existing coverage start/last date through `as_of`) and add a regression test with a persisted 252-row ledger plus short recent fetch window. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `main()` builds the trade calendar to SPAN the persisted ledger (`coverage.start`→`as_of` for an existing ledger; ~400d back for bootstrap) while fetching `daily`/`stk_limit` only for the recent window actually computed (new dates + MA20 lookback). Test `test_weekly_append_with_spanning_calendar_and_narrow_daily` (252-row ledger + spanning calendar + 30-day daily → appends). Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner thin bootstrap evidence gap (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / bootstrap minimum evidence coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-THIN-BOOTSTRAP-COUNTS-EVIDENCE`): absent-ledger runs currently bootstrap from whatever calendar is provided, and an independent probe with only 30 synthetic trading dates wrote a 30-row ledger and produced panel/evidence counts. This violates the 252-day percentile-ledger intent and can make a failed/partial first execution look like a valid comparison artifact. Required repair: when the ledger is absent, non-bootstrap mode should reject; bootstrap mode should require the policy minimum eligible trading-day coverage before writing/counting evidence, with tests for missing-ledger non-bootstrap rejection and insufficient bootstrap coverage rejection. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `run_regime_step` gained `bootstrap` — an absent/empty ledger with `bootstrap=False` raises, and a bootstrap yielding `< BACKFILL_MIN_TRADING_DAYS` (252) rows raises (no evidence clock from an insufficient window); `main()` also refuses a first run without `--bootstrap`. Tests: no-ledger+no-bootstrap raise, 6-day bootstrap raise, 252-day bootstrap persists. Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner IV feed validation gap (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_build.py`, `schemas/a_short_iv_feed.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / IV percentile input integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-IV-FEED-UNVALIDATED`): `iv_series_to_map()` blindly consumes any `series` rows and silently overwrites duplicate dates; a wrong-schema feed with duplicate `20240102` values and a future `29991231` row was accepted as `{'20240102': 95.0, '29991231': 88.0}`. Because IV percentile can directly trigger V14.3 defense logic, an unvalidated or corrupt IV feed can flip comparison labels. Required repair: validate the loaded IV feed against the feed schema and summary-consistency guard (or equivalent canonical-date/duplicate/range checks) before mapping, and add adversarial tests for wrong schema, duplicate dates, non-canonical/future dates, and non-finite/out-of-range values. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `iv_series_to_map` now calls `validate_iv_feed` first — jsonschema against `a_short_iv_feed.schema.json` + the feed's own `validate_feed_summary_consistency` (reuse single-source: strictly-ascending/no-dup/no-future trade_date, iv_value>0, percentile 0-100) — so a wrong-schema / duplicate / future feed raises before mapping. Tests: duplicate-date and wrong-schema feed rejected. Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner CLI as_of leniency (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / authorized fetch date guard`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-CLI-ASOF-LENIENT-FETCH`): `main()` relies on `datetime.strptime(as_of, "%Y%m%d")`, which accepts malformed short dates such as `2024011` as `20240101`; an independent monkeypatch probe showed `_fetch_trade_calendar` was called with malformed `end=2024011`. The earlier ledger repairs made canonical date validation explicit, but this runner can still reach the authorized fetch layer before applying that guard. Required repair: reject non-canonical `YYYYMMDD` `--as-of` before `_init_pro()` or any provider fetch, using the ledger canonical-date helper or identical logic, and add a test proving malformed dates fail before fetch initialization. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `main()` calls `is_canonical_date(as_of)` (single-source ledger helper) and `SystemExit`s on a malformed `--as-of` before `_init_pro()` / any fetch. Test `test_noncanonical_as_of_rejected_before_fetch` (`2024011` → SystemExit, no provider touched). Optional findings also addressed: `save_comparison_records` now validates each record + rejects duplicate as_of; route docs narrowed to "CLI default writes under the lane; all writers refuse production `result/a_short` paths". Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 regime comparison runner (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / I/O + persistence / authorized bootstrap 执行`): the I/O + execution layer, deliberately a STANDALONE runner (NOT an egs_main side-output — egs_main is production-frozen-adjacent and the ledger amortizes the 252-day cost, so a standalone runner stays isolated; **design deviation from §5's "EGS run side-output", evaluated & documented**). Real-fetch/CLI fetch body not unit-tested (the authorized execution layer, mirrors weekly_pipeline / iv_feed). Panel = standalone lane artifact (frozen weekly M6.7 untouched). Pre-flight self-sweep run before review. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner` 12/12 + full V14.3 212/212 + route-doc OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 empty/no-eligible bootstrap calendar accepted by orchestration (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex deeper `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / bootstrap calendar coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PIPELINE-EMPTY-BOOTSTRAP-CALENDAR-ACCEPTED`): `extend_ledger` is now the public pure orchestration step for both one-time bootstrap and weekly increment, but it currently inherits the low-level ledger rule that an empty ledger can be valid pre-bootstrap. Probe: `extend_ledger(build_ledger([]), "20240105", [], provider)` and `extend_ledger(build_ledger([]), "20240105", ["20240110","20240111"], provider)` both returned a valid 0-row ledger with `coverage.n=0` and made zero provider calls. That is safe for `validate_ledger` as an envelope/pre-bootstrap gate, but unsafe for the orchestration function because slice ②b-2's bootstrap runner is expected to call `extend_ledger(empty, ...)` and persist the returned ledger; a failed/empty calendar fetch or wrong calendar range could be written as a successful empty bootstrap artifact. Required repair: in `extend_ledger`, reject empty final ledgers when bootstrapping/extending from an empty existing ledger and no eligible trading days `<= as_of` were available (or otherwise make the caller explicitly acknowledge pre-bootstrap/no-data mode, not the default workflow). Add tests for an empty calendar and for a calendar whose dates are all after `as_of`. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `extend_ledger` now raises when bootstrapping from an empty existing ledger produces no rows (empty/all-future calendar), so a bad calendar fetch can't be persisted as a successful empty bootstrap; idempotent non-empty rerun unaffected. Tests: empty calendar and all-after-as_of calendar both raise. Verification 200/200 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 trade-calendar iterator consumption in orchestration (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / calendar materialization`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PIPELINE-CALENDAR-ITERATOR-CONSUMED`): `extend_ledger(existing_ledger, as_of, trade_calendar, feature_provider)` accepts `trade_calendar: Iterable[str]` but passes the same iterable to `validate_ledger_for_append`, `plan_append`, and final `validate_ledger` without materializing it once. Because each lower-level helper materializes its own iterable, a one-shot generator is consumed by the first validation call. Probe: `extend_ledger(build_ledger([]), cal[-1], (d for d in cal), provider)` returned an empty valid ledger and made zero provider calls instead of bootstrapping the calendar rows; `weekly_regime_step` with the same generator failed later only because `classify_raw_regime` saw empty history. This reintroduces the prior generator-calendar bug class at the new orchestration layer and can let slice ②b-2 persist an empty bootstrap ledger if it supplies a generator. Required repair: materialize `trade_calendar` once at the start of `extend_ledger` / `weekly_regime_step` and reuse that list for every downstream helper call. Add tests proving generator calendars bootstrap and weekly-step correctly. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** both `extend_ledger` and `weekly_regime_step` now `list(trade_calendar)` once at the top and reuse it for all helper calls. Test: a generator calendar bootstraps the full rows. Verification 200/200 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 weekly comparison rerun is not idempotent for current as_of (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / rerun recovery`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PIPELINE-WEEKLY-RERUN-DUPLICATE-CURRENT`): `weekly_regime_step` always appends the newly built current comparison record to `prior_comparison_records`. If the prior comparison history already contains the same `as_of` from a previous successful or partial same-date run, the audited evidence path rejects the duplicate and the whole step fails. Probe: a fresh ledger through `as_of` plus `prior_comparison_records=[build_comparison_record(... as_of=same day)]` raised `ValueError: duplicate as_of`. The next ②b-2 persistence/EGS wiring slice needs repeatable weekly execution and recovery semantics; this function is only ledger-idempotent, not full-step idempotent. Required repair: before appending current, define same-date comparison-record handling: if the prior current record matches the newly built/audited record, reuse/dedupe it; if it differs, raise an immutable-history conflict. Add tests for same-date identical rerun success and same-date divergent conflict. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `weekly_regime_step` now checks `prior_comparison_records` for the current `as_of` before appending: if present with identical IMMUTABLE classification fields (`_same_classification`: regimes/divergence/fired_rule/window/flags/basis/boundary — forward_returns excluded as they are re-derived) → replace the prior row (dedupe); if classification differs → raise immutable-history conflict; output `comparison_records` sorted by `as_of`. Tests: identical same-week rerun → total_weeks stays 1; divergent same-week → raise; output sorted. Verification 200/200 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 regime weekly orchestration (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / cadence workflow`): PURE orchestration composing the reviewed pieces with the data side INJECTED. `extend_ledger(existing, as_of, calendar, feature_provider)` encodes the fail-closed cadence workflow (`validate_ledger_for_append` → `plan_append` → `feature_provider(d)` per planned day [must be dated exactly d] → `merge_rows` → `build_ledger` → `validate_ledger`); the same function does bootstrap (empty→last 252) and weekly increment, is idempotent on rerun, materializes the calendar once (generator-safe), rejects a gappy existing ledger, and rejects an empty/all-future bootstrap calendar. `weekly_regime_step` composes extend + `build_comparison_record` + audited backfill/summarize/render (PIT-capped at the run date), with same-week rerun semantics (identical classification → dedupe-replace; divergent → immutable-history conflict raise) and `as_of`-sorted output → {ledger, comparison_record, comparison_records, evidence, panel_markdown}. 13 tests (bootstrap, increment, idempotent, provider-wrong-date/gappy/empty-cal/all-future raise, generator calendar, step-from-empty, step-accumulates-prior, identical-rerun dedupe, divergent-rerun raise, sorted output). **No Tushare fetch, no EGS wiring, no file write, no production** — those are slice 2b-impl ②b-2 (real provider + persistence + in-EGS side-output + bootstrap-runner CLI [bootstrap RUN = user 执行 + TUSHARE_TOKEN] + panel-into-weekly-report). Pre-flight self-sweep run before review. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_pipeline` 13/13 + full V14.3 200/200 + route-doc OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a future-as_of record can advance evidence counts (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex deeper `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-live evidence clock / as_of cap`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-FUTURE-ASOF-COUNTS`): the audited evidence path requires a canonical `as_of_now`, but it never rejects comparison records whose own `as_of` is later than that cap. Such a future record is treated like an anchor-missing row: all forward returns remain pending, but the row still advances `total_weeks` and `divergence_weeks`, and a future current record can be rendered in the panel. Independent probe with `as_of_now=20240112` and record `as_of=20991231` returned `{'total_weeks': 1, 'divergence_weeks': 1, 'backfill_complete_weeks': 0}` from `summarize_comparison_records`, and `render_regime_comparison_block` rendered the future `as_of`. Required repair: in the sanctioned summarize/render path, reject any current/history comparison record with `as_of > as_of_now` before counting or display; add tests for future history rejection and future current-record render rejection. `backfill_forward_returns` may stay a lower-level helper only if the evidence/render path remains stricter. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_audited_history` (backfill-audit + reject `as_of > as_of_now` future records + reject duplicate `as_of`), used by `summarize_comparison_records`; `render_regime_comparison_block` rejects a current record dated after `as_of_now` and audits history via `_audited_history`. Tests: future history record → summarize raises; future current → render raises. Verification 187/187 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a render history can exclude or contradict the displayed current record (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex deeper `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only panel evidence progress / current-history consistency`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-RENDER-HISTORY-CURRENT-MISMATCH`): `render_regime_comparison_block(record, csi1000, as_of_now, records=...)` audits and displays the current `record`, but evidence progress is summarized from arbitrary `records` without requiring that the displayed current week is present and payload-consistent in that history. Probe: current `record.as_of=20240107` with V14.3 `attack` divergence rendered as the current panel row, while `records=[20240102 shock/non-divergent]` produced evidence progress `1/12` and divergence `0`; the displayed divergent week was excluded from the counter. Required repair: either compute render evidence from `records + [current]` with de-duplication and same-`as_of` consistency checks, or require `records` to contain exactly one row matching the audited current record and raise on missing/mismatched current. Add tests for missing current in `records` and mismatched same-`as_of` payload. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `render_regime_comparison_block` now requires (when `records` is given) that the audited history contain exactly one row matching the audited current record's `as_of`, and that that row equals the audited current record — else raises (missing / payload-mismatch); the displayed evidence counts are derived from that same audited history. Tests: current absent from `records` → raise; same-`as_of` different payload → raise. Verification 187/187 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a audited evidence path `as_of_now` optional look-ahead (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-return evidence clock / no-look-ahead cap`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-AUDITED-PATH-ASOF-NOW-OPTIONAL`): `summarize_comparison_records(records, csi1000, as_of_now=None)` and `render_regime_comparison_block(record, csi1000, as_of_now=None, ...)` document `as_of_now` as REQUIRED, but the signature defaults it to `None` and tests exercise the default. Independent probe with an 11-day CSI1000 series and a 20240102 record: `summarize_comparison_records([record], idx)` returned `backfill_complete_weeks=1`, and `render_regime_comparison_block(record, idx)` rendered fully filled forward returns, even though no as-of cap was supplied. This is a look-ahead path for the evidence clock whenever the caller passes an index frame containing future rows. Required repair: for the sanctioned evidence/render path, make `as_of_now` truly mandatory and canonical. `summarize_comparison_records` and `render_regime_comparison_block` should raise when `as_of_now is None`; add tests proving omitted `as_of_now` raises, and proving an as-of cap at the anchor leaves all forward horizons pending even if the supplied CSI1000 frame contains future rows. `backfill_forward_returns` may remain a lower-level pure helper with optional `as_of_now` only if docs/tests clearly distinguish it from the sanctioned evidence path. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `summarize_comparison_records` and `render_regime_comparison_block` now take `as_of_now` with NO default and raise `ValueError` if it is None or non-canonical (omitting → TypeError), so the sanctioned evidence/render path is always PIT-capped + audited. `backfill_forward_returns` stays the lower-level helper (optional `as_of_now`), documented as such. Tests: omitted/None/non-canonical `as_of_now` raise; an as-of cap at the anchor leaves all forward horizons pending (backfill_complete_weeks=0) even with a future-containing CSI1000 frame. Verification 183/183 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a route-doc API drift after audited-path repair (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P3`; scope `docs/README.md`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-return evidence clock / cross-LLM wiring contract`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-ROUTE-DOC-API-DRIFT`): durable docs still describe the pre-repair API/contract. `docs/README.md` still says `render_regime_comparison_block(record, records)` and "`summarize_comparison_records` = pure counts"; `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md` still says `summarize`/`render` validate records but does not state they require `csi1000 + as_of_now` and internally audit via backfill. This can mislead slice ②b wiring back to the old call shape. Required repair: update README and cadence design to the actual audited API after the `as_of_now` fix, including the mandatory cap and no-look-ahead evidence path. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** README ②a route row and cadence design §5 ②a rewritten to the audited API — `summarize_comparison_records(records, csi1000, as_of_now)` / `render_regime_comparison_block(record, csi1000, as_of_now, records=None)` require csi1000 + mandatory canonical as_of_now and internally audit via backfill (PIT-capped, no look-ahead); `backfill_forward_returns` noted as the lower-level helper. Active register summary test count updated to 19. Docs-only.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a render/summary backfill-audit bypass (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-return evidence clock / sanctioned render path`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-RENDER-SUMMARY-BACKFILL-AUDIT-BYPASS`): The repaired `backfill_forward_returns` audits existing non-null horizons correctly, but `summarize_comparison_records` / `render_regime_comparison_block` only validate comparison-record shape and duplicate `as_of`; they cannot verify forward-return numerical truth against CSI1000. Independent probe: a shape-valid record with `h1=999.0`, `h3=3.0`, `h5=5.0`, `h10=10.0`, `forward_returns_pending=[]`, and `backfill_complete=True` was accepted by `summarize_comparison_records` and counted as `backfill_complete_weeks=1` when passed directly without `backfill_forward_returns`. Required repair: make the sanctioned panel/evidence path impossible or clearly invalid without the backfill audit step. Acceptable shapes include: render/summarize wrapper takes `csi1000` + `as_of_now` and internally calls `backfill_forward_returns` before counting; or add explicit audited provenance/metadata and require it for complete evidence; or partition raw summary from evidence summary and document/test that slice ②b must run `backfill_forward_returns(full_history, csi1000, as_of_now)` immediately before count/render. Add a test proving a fabricated prefilled complete record cannot advance the sanctioned evidence-render path. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** chose option (a) — `summarize_comparison_records(records, csi1000, as_of_now)` and `render_regime_comparison_block(record, csi1000, as_of_now, records=None)` now REQUIRE `csi1000` + `as_of_now` and run `backfill_forward_returns` internally (which audits every existing forward-return value vs the deterministic CSI1000 return + validates each record, raising on fabrication/look-ahead) before counting/rendering. There is no unaudited evidence path. Tests: `summarize`/`render` with a fabricated `h1=999.0` record raise; existing duplicate/invalid still raise. Verification 181/181 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① non-finite numeric input gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / finite numeric input contract`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-NONFINITE-NUMERIC-INPUTS`): The implementation documents usable prices/limits as finite positive, but `_usable_price_codes` and `_usable_limit_codes` only check `notna` and `>0`, so `+Inf` passes as usable. Independent probes showed as_of `daily.close=high=inf` emits `limit_up_count=1`, as_of `stk_limit.up_limit=down_limit=inf` emits `limit_down_count=1`, and as_of `csi300.close=inf` returns `csi300_ret_1d=inf` (then `daily_row_semantic_errors` rejects it, proving the producer can return semantic-invalid rows). Required repair: enforce finite numeric values after coercion for daily `close/high`, `stk_limit` `up_limit/down_limit`, and index `close`; invalid as_of daily/limit data remains fail-closed, while invalid/stale index data should become unavailable/null+flag instead of non-finite output. Invalid as_of daily/limit → fail closed; ±Inf index close → filtered → last row ≠ as_of → null + `csi*_unavailable`. Added a producer self-check: the returned row is run through `daily_row_semantic_errors` and a non-empty result raises (a semantic-invalid row can no longer escape). Tests: `+Inf` as_of price → raise, `+Inf` as_of limit → raise, `+Inf` index close → unavailable. Verification 161/161 OK.

Added `history_masked` to the classifier output for transparency. Classifier tests: slow_bleed, streak_collapse, and attack each suppressed under the flag → shock; defense (broad-index crash) still fires. Verification 161/161 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① as_of `stk_limit_missing` still fabricates limit-derived metrics (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / classifier consumption of missing limit data`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-ASOF-LIMIT-MISSING-METRIC-FABRICATION`): The repair flags fully missing as-of `stk_limit` with `stk_limit_missing`, but still emits limit-derived integer metrics as real zeros and can emit `promotion_rate=0.0` when the prior-day denominator is valid. Required repair: missing/partial/unusable as-of limit data must not feed limit-dependent raw rules as real observations. Acceptable fixes include fail-closing producer output for as-of limit-data absence, or updating the producer/classifier contract so `stk_limit_missing` / `stk_limit_partial` / unusable-limit flags null or mask all limit-derived operands (`limit_down_count`, `net_limit`, `max_limit_streak`, `promotion_rate`, `failed_limit_rate`) before classification. Add tests proving missing as-of limit data cannot produce `promotion_rate=0.0` and cannot fire `streak_collapse`, exhaustion, defense limit-down, or attack rules. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** chose **fail-closed** (the limit count fields are non-null integers that cannot honestly encode "unknown", so a fabricated 0 is itself the defect). `compute_regime_daily_features` now RAISES if any as_of traded stock lacks a usable limit (so no fabricated-zero row is ever produced → nothing reaches `classify_raw_regime`; the classifier needs no change). Prior-day window incompleteness degrades instead: `promotion_rate=None` + `stk_limit_history_incomplete` flag (never `0.0` from missing data). Tests: `test_missing_asof_limit_fails_closed` (raises), `test_partial_asof_limit_fails_closed` (raises), `test_partial_stk_limit_prior_day_nulls_promotion` (promotion null + history flag, not 0.0). The earlier partial→flag-and-keep-counts behavior was intentionally escalated to fail-closed to satisfy this finding. Verification 149/149 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① unusable `stk_limit` values are treated as valid coverage (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / usable limit-price coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-UNUSABLE-LIMIT-VALUES-SILENT`): `_uncovered(day)` only checks whether a `stk_limit` row exists for each `ts_code`, while `_limit_sets` marks `have_limit=True` as soon as the merge is non-empty. If rows exist but `up_limit/down_limit` are NaN, nonnumeric, or non-positive, the producer emits `limit_up_count=0`, `limit_down_count=0`, no `stk_limit_missing` / `stk_limit_partial` / limit-quality flag, schema OK, and no ledger semantic error. Probe with two current daily stocks and two unusable limit rows (`up_limit=NaN` / `up_limit=0`) reproduced exactly that. Required repair: define "covered" as having usable finite positive `up_limit` and `down_limit` values for the stock/date, not merely row presence; unusable values must be treated as missing/partial or fail closed. Add adversarial tests for NaN, nonnumeric, zero, and negative limit values. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_usable_limit_codes(limit_day)` = ts_codes with finite positive up_limit AND down_limit (NaN/nonnumeric→`_num`→NaN→excluded; zero/negative→excluded). Coverage (`_incomplete`) now requires usable values, not row presence; combined with the as_of fail-closed gate, an as_of with any unusable limit raises. Test `test_unusable_asof_limit_values_fail_closed` (NaN up, zero up, zero down, negative down all raise). Verification 149/149 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① partial `stk_limit` coverage silently treated as complete (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / limit breadth integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-PARTIAL-STK-LIMIT-SILENT`): `_limit_sets` inner-joins `daily_day` to `stk_limit` and returns `have_limit=True` as soon as any matched stock exists. `compute_regime_daily_features` only flags `stk_limit_missing` when the entire day has no usable limit rows. Therefore a partial `stk_limit` panel (for example 2 daily stocks but only 1 limit row) emits `limit_up_count=1` with no limit-data quality flag, passes schema, and has no ledger semantic error. This can undercount/overstate limit-up/down breadth, failed-limit rate, max streak, and promotion-rate denominator, then contaminate the 252d comparison ledger. Required repair: detect partial `stk_limit` coverage per day (at minimum as_of and previous day used for promotion, preferably all dates used for streak), either fail closed or emit explicit null/degraded metrics plus a `stk_limit_partial`/coverage flag; do not compute full-universe breadth from a subset silently. Add adversarial tests for partial as_of coverage and partial prior-day promotion coverage. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_uncovered(day)` (daily-traded stocks lacking a stk_limit row); `compute_regime_daily_features` now flags `stk_limit_partial` when ANY window day used by counts/streak/promotion is incomplete (as_of has some coverage; a fully-missing as_of stays `stk_limit_missing`), and **nulls the coverage-sensitive ratios** (`failed_limit_rate` on partial as_of; `promotion_rate` on partial as_of OR partial prior day). Integer counts stay (schema requires non-null) but are flagged as lower bounds. Tests: partial-as_of (flag + failed_limit_rate null) and partial-prior-day (promotion null + flag). Verification 148/148 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① stale index row reused when index lacks `as_of` (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / index date alignment`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-STALE-INDEX-ASOF`): `_index_ret_and_below` filters index rows to `<= as_of` and computes return / MA from the last available row without requiring that row's `trade_date == as_of`. A missing current-day CSI300/CSI1000 row is therefore treated as valid by silently reusing a prior day; probe showed `as_of=20240105` with index rows only through `20240104` returned finite CSI returns and did not flag `csi300_unavailable`. This can suppress or fabricate broad-index-crash and slow-bleed operands. Required repair: require latest index row exactly equals `as_of` before emitting `ret_1d` or `csi1000_below_ma20`; otherwise return null and flag unavailable/stale. Add adversarial tests for stale CSI300 and stale CSI1000 panels. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `_index_ret_and_below` now returns `(None, None)` unless the latest index row at/<= as_of has `trade_date == as_of`, so a stale/missing current-day index → null + the caller's `csi300_unavailable` / `csi1000_unavailable` flag (no stale ret or stale below_ma20 emitted). Test `test_stale_index_flagged_unavailable` (index through as_of−1 → both indices unavailable). Verification 148/148 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① producer can emit schema-invalid rows for invalid date / IV inputs (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / output contract`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-SCHEMA-INVALID-OUTPUT-GUARDS`): The producer documentation says the returned row is intended to pass `a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json` and ledger `daily_row_semantic_errors`, but the function does not validate canonical real `YYYYMMDD` before using lexicographic PIT filters and accepts any finite IV value. Probes showed `as_of="2024011"` with matching daily rows returns a schema-invalid row, and `iv_percentile_252d=150.0` returns `150.0` with no `iv_unavailable` flag even though the daily schema max is 100. Downstream ledger validation would catch some writes, but the producer itself currently violates its own contract and leaves a footgun for slice 2b-impl ② wiring. Required repair: reject non-canonical real `YYYYMMDD` `as_of` before filtering, and treat IV outside `[0,100]` as invalid (null+flag or fail closed, but not schema-invalid output). Add adversarial tests for malformed `as_of`, impossible dates, negative IV, and IV >100. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `compute_regime_daily_features` now raises on a non-canonical `as_of` (reuses the now-public single-source `engine.a_short_regime_ledger.is_canonical_date` — `_is_canonical_date` kept as internal alias), before any lexicographic filter; IV outside `[0,100]` (also NaN/Inf/None) → `None` + `iv_unavailable`. Tests: malformed/impossible `as_of` (`2024011`/`20240231`/`202401 1`) raise; IV `150`/`-5` → null+flag. Verification 148/148 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① regime daily-feature computation (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / limit caliber / PIT`): PURE producer `compute_regime_daily_features` computing one `a_short_market_regime_daily` row from in-memory panels, faithful to slice-1 governance calibers (per-stock stk_limit up/down → ±10/20/ST±5; failed_limit_rate = touched-up-closed-below / touched; max consecutive limit-up streak; promotion_rate prior-day-up→today-up / denom, denom<5→null+insufficient_sample; pct_above_ma20 20d-window-or-null; csi300/csi1000 ret_1d + csi1000_below_ma20; iv passthrough from IV feed with NaN/None→null+iv_unavailable). ST-caliber, zero/thin denom, short window, missing index/iv, PIT. **No data fetch, no EGS wiring, no file write, no production** — those are slice 2b-impl ② (in-EGS fetch+append + bootstrap runner [needs user 执行 + TUSHARE_TOKEN] + panel + forward backfill). A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence plan_append stale pre-gate docstring (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex final re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / API documentation / safe append wiring`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PLANAPPEND-DOCSTRING-STALE-PREGATE`): After the gate split, the correct pre-planning gate is `validate_ledger_for_append`, while the final/read gate is `validate_ledger`. The `plan_append` docstring still says the safe pre-planning reason is that sanctioned `validate_ledger` rejects gappy ledgers and that callers must validate before planning. That wording recreates the earlier append pre-gate contradiction for future implementers: `validate_ledger` enforces freshness and rejects the normal prior-run ledger before append. Required repair: update the `plan_append` docstring to name `validate_ledger_for_append` as the required pre-append historical gate and reserve `validate_ledger` for the merged final/read gate; add a docstring assertion only if local testing conventions make that worthwhile. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** rewrote the `plan_append` Note to require `validate_ledger_for_append` as the pre-append gate and to explicitly warn against using `validate_ledger` there (it adds freshness and would reject the normal prior-run ledger; reserved for the merged final/read gate). Docstring-only; behavior + tests unchanged (125/125 OK). No code-pattern for docstring assertions in this repo, so text fix per Codex's allowance.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence API hardening / schema-doc drift (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex two-round re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_daily_ledger.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / implementation-proofing / schema-vs-gate clarity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-API-HARDENING-DOC-DRIFT`): Fresh second-pass review found lower-severity but real footguns around the still-open P1 blockers. `validate_ledger()` consumes `trade_calendar` twice, so a valid generator calendar is falsely rejected as "dates not on calendar"; `plan_append(..., backfill_min=0)` returns the full eligible calendar and `backfill_min=-1` returns a sliced subset instead of rejecting invalid policy input; `plan_append(["20240101","20240101"], ...)` silently deduplicates corrupt existing dates; `merge_rows()` accepts semantic-invalid new rows such as inconsistent `net_limit` unless final validation is called; the design doc still says "ledger 已到/超 as_of -> 返回 []" while repaired code raises on `max(existing)>as_of`; and the ledger schema text overclaims that `merge_rows` enforces daily-row validity even though generic schema-only validation accepts a row with only `as_of`. These should be repaired or explicitly narrowed while fixing the P1s: materialize `trade_calendar` once, reject invalid `backfill_min`, reject duplicate `existing_dates` or require/route through the new append pre-gate, clarify `merge_rows` is merge-only and final/current validation is mandatory before write/read, and align design/schema wording with actual gate responsibilities. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** (1) `validate_ledger` now materializes `trade_calendar` once (`cal_entries` reused for both the canonical check and the `cal` set), so a generator calendar is no longer falsely rejected; (2) `plan_append` rejects `backfill_min <= 0`; (3) `plan_append` rejects duplicate `existing_dates` (no silent dedup); (4) `merge_rows` docstring clarified merge-only + gates mandatory before write/read; (5) design doc corrected: `==as_of`→`[]` (idempotent) vs `>as_of`→raise; (6) ledger schema description corrected to state `merge_rows` enforces only append-only immutability, daily-row validity is the gates'. Tests: generator calendar accepted (validate + plan_append), `backfill_min` 0/-1 rejected, duplicate existing rejected. Verification 125/125 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence canonical-date leniency gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / canonical YYYYMMDD / string-order PIT precondition`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-CANONICAL-DATE-LENIENCY-GAP`): The repaired `_is_canonical_date()` relies only on `datetime.strptime(s, "%Y%m%d")`, which is lenient: Python accepts non-canonical strings such as `2024011` and `202401 1` as 2024-01-01. Independent probes showed the sanctioned gate accepts empty-ledger validation with `as_of="2024011"` and `as_of="202401 1"`, accepts malformed calendar entries through `validate_ledger`, and `plan_append` accepts or can return these malformed dates. This reopens the date-semantics/PIT ordering risk because lexicographic comparisons are only sound after exactly-8-digit canonical dates are guaranteed. Required repair: require `isinstance(str)`, exactly 8 ASCII digits, successful parse, and `strftime("%Y%m%d") == s`; add adversarial tests for 7-digit and whitespace-containing `as_of` and calendar entries through both `validate_ledger` and `plan_append`. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `_is_canonical_date` now requires `isinstance(str)` + exactly 8 ASCII digits (`all(c in "0123456789")`) + `strptime(s).strftime("%Y%m%d") == s` round-trip, so `2024011` / `202401 1` / leading-or-trailing-space strings are rejected. Tests: lenient `as_of` (`2024011`/`202401 1`/` 20240101`/`20240101 `) and lenient calendar entry rejected through both `validate_ledger` and `plan_append`. Verification 125/125 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence append pre-gate contradiction (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / safe weekly append cadence / validation gate split`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-APPEND-PREGATE-CONTRADICTION`): The design says callers must validate a ledger before planning/writing because `plan_append()` does not repair internal gaps before `max(existing)`, but the only sanctioned `validate_ledger()` now enforces freshness and rejects the normal weekly pre-append state: a contiguous existing ledger ending at the previous run date. Probe: an existing ledger through `20240105` with current `as_of=20240110` fails `validate_ledger` as stale; `plan_append` then correctly plans the missing dates and the merged final ledger validates. Therefore either implementation skips validation before planning (reopening internal-gap corruption) or follows the doc and cannot perform normal appends. Required repair: split the gate semantics, e.g. an append-pre-gate/historical validator that enforces schema, row semantics, canonical dates, sorted/coverage/policy/boundary, no duplicates, calendar subset, contiguity within existing coverage, and no row > as_of without requiring freshness; plus a current/read/final gate that adds freshness and, for read use, likely non-empty. Add tests for stale-contiguous pre-append acceptance, gappy stale rejection, plan+merge+current validation pass, and stale read/current rejection. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** split the gate into `_validate_ledger_core(..., require_fresh)` exposed as `validate_ledger_for_append` (require_fresh=False: schema + row semantics + canonical dates + sorted/coverage/policy/boundary + no dups + calendar subset + contiguity within existing coverage + no row > as_of) and `validate_ledger` (require_fresh=True: adds freshness). `validate_ledger_envelope` stays context-free / non-sanctioned. Design §3 documents the fail-closed weekly workflow (for_append → plan_append → merge_rows → validate_ledger → write). Tests: stale-but-contiguous accepted by for_append + rejected by validate_ledger; gappy rejected by for_append; future row rejected; full pregate→plan→merge→current workflow passes. Verification 125/125 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence date semantics gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex comprehensive re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / date validity / PIT ordering`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-LEDGER-DATE-SEMANTICS-GAP`): The full gate still relies on string ordering and schema patterns rather than real calendar-date validation. Lexicographic ordering only preserves PIT semantics after every date is a canonical real `YYYYMMDD`. Required repair: validate `as_of`, row dates, and trade-calendar entries as real calendar dates in canonical `YYYYMMDD` form before sorting/comparison; reject malformed/impossible dates; add adversarial tests for impossible row dates, impossible calendar dates, malformed `as_of`, and a valid non-trading-day `as_of` if weekend/run-date semantics are intentionally allowed. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_is_canonical_date` (`datetime.strptime "%Y%m%d"`); `validate_ledger` / `plan_append` / `merge_rows` now reject non-canonical `as_of`, trade-calendar entries, and existing/new row dates before any lexicographic comparison; row dates also checked via `daily_row_semantic_errors` in the envelope. Tests: malformed `as_of` (`20240231`/`20240199`/`202401105`), non-canonical calendar entry, impossible row date. Verification 113/113 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence net-limit invariant gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex comprehensive re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / cross-field row integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-LEDGER-NET-LIMIT-INVARIANT-GAP`): The daily row contract accepts inconsistent `net_limit` values. A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence row-validation bypass (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex comprehensive re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / sanctioned gate bypass`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-LEDGER-ROW-VALIDATION-BYPASS`): The sanctioned `validate_ledger(..., validate_rows=False)` path can still accept schema-invalid rows. A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence merge existing-duplicate collapse (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex comprehensive re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / append-only immutability`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-MERGE-EXISTING-DUPLICATE-COLLAPSE`): `merge_rows()` converts `existing_rows` to a dict before duplicate detection, so duplicate existing dates are silently collapsed. A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence non-finite daily-feature gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / numeric validity / 252d percentile integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-LEDGER-NONFINITE-DAILY-FEATURES`): The freshness repair works, but `validate_ledger_envelope()` still relies on JSON Schema for row numeric validity. Python `jsonschema` accepts `float('nan')` as `number`; fields without min/max (`csi300_ret_1d`, `csi1000_ret_1d`) also accept `+Inf` and `-Inf`. Independent probe: `validate_ledger()` accepted rows with `promotion_rate=NaN`, `failed_limit_rate=NaN`, `iv_percentile_252d=NaN`, `pct_above_ma20=NaN`, and accepted `csi300_ret_1d` / `csi1000_ret_1d` as `NaN`, `+Inf`, and `-Inf`. These values are not valid daily observations and can poison `resolve_percentiles()` / `np.percentile()` or trigger nonsensical raw-regime comparisons. Required repair: explicitly reject non-finite values for every numeric daily-feature field in the ledger validator (integers and nullable floats), with adversarial tests for NaN/+Inf/-Inf across at least bounded rate/percentile fields and unbounded index-return fields. Keep null semantics unchanged where the schema allows null. Also update the `docs/README.md` route row to mention the repaired freshness gate after the numeric fix. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `daily_row_semantic_errors` rejects non-finite (NaN/+Inf/-Inf) values for the 6 nullable float fields (`promotion_rate`, `failed_limit_rate`, `iv_percentile_252d`, `csi300_ret_1d`, `csi1000_ret_1d`, `pct_above_ma20`), null preserved; integer count fields are already rejected as non-integer by jsonschema for NaN. Test `test_reject_non_finite_daily_feature` (6 fields × NaN/+Inf/-Inf). README route row updated to mention freshness + the new finite/date/net_limit/no-bypass/dup guards. Verification 113/113 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence stale coverage freshness gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / as_of freshness / evidence-clock date alignment`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-LEDGER-STALE-COVERAGE-GAP`): The PIT/as_of repair blocks future rows and missing-context validation, but the sanctioned `validate_ledger` still accepts a contiguous **stale** ledger whose `coverage.end` is earlier than the latest trading day `<= as_of`. Independent probe: with a trade calendar covering `20240101..20240112`, a ledger containing rows only through `20240105` passes `validate_ledger(ledger, as_of="20240110", trade_calendar=calendar)`. This can let the next comparison/read path classify stale history for a later run date, creating run-date / feature-date mismatch and poisoning the comparison evidence clock. Required repair: the full sanctioned gate must enforce freshness for write/read use — `coverage.end` / last row date must equal the latest trading calendar date `<= as_of` when rows are present, with explicit behavior for empty ledgers/bootstrap before first write. Add adversarial tests proving stale-but-contiguous ledgers are rejected, current-through-as_of ledgers pass, and comparison/read paths cannot silently use stale rows for a later `as_of`. Keep `validate_ledger_envelope` context-free but clearly non-sanctioned. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added a freshness check to the sanctioned `validate_ledger` — when rows are present, the last row date must equal the latest trading day `<= as_of` (computed from the calendar), else `ValueError`; an empty (pre-bootstrap) ledger passes and a reader must separately require non-empty (documented in the docstring). `validate_ledger_envelope` stays context-free / non-sanctioned. Added 3 adversarial tests (stale-but-contiguous rejected, current-through-as_of passes, empty pre-bootstrap passes). Codex's Optional (`plan_append` not repairing internal gaps before `max(existing)`) addressed by documentation: `plan_append` is fail-closed because `validate_ledger` rejects gappy ledgers before any read/write. Boundary unchanged (pure logic). Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_ledger` 31/31 (+3) + full V14.3 + route-doc = 105/105 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence ledger PIT validation gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_daily_ledger.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / as_of PIT integrity / 252d percentile evidence clock`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-LEDGER-PIT-ASOF-GAP`): The slice direction is correct, but the advertised PIT/as_of validation gate is not closed. The public contract says `validate_ledger` enforces sorted/contiguous/PIT rows and that `merge_rows` / `plan_append` are PIT-capped at `as_of`; however independent probes found: `merge_rows([row("20240108")], [], "20240105")` accepts an already-existing future row; `plan_append(["20240108"], "20240105", calendar)` returns `[]`, treating the future-contaminated ledger as current; `validate_ledger(build_ledger(rows_through_20240108), trade_calendar=calendar)` passes because `validate_ledger` has no `as_of`; and a gappy ledger passes `validate_ledger(ledger)` when `trade_calendar` is omitted. This can let a future row or non-contiguous ledger survive the sanctioned gate and later poison the 252d percentile window / comparison evidence clock. Required repair: make the full ledger validation API require the run `as_of` and trading calendar (or split schema-only validation from a mandatory full gate), reject any existing or new row dated `> as_of`, reject future-contaminated existing ledgers in `merge_rows` / `plan_append` or require a validated ledger before planning, and add adversarial tests for existing-future rows, `plan_append` with future existing dates, validate-with-as_of future rows, and gappy-ledger-without-calendar acceptance. Keep the slice boundary unchanged: no data fetch, no EGS wiring, no file write, no production. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** split `validate_ledger_envelope` (context-free schema/sorted/coverage/policy/boundary/per-row, explicitly NOT a substitute gate) from the sanctioned `validate_ledger(ledger, *, as_of, trade_calendar)` whose `as_of` + `trade_calendar` are now **mandatory** (omitting → TypeError) and which rejects any row dated `> as_of` (PIT) and any gap/non-subset vs the calendar (contiguity). `plan_append` now raises on a future-contaminated ledger (`max(existing) > as_of`) instead of returning `[]`; `merge_rows` now rejects existing rows dated `> as_of` as well as new ones. Added 5 adversarial tests (existing-future merge, future-contaminated plan_append, validate-with-as_of future row, mandatory-as_of/calendar TypeError, envelope-accepts-but-full-gate-rejects gap). Boundary unchanged. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_ledger` 28/28 (+5) + full V14.3 + route-doc = 102/102 OK.
