A-short V14.3 slice 2a non-finite forward-return gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`; PIT label `comparison-record numeric validity / evidence-clock artifact integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2A-NONFINITE-FORWARD-RETURNS`): The producer schema-validation repair blocks string values and malformed horizon objects, but Python `jsonschema` accepts `float('nan')`, `float('inf')`, and `float('-inf')` as JSON Schema `number`. Independent probe: `build_comparison_record(..., forward_returns={"h1": float("nan")})`, `{"h1": float("inf")}`, and `{"h1": float("-inf")}` all returned records successfully. These values are not valid finite return observations and can serialize to non-standard JSON (`NaN` / `Infinity`) or contaminate evidence-clock comparisons. Required repair: explicitly reject non-finite forward-return values in `validate_comparison_record()` / producer path (e.g. `math.isfinite` for non-null horizons), and add adversarial tests proving builder/validator reject NaN, +Inf, and -Inf. Keep the const-pinned CSI1000 raw-return basis and comparison-only boundary unchanged. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added a non-finite guard in `validate_comparison_record()` — each non-null `forward_returns.h*` must pass `math.isfinite(float(v))`, else `ValueError`; runs alongside the full-schema + cross-field checks, so `build_comparison_record` (which self-validates before returning) rejects NaN/+Inf/-Inf at the producer. Added 6 adversarial cases (builder + validator × NaN/+Inf/-Inf). Const-pinned CSI1000 raw-return basis + comparison-only boundary unchanged. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_classifier` 46/46 (+2) + governance + route-doc = 74/74 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2a producer schema-validation gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`; PIT label `comparison-record producer / evidence-clock artifact validity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2A-BUILDER-SCHEMA-INVALID-OUTPUT`): The repair correctly added `validate_comparison_record()` for cross-field invariants and const-pinned `forward_return_basis`, but `build_comparison_record()` only calls that bespoke validator before returning; it does not validate field-local schema constraints or forward-return value types. Independent probe: `build_comparison_record(..., forward_returns={"h1": "not-a-number"})` returns a record with `forward_returns.h1` as a string; the schema then rejects that returned record (`'not-a-number' is not of type 'number', 'null'`). This contradicts the repair claim that the producer self-validates before returning and can let future writer/backfill code persist schema-invalid evidence if it trusts the builder. Required repair: make the sanctioned producer reject schema-invalid forward return payloads before returning (either by calling schema validation in the builder/writer path or by adding equivalent exact horizon-key + number/null type checks to `validate_comparison_record`), and add adversarial tests proving non-number return values and missing/extra horizon keys are rejected at the producer/validator layer. Also clean the stale `forward excess` docstring in `build_comparison_record`; the pinned basis is CSI1000 raw forward return, not excess. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `validate_comparison_record()` is now the single sanctioned validity gate and runs **full JSON-Schema validation** (field-local types/enums/consts, required keys, exact horizon keys, number|null forward-return values) **in addition to** the cross-field invariants, reporting any schema error as `ValueError` (single-exception contract). `build_comparison_record()` calls it before returning, so a non-number/missing/extra-horizon payload is rejected at the producer layer instead of being returned. Stale `forward excess` docstring corrected to CSI1000 raw forward return. Adversarial tests added: builder rejects non-number `h1`; validator rejects non-number value, missing horizon key, and extra horizon key. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_classifier` 44/44 (+4) + governance + route-doc = 72/72 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2a comparison-record evidence-contract gaps (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime evidence clock / forward-return backfill / switch-candidate integrity`; Required IDs `R-V143-SLICE2A-COMPARISON-INVARIANTS`, `R-V143-SLICE2A-FORWARD-RETURN-BASIS-UNPINNED`): Slice 2a's raw classifier logic is directionally sound, but the weekly comparison record contract is not commit-safe as evidence-clock infrastructure. (`R-V143-SLICE2A-COMPARISON-INVARIANTS`) `build_comparison_record()` currently emits self-consistent `divergence`, `forward_returns_pending`, and `backfill_complete`, but `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json` accepts contradictory artifacts: equal `v14_2_regime`/`v14_3_raw_regime` with `divergence=true`; non-null `h1` while `forward_returns_pending` still contains `h1`; all-null returns with `backfill_complete=true`; all-null returns with an empty pending list; and arbitrary `v14_2_regime` strings such as `nonsense_status`. That can corrupt the 12-week evidence clock or later switch review even if the initial builder is correct. Required repair: constrain what JSON Schema can constrain (at minimum `v14_2_regime` enum matching production statuses, boundary already const) and add producer/backfill-side consistency validation with adversarial tests for divergence, pending/null symmetry, backfill_complete, and fired-rule/regime consistency where schema cannot compare fields. (`R-V143-SLICE2A-FORWARD-RETURN-BASIS-UNPINNED`) the schema calls `h1/h3/h5/h10` "forward excess return" but does not pin benchmark, unit, price basis, universe/portfolio scope, or cost/slippage basis. Future backfill can mix raw index returns, candidate returns, CSI300/CSI1000 excess, percentage points, or decimals while still validating. Required repair: add an explicit `forward_return_basis` contract (or const-pinned governance reference) defining unit and measurement basis before any record is used for switch-candidate evidence; tests must reject missing/mismatched basis. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** (`R-V143-SLICE2A-COMPARISON-INVARIANTS`) added `validate_comparison_record()` enforcing the cross-field invariants JSON Schema cannot — `divergence==(v14_2≠v14_3_raw)`, `forward_returns_pending` equals exactly the null horizons, `backfill_complete==(no nulls)`, `v14_3_fired_rule` is a rule of `v14_3_raw_regime` (single-source `FIRED_RULES_BY_REGIME`); `build_comparison_record` now self-validates before returning and rejects an out-of-enum `v14_2_regime`. Schema tightened: `v14_2_regime` enum `{attack,shock,defense,contraction,unknown}` (mirrors production `market_regime.status`), `v14_3_fired_rule` enum, `forward_returns_pending` uniqueItems. Adversarial tests added for all five contradiction probes (equal-regime+divergence-true, non-null-in-pending, all-null+backfill-true, all-null+empty-pending, nonsense v14_2_regime) + fired-rule/regime mismatch, validated at both validator and schema layers. (`R-V143-SLICE2A-FORWARD-RETURN-BASIS-UNPINNED`) added const-pinned `forward_return_basis` (required field, all subfields `const` in schema + `FORWARD_RETURN_BASIS` in code): CSI1000 `000852.SH`, forward close-to-close simple return, horizons 1/3/5/10 trading days, unit=percent, index_close_unadjusted, gross, market-level regime indicator; validator + schema reject missing/mismatched basis (decimal-unit and missing-basis probes tested). Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_classifier` 40/40 (+11 invariant/basis tests) + governance + route-doc = 68/68 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2a raw-classifier + slice 2b cadence design decision (status `slice2a resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; **slice2b cadence/ledger decision remains `open` by design**; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md` (§6 split), `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime raw classifier / weekly comparison record / 252d-percentile cadence feasibility`): Slice 2a delivers the PURE per-day raw classifier (`classify_raw_regime`) + weekly comparison record (`build_comparison_record`) + schema + 29 tests — comparison-only, no data fetch, no EGS wiring, no production, no state machine (slice 3). Faithful to slice-1 governance const thresholds (percentile resolvers parity-tested against the const strings; consecutive-day operands break on null; attack requires all 6 operands, any null blocks it; insufficient-252d-window and missing-CSI1000 flagged). **Open design decision carried to slice 2b (NOT resolved here):** the 252d rolling-percentile thresholds need a daily-feature history, but the EGS run is weekly and produces one row per run — so breadth percentiles cannot be computed in-run from a single day. Proposed resolution: a persisted **incremental daily-feature ledger** in the a_short lane (one-time 252-trading-day backfill, then ~5 new days appended per weekly run; percentiles read from the ledger), rather than recomputing 252×(daily+stk_limit) calls every run. This ledger is a new managed state file and must be specified + reviewed before slice 2b wires the in-EGS producer + panel comparison-only block. Also noted: production `market_context.market_regime.status` is currently `unknown` (EGS does not compute V14.2 M1), so the comparison record's `v14_2_regime` will often be `unknown` (contract already allows it; divergence stats stay meaningful). No production / Phase 5 / veto / overlay / M6.7 behavior changed.

A-short V14.3 residual missing-index flag schema gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily feature contract / missing index data honesty`; Required ID `R-V143-REGIME-MISSING-INDEX-FLAG-NOT-ENFORCED`): The repair for `R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP` correctly added `csi1000_below_ma20` as the explicit slow-bleed operand and documents that missing index data must be represented as `csi1000_below_ma20=null` plus `data_quality_flags` containing `csi1000_unavailable`. However this is still only prose: a row with `csi1000_below_ma20=null` and empty `data_quality_flags` validates against `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`. That leaves Slice 2 free to emit ambiguous missing-index rows while still passing the advertised schema-first contract. Required repair: add a schema-level conditional (or an equivalent adversarial test plus producer-facing contract if JSON Schema support is intentionally limited) so `csi1000_below_ma20: null` requires `data_quality_flags` to contain `csi1000_unavailable`; add a negative test proving null-without-flag is rejected and a positive test proving null-with-flag is accepted. Keep the comparison-only / no-production boundary unchanged. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added a top-level `if/then` to `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json` — when `csi1000_below_ma20` is `null`, `data_quality_flags` is required and must `contain` `csi1000_unavailable`; null-without-flag now fails validation. Tests added: `test_null_csi1000_requires_unavailable_flag` (negative), `test_null_csi1000_with_flag_validates` (positive), `test_nonnull_csi1000_does_not_require_flag` (conditional does not over-fire). Design §2 notes the schema-enforced constraint. Boundary unchanged (comparison-only / non-production). Verification: `tests.test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance` 28/28 + route-doc guard 14/14 OK.

A-short V14.3 regime classifier design/schema blockers (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`, `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.py`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only market-regime classifier / schema-first governance / future evidence clock`; Required IDs `R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP`, `R-V143-REGIME-GOVERNANCE-CONST-PIN-GAP`): Codex review of the schema-first V14.3 proposal found the comparison-only boundary itself is directionally sound, but the slice is not commit-safe. (`R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP`) The design's contraction slow-bleed rule requires `pct_above_ma20<30%` for 5 days **and** `csi1000_close < csi1000_ma20`, but the daily feature contract only carries `csi1000_ret_1d` and `pct_above_ma20`; no `csi1000_close`, `csi1000_ma20`, or boolean `csi1000_below_ma20` is required. Slice 2 therefore cannot implement the documented rule from the schema without adding an unstated data dependency or silently dropping half the slow-bleed condition. Required repair: add the CSI1000 MA20 state to the daily schema/design/governance/test fixture (or remove/rewrite the rule so every operand is represented), and pin null/quality-flag behavior for missing index data. (`R-V143-REGIME-GOVERNANCE-CONST-PIN-GAP`) The governance schema claims to pin metric definitions, thresholds, action matrix, and switch gate, but those areas are mostly unconstrained `object`s: a mutation changing `attack_all_of_confirm_3d.promotion_rate_ge` to a broken string and removing `switch_candidate_gate.backtest_years_min` still validates against the schema. Current tests only catch a subset (boundary and the low-risk ceiling string), so threshold/action/gate drift can pass the advertised schema-first contract. Required repair: make the governance schema or tests actually pin the frozen keys and key values required by the design, including threshold formulas, documented-only action matrix status, `backtest_years_min=2`, `divergence_samples_min=8`, no-auto-switch, and the review/no-fluke/no-missed-opportunity gate booleans. Do not commit the V14.3 slice until repaired and re-reviewed. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree):** (`R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP`) added required `csi1000_below_ma20` (boolean|null) to the daily schema; design §2/§3 + governance slow_bleed now reference it (operand represented); null + `csi1000_unavailable` flag pinned for missing index data; test `test_daily_requires_csi1000_below_ma20`. (`R-V143-REGIME-GOVERNANCE-CONST-PIN-GAP`) rewrote the governance schema to **const-pin** every frozen value (defense/contraction/attack threshold formulas+anchors incl. the ceiling `min(50,max(P25_252,10))`, shock residual, state-machine days, action-matrix `_status` documented-only, switch gate `forward_live_weeks_min=12`/`backtest_years_min=2`/`divergence_samples_min=8`/`auto_switch_allowed=false`+review booleans, comparison-only boundary) with `additionalProperties:false`; adversarial tests prove a broken `promotion_rate_ge`, a removed `backtest_years_min`, a flipped `auto_switch_allowed`, and a flipped `production_switch_authorized` are all rejected. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance` 25/25 (incl. mutation-rejection) + route-doc OK; governance validates against the tightened schema; all JSON valid.

[HISTORICAL — its option-(a) **pit-only** decision was SUPERSEDED by (b) on 2026-06-16: live `today`-mode now ALSO emits overlay with `concept_membership='forward'` (honest live-decision-day members, no look-ahead) so the overlay comparison accumulates on live weekly; see the comparison-only index track ② above. The governance boundary (egs_main_runtime_changed=true / production_behavior_changed=false) and the in-EGS wiring described below remain accurate.] A-short Slice A overlay in-EGS wiring governance / mode-boundary drift (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS, committed in this overlay-wiring commit; severity `P1`; scope `A-EGS/egs_main.py`, `runners/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py`, `presets/a_short_theme_overlay_governance_20260610.json`, `docs/README.md`, and the overlay tests; PIT label `comparison artifact governance / L3 source boundary / cross-LLM route state`; Required IDs `R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-GOVERNANCE-RUNTIME-DRIFT`, `R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-L3-MODE-BOUNDARY-DRIFT`): Codex review on 2026-06-11 found the functional overlay/panel tests pass, but the current slice is not commit-safe because its runtime boundary and governance state contradict the actual wiring. The implementation now adds an `egs_main.py` side-output block that writes `overlay.json` from in-memory EGS data, but the governance artifact still states `scope.egs_main_runtime_changed=false`, the runner docstring still describes the overlay runner as a read-only consumer that does not modify `egs_main.py`, and `docs/README.md` says both "does NOT modify egs_main.py" and "Data-loading WIRED inside the EGS run" in the same current route row. Separately, the current comments/docs say only `--l3-mode pit` runs emit overlay, but the code does not check `CONF["l3_mode"]`; default `today` mode can write a same-day L3 snapshot in `score_l3()` and the overlay block then calls `_load_l3_snapshot(TODAY)`, so a default today-mode EGS run can also emit `overlay.json` while still recording `pit_source={"concept_membership":"pit"}`. Required repair: make the boundary explicit and machine-tested. Either (a) if overlay must be pit-only, add an explicit `CONF["l3_mode"] == "pit"` gate before building overlay and add a regression test proving today/neutralize skip; or (b) if today-mode overlay is allowed, update the route docs/governance/schema or emitted provenance so the artifact honestly records the source mode/snapshot date and no longer claims pit-only behavior. In both cases update `presets/a_short_theme_overlay_governance_20260610.json`, `docs/README.md`, and `runners/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py` so they no longer claim `egs_main` was untouched / runtime unchanged, while preserving the important boundary that production `final_score` / `tier` / admission are unchanged. A-short comparison-only tracks — standing promote/retire reminder index (2026-06-16; status `open`; severity `P2` standing forward-item, NOT a current blocker; scope this index + the three tracks' design/governance/artifacts; PIT label `comparison-only experiments / cross-LLM promote-or-retire reminder`; origin: user-directed 2026-06-16 — "未来在任何 LLM 运行、达到条件时都要被提醒"): the project runs three comparison-only / sidecar tracks that OBSERVE but never touch production. Each needs a durable cross-LLM reminder to **PROMOTE** (if it proves out) or **RETIRE** (if it never does), because the active auto-flag is unbuilt and the passive panels rely on a human noticing. **`执行` must read this index every session; when any track below reaches its review window, surface the promote-OR-retire decision to the user — do NOT silently keep accumulating/maintaining it forever.** Closure: each track closes when promoted (reviewed governance change), retired (user decision), or when its own auto-flag/scoreboard ships so code (not this index) enforces the reminder. Boundary: index/tracking only — no production switch, no scoring change, no data fetch from this entry.
  - **(1) V14.3 market-regime comparison** — artifact `research/results/a_short/regime_daily_ledger.json` + weekly comparison panel; **clock IS ticking** (`weekly_screening.ps1` Stage 5 increments every live run; 252-row bootstrap done 2026-06-15). PROMOTE trigger (classifier design `a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md` §6-7 + governance const `forward_live_weeks_min=12` / `divergence_samples_min=8` / `backtest_years_min=2` / `auto_switch_allowed=false`): forward-live ≥12 weeks (or backtest ≥2yr) AND divergence samples ≥8 AND net improvement (earlier defense/contraction, less drawdown, not materially missing attack) AND reviewed non-fluke/non-contaminated → emit the FIXED prompt **"V14.3 regime 可能优于 V14.2,是否进入生产切换审查?"** (only-prompt-never-auto-switch; user confirms → a separate production-switch slice). RETIRE trigger (**the gap, added per user 2026-06-16**): at the ≥12-week review, if divergence carries no value / V14.3 is not better → prompt the user to RETIRE (stop incrementing/maintaining the ledger) instead of accumulating forever. Active auto-reminder = Slice 3 (deferred, not built); until it ships, THIS index is the reminder. (CURRENT §1 currently says the regime line has "无待办" — that understates this standing review obligation.) **v14_2-sourcing deferred-wiring decision (user 2026-06-15; 固化 from memory `project_regime_v14_2_sourcing_deferred` to register here 2026-06-16):** `weekly_screening.ps1` Stage 5 does NOT pass `--v14_2-regime` → it defaults `unknown`, so the comparison is V14.3-vs-`unknown` (a no-op baseline, because production `market_regime` is itself always `unknown` today). Decide whether to wire REAL production V14.2 M1 regime into the comparison (source `analysis_input.market_context.market_regime.status` → runner `--v14_2-regime`) at EITHER trigger: (i) at this regime ≥12-week review, OR (ii) when production `market_regime` first emits a non-`unknown` value. Until a trigger hits, do NOT wire (it is genuinely no-op now). This was memory-only and is now durable here so any LLM is reminded.
  - **(2) Theme/overlay heat comparison (Slice A)** — artifact `result/a_short/<as_of>/overlay.json` (`overlay_path`; schema `a_short_theme_overlay_comparison`, `track=comparison_non_production`). **STATUS (2026-06-16): WIRED in `A-EGS/egs_main.py` after `score_l5` (~L3316-3340, A 方案) + (b) DONE — now accumulates on LIVE weekly.** Builds via `build_overlay_summary_from_panels` from in-EGS-run panels (top50 pool + `all_daily` + L3 concept snapshot + `sw_map`) → `write_overlay_summary` to the run bucket; non-fatal/旁路. **(b) change (user-approved 2026-06-16):** `overlay_emit_allowed` now allows BOTH `pit` (concept_membership='pit', 回放) AND live `today` (concept_membership='forward' — 决策当日 live 成员, no look-ahead) — so the LIVE weekly cadence (`l3-mode=today`) NOW produces `overlay.json` and the overlay comparison accumulates forward (the ≥12wk forward clock now ticks on live runs). `neutralize`/none still skip (no concepts, never fabricated). The egs emit block labels `concept_membership` by mode (`pit`/`forward`); `forward` is a valid schema enum + passes the consistency validator. PROMOTE trigger (design `a_short_theme_overlay_phase5_design_spec_20260610.md` §120-122): ≥12 forward obs + stable outperformance vs the 20/30/50 baseline (monthly clustered t / drawdown / win-rate / bad-name / false-negative, both CSI1000 & CSI300) → overlay enters production ranking; full-size still needs the separate 12-month live ship gate. RETIRE: no stable outperformance → stays research, never promote (design §122); or user drops it. **Reminder for `执行` — now ACTIVE / data-driven (2026-06-17, `R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-EVAL-READINESS-REMINDER`):** `runners/a_short_overlay_eval.py` + `weekly_screening.ps1` Stage 6 count forward overlay obs every live run and print a LOUD promote-or-retire banner once `n_forward_obs ≥ min_forward_observations` (governance 12); the eval summary `research/results/a_short/overlay_eval_summary.json` carries `promotion_review_due` + `decision_status`. So ANY AI running the weekly (not just an LLM that remembers this index) is reminded. **At the review point the §6 outperformance metrics still must be computed** — that is the deferred follow-up `R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-EVAL-METRICS-FOLLOWUP` (overlay-vs-baseline monthly clustered-t / drawdown / win-rate / bad-name / false-negative on CSI1000∧CSI300), which itself needs the governance `stable_win_margin` + top-K / holding-horizon FROZEN first (currently `stable_win_margin_pending` → no promotion authorized yet); the harness reports `decision_status=review_due_margin_pending` until they are frozen. (Guard test ADDED 2026-06-17: the emit core was extracted to `emit_overlay` and `EmitOverlayEgsBlockTests` cover today→forward / pit→pit / neutralize·no-snapshot→skip, so a silent break/mislabel of the emit block now fails a test.)
  - **(3) EGS industry/weight comparison (legacy / balanced / aggressive / 双提)** — artifact `<EGS bundle>/<as_of>/egs_weight_comparison.json`; **clock IS ticking** (produced each EGS run; `balanced` is LIVE production, `legacy` is the rollback/regression anchor). **Already tracked in full by the open forward-item immediately below** ("A-short production EGS 行业/赛道 weight promotion — design forward-item"): the 双提 variant promotes only if a rolling ≥12-week window shows its Tier1 forward 1–3wk return beats balanced by a pre-set noise-passing margin AND overheat/chasing share + drawdown are not worse → via reviewed governance; else logged forever, never auto-promoted; anti-forget = log all variants + auto-flag + keep open. Cross-ref that item for detail. RETIRE side (added per user 2026-06-16): make the same ≥12-week review two-sided — if a variant clearly underperforms over the window, prompt the user to stop tracking it rather than log forever.

A-short production EGS 行业/赛道 weight promotion — design forward-item (status `open`; severity `P2` roadmap forward-item, NOT a current blocker; scope FUTURE reviewed change to `A-EGS/egs_main.py` egs_base scoring + `presets/` governance + `schemas/analysis_input.schema.json` + EGS CSV output + parity/adversarial tests + Slice A overlay industry_heat concept adaptation (production-pinned, not literal reuse); PIT label `production selection scoring / SW L2 industry beta`; origin: user-directed 2026-06-11, Codex-refined): user (approver) directed raising 行业/赛道 importance in the PRODUCTION selection scoring (not only the analysis/overlay layer), rationale = sector beta (hot 赛道 lifts the whole SW L2 industry). **Locked v1 production default formula** (IMPLEMENTED + LIVE in the implementation slice above; this forward-item now tracks only the still-open forward-evaluation parts): `egs_base = esp 0.20 + cat 0.25 + l4 0.40 + industry_heat 0.15` (was `esp 0.20 + cat 0.30 + l4 0.50` at `egs_main.py:2743`); new `industry_heat_score` = SW L2 industry beta, weight taken from cat(-0.05)+l4(-0.10), **esp 0.20 kept (fundamentals anchor)**. Constraints: industry heat ADDS to ranking only — never rescues hard_veto/停牌/涨停锁/ST/减持/闪崩; keep `chasing_high·overheat → Tier2` (egs_main:2778-2781); industry_heat is a production-pinned per-SW-L2 median-momentum cross-industry percentile that ADAPTS the overlay percentile concept (NOT a literal reuse; no L1 fallback v1); avoid double-counting l4's existing industry kickers (`ind_mom_cnt≥3 +5`, industry-not-falling +10 at egs_main:2601/2614) — remove or quantify overlap in parity tests; the drafted slice MUST emit a "new vs current" selection diff (Tier1 membership + overheat-name share) so activation is not blind. **Deferred condition-triggered variant — 概念+行业双提 (esp.15/cat.35/l4.35/ind.15): NOT scheduled, do NOT rely on memory.** It is promoted ONLY if, over a rolling ≥12-week window, the comparison-track shows (a) its would-be Tier1 forward 1–3wk return beats the balanced default by a pre-set noise-passing margin AND (b) its selected-set overheat/chasing share + realized drawdown are not materially worse, then (c) via a reviewed governance change; otherwise it stays logged forever, never auto-promoted. **Anti-forget requirement on the future slice:** the comparison-track must (1) log all 3 variants (balanced default / aggressive / 双提) weekly Tier1 selection + forward return, (2) auto-raise a "variant = promotion candidate" flag when the trigger fires, (3) keep this forward-item open until the slice bakes the scoreboard + flag in with tests. Closes only when the production weight change + variant scoreboard + auto-flag ship reviewed, or the user explicitly drops it. Does NOT block batch ② or any current work.

A-short weekly pipeline batch ② Required repair closeout (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Codex re-`审查` PASS; severity `P0/P1 closeout`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `tests/test_a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/a_short_weekly_pipeline_design_20260610.md`, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`; PIT label `weekly report / batch ② official-output integrity`; Required IDs `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-IV-FEED-PIT-CROSS-ASOF`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-FETCH-FAIL-OPEN`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-EGS-HARD-RISK-MAPPING-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-OFFICIAL-OUTPUT-PATH-BOUNDARY`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-OVERLAY-CONSUMER-VALIDATION-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-ANALYSIS-INPUT-CONSUMER-VALIDATION-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-OFFICIAL-OUTPUT-ROOT-MISMATCH`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-WRITE-ASOF-CALENDAR-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-SERIES-PIT-FRESHNESS-GAP`): Codex final re-review on 2026-06-10 verified all nine Required findings in the A-short batch ② weekly pipeline slice are repaired in the working tree. The final price-series repair validates provider `trade_date` values as real calendar dates, rejects future rows, requires latest fetched bar to equal `--as-of`, and aborts without writing on violation; independent probes confirmed future/stale/non-calendar price rows and fake-`tushare` future rows no longer write. The prior repairs also hold: IV feed cross_as_of is rejected, required price coverage fail-open is closed, real EGS hard-risk fields map into hard veto behavior, production-shaped `result/a_short/...` output paths are refused under the narrowed caller-controlled output contract, overlay artifacts are schema/consistency/as_of validated, `analysis_input` is validated through the repo contract with `trade_date == --as-of`, and empty weekly reports with invalid calendar `as_of` are rejected. Verification: targeted A-short batch suite 182/182 OK, route-doc guard included; `py_compile` OK; `git diff --check` OK before review-log edits; full `unittest discover -v` still reports the known six import-time `A-EGS/egs_main.py` environment errors outside this slice. This closeout supersedes the detailed batch ② `in_progress` entries below and authorizes committing this reviewed slice only; it does not authorize live Tushare execution, provider fetch expansion, production `result/a_short` writes, ship-gate evidence, real-money sizing, DataHub work, or broker/order automation.

A-short weekly pipeline price-series PIT / freshness validation gap (status `superseded` by the batch ② Required-repair closeout above (committed `89a1318`, Codex PASS); severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `weekly report / required stock price coverage and PIT freshness`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-SERIES-PIT-FRESHNESS-GAP`): Codex re-review on 2026-06-10 found `_fetch_price_series()` sorts provider rows and returns only `{high, low, close}`; it never validates provider `trade_date` as a real calendar date, never rejects rows after `end` / weekly `as_of`, and never requires the latest returned bar to match the run `as_of`. Independent probes showed `_fetch_price_series(..., end=20260609)` accepts a future `trade_date=20260630` row and includes its `high=99.0 / low=1.0 / close=50.0`; it also accepts a 30-row series whose latest dates are all in `202601xx`. A fake `tushare` module through `main(... --confirm-fetch-authorized)` wrote `weekly.json` in both the future-row and stale-row cases. Required repair: validate every fetched `trade_date` as real `YYYYMMDD`, reject provider rows with `trade_date > end`, require latest fetched bar to equal `analysis_input.trade_date / --as-of` for included candidates (or document and test an explicit reviewed tolerance), abort without writing on violation, and add adversarial tests for future rows and stale latest bars. Blocks committing / executing batch ②. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-10, working tree)**: `_fetch_price_series()` now validates every `trade_date` as a real calendar date, rejects any `trade_date > end` (future bar), and requires the latest bar to equal `end` (== `--as-of`; chose exact-match over a tolerance), aborting via `SystemExit` without writing on any violation; still returns `{high,low,close}` engine shape. Tests: `test_future_bar_aborts`, `test_stale_latest_bar_aborts`, `test_noncalendar_trade_date_aborts`, `test_main_future_price_row_writes_no_file` (fake `tushare` through `main(--confirm-fetch-authorized)` → SystemExit, no file), and `test_uses_stock_asset_E_and_returns_bars` (latest==end happy path). Verification: A-short batch suite 170/170 OK (weekly file 41 tests); route-doc guard 14/14 incl.; `py_compile` OK.

A-short IV feed probe execution Tushare init side-effect (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Codex re-`审查` PASS; severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_iv_feed_probe_execution.py`; PIT label `provider execution wiring / token handling and endpoint pin`; Required ID `R-AIV-PROBE-EXEC-TUSHARE-INIT-SIDE-EFFECT`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found the new execution wiring was not execution-safe because `main()` called `ts.set_token(token)` before `ts.pro_api()`, causing a sandbox `PermissionError` on `C:\\Users\\cnhea\\tk.csv`, and because the new runner did not pin the known-problem Tushare 1.4.29 default endpoint. Repair: `main()` now initializes through `init_tushare_pro(token)`, which does not call `set_token`, hard-pins `ts_module.pro.client.DataApi._DataApi__http_url`, and then calls `ts.pro_api(token)`. Pin failure is a hard `RuntimeError` for missing `pro.client.DataApi` or missing `_DataApi__http_url`, before `pro_api(token)` can run. Verification: tests cover no `set_token`, happy-path pinning, missing-DataApi hard error, and missing-attr hard error; independent probe confirmed installed Tushare 1.4.29 pins from `http://api.waditu.com/dataapi` to `https://api.tushare.pro/dataapi`, while degraded fake modules hard-fail. This resolution authorizes Claude `提交` of the execution-wiring slice only; the real provider probe still requires a separate user-authorized `执行`.

A-short IV feed probe latest-usable summary consistency gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Claude `审查` PASS; severity `P1`; scope `schemas/a_short_iv_feed_probe_summary.schema.json`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `docs/a_short_iv_feed_design_20260610.md`; PIT label `IV-feed probe official summary contract / latest usable valuation date`; Required ID `R-AIV-PROBE-SUMMARY-LATEST-USABLE-CONSISTENCY`): Codex re-review on 2026-06-10 found the runner-side as_of and latest-usable-date repair fixed the prior P0 false positives, but the official summary contract still accepts contradictory `computable=true` summaries. Independent mutation probes passed both `jsonschema.validate(...)` and `validate_probe_summary_consistency(...)` with `computable=true` while `assessment.latest_usable_date` was `null`, `notadate`, invalid calendar `20260631`, or after top-level `as_of`; while `spot_ref` was `null` or `0`; and while `n_strikes_with_valid_quotes=0`. These fields are part of the evidence contract for PIT-safe latest usable valuation date and ATM bracketing, so accepting those contradictions can let a malformed execution summary claim IV computability without a valid valuation date or usable ATM spot / strike basis. Required repair: for `computable=true`, schema and/or producer consistency must reject null / non-date / invalid-calendar / future `latest_usable_date`, null or non-positive `spot_ref`, and zero quoted strike count; add adversarial tests for those mutations. Repair (Codex `修复` 2026-06-10, working tree): `validate_probe_summary_consistency()` now rejects `computable=true` with null / non-date / invalid-calendar / future `latest_usable_date`, null or non-positive `spot_ref`, and zero `n_strikes_with_valid_quotes`; schema now rejects computable summaries with null / non-8-digit `latest_usable_date`, null or non-positive `spot_ref`, zero quoted strike count, and `atm_bracketed=false`; adversarial tests were added. Closure still requires review/commit. Do not commit this probe slice, run a Tushare probe, fetch option data, build the IV feed, wire Slice B, change production scoring, claim IV coverage, ship-gate evidence, real-money readiness, or use IV status in manual trading until this Required item receives repair + independent review PASS.

A-short IV feed probe invalid-as_of / latest-quote false-positive gates (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Claude `审查` PASS; severity `P0`; scope `docs/a_short_iv_feed_design_20260610.md`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_iv_feed_probe_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`; PIT label `IV-feed BS-inversion computability probe / as_of validity and latest valuation quote availability`; Required ID `R-AIV-PROBE-ASOF-LATEST-QUOTE-GATES`): Codex re-review on 2026-06-10 found the latest IV-feed probe repair fixed the six prior PIT/date-quality probes, but still overclaims `computable=true` in two as-of-critical cases. First, `as_of` itself is not validated as a real calendar date: `as_of=20260631` produces `computable=true` with empty reasons, and the JSON schema accepts the resulting summary because `as_of` is only constrained to eight digits. Second, the probe returns `computable=true` when the latest common PIT date has zero valid option quotes: valid quotes on only the first 15 of 20 common PIT days satisfy `valid_quote_days`, while `spot_ref` is taken from the latest common date and ATM bracketing uses valid quote contracts collected across the whole window. Required repair: validate `as_of` as a real YYYYMMDD calendar date in producer and schema/consistency layers; require the latest usable PIT valuation date to have valid option quotes and the two quotable future maturities needed for that date; compute ATM bracketing from contracts quoted on that latest usable valuation date, not stale contracts seen anywhere in the window; add adversarial tests for invalid calendar `as_of` and latest-common-date zero quotes / stale valid-code bracketing. Do not commit this probe slice, run a Tushare probe, fetch option data, build the IV feed, wire Slice B, change production scoring, claim IV coverage, ship-gate evidence, real-money readiness, or use IV status in manual trading until this Required item receives repair + independent review PASS.

A-short IV feed probe PIT/date-quality false-positive gates (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Claude `审查` PASS; severity `P0`; scope `docs/a_short_iv_feed_design_20260610.md`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_iv_feed_probe_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`; PIT label `IV-feed BS-inversion computability probe / market-data PIT and date-quality`; Required ID `R-AIV-PROBE-PIT-DATE-QUALITY-GATES`): Codex re-review on 2026-06-10 found the latest IV-feed probe repair fixed the prior one-quote / expired-maturity / disjoint-date examples, but still overclaims `computable=true` under stronger adversarial data-quality and PIT cases. A-short Slice A theme-overlay runner eligibility / crowding / schema contract gaps (status `resolved`; severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py`, `schemas/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.schema.json`, `presets/a_short_theme_overlay_governance_20260610.json`, `tests/test_a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py`, `tests/schema/test_a_short_theme_overlay_comparison_schema.py`; PIT label `comparison-track scoring/schema contract / no market-data PIT`; Required IDs `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-ELIGIBILITY-BONUS-GATE`, `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-CROWDING-HARD-GATE`, `R-ASLICEA-SCHEMA-OUTPUT-INVARIANTS`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found the uncommitted Slice A runner/schema slice is not commit-safe or execution-safe. `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-ELIGIBILITY-BONUS-GATE`: the committed Slice A design requires at least 2 of `theme_heat / industry_heat / breadth` plus `fit_pass` before any theme/industry bonus; otherwise overlay must be `esp+l4` only. The runner computes `eligible`, but gates `theme_eff` and `industry_term` only on `fit_pass`; an independent probe produced `eligible=false` with an extra `35.0` points of theme/industry bonus. `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-CROWDING-HARD-GATE`: the design invariant says hard-veto/crowding means heat bonus cannot rescue the candidate, but current crowding handling only multiplies the already-bonused `overlay_raw` by `0.5`, and tests do not prove high heat cannot rescue. `R-ASLICEA-SCHEMA-OUTPUT-INVARIANTS`: the schema allows unpinned threshold-like extras under `thresholds` and does not enforce `candidate_count == len(candidates)`; an independent probe showed both mutations still validate. Required repair: use a single bonus gate equivalent to `eligible` (plus any clarified hard-veto/crowding risk-family condition) for both theme and industry terms; add high-heat adversarial tests for ineligible and crowding/hard-veto cases; close or const-pin every emitted threshold/governance key; add producer-side summary consistency checks for candidate count and rank uniqueness/contiguity with adversarial tests. Do not commit this runner slice, wire data loading, run overlay comparison, fetch data, modify `egs_main.py` / production `final_score` / `tier` / admission, claim production / ship-gate / buy-advice readiness, or use the overlay for manual trading until these Required items receive repair + independent review PASS. Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-10): introduced one `bonus_gate = eligible ∧ ¬crowding_hit` (`eligible = fit_pass ∧ ≥2 of theme/industry/breadth`); both theme and industry terms are zeroed unless `bonus_gate`, so an ineligible row scores `esp+l4` base (R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-ELIGIBILITY-BONUS-GATE). Crowding now STRIPS the heat bonus (removed the `×0.5` demote) so a crowded high-heat row scores exactly base — heat cannot rescue it (R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-CROWDING-HARD-GATE). Schema `thresholds` is now `additionalProperties:false` with every key const-pinned incl. `theme_window_blend`, and `validate_overlay_summary_consistency` enforces `candidate_count == len(candidates)` + unique-contiguous `overlay_rank` (R-ASLICEA-SCHEMA-OUTPUT-INVARIANTS). Design doc §2 + governance `thresholds` aligned to the runner. Verification: overlay unit+schema 28/28, route-doc guard 14/14, py_compile OK; independent mutation probes pass. Comparison-track / non-production / no fetch / V14.2 frozen unchanged. Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-10 (no Required; two wording-only Optionals fixed at commit). Resolved on Claude `提交` of the Slice A runner repair (local master, no push). The runner is commit-safe but NOT execution-safe: data-loading wiring + any forward run remain a user-authorized `执行` step.

A-short Slice A theme-overlay computation contract gaps (status `resolved`; severity `P1`; scope `docs/a_short_theme_overlay_slice_a_design_20260610.md` and future Slice A runner/governance/schema tests; PIT label `overlay scoring contract / no market-data PIT`; Required IDs `R-ASLICEA-INDUSTRY-ORTHO-SCALE`, `R-ASLICEA-FIT-GATE-CONSISTENCY`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found the Slice A design is not commit-safe yet. `R-ASLICEA-INDUSTRY-ORTHO-SCALE`: the spec defines `industry_heat_score` as 0-100, then says `industry_heat⊥` is a regression residual used directly in `overlay_raw` with a 0.15 weight, but does not define whether the residual is re-ranked / clipped / shifted back onto the same 0-100 scale. This makes the frozen weight mathematically unstable and leaves the runner free to use raw residual units. Required repair: define the exact post-orthogonalization scale before weighting, preferably residualize cross-sectionally and then percentile-rank / normalize `industry_heat⊥` to 0-100, or explicitly choose a bounded exposure-cap method; add the corresponding output field / invariant wording for the runner sub-slice. `R-ASLICEA-FIT-GATE-CONSISTENCY`: the formula says theme/industry bonus is zero unless `fit != unknown` and `fit >= fit_floor`, but the "赛道红利资格门槛" only requires `fit != unknown`, and the output flags mention `fit_unknown` but not `fit_pass`. Required repair: make the gate one definition everywhere, e.g. `fit_pass = fit != unknown AND fit >= fit_floor`, use `fit_pass` for both theme and industry bonus eligibility, and include the flag in the overlay artifact/test checklist. A-long large-cap batch signal-search runner coverage/audit gaps (status `resolved`; severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search.py`, `schemas/a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search_execution_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search.py`, `tests/schema/test_a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search_schema.py`; PIT label `primary cohort construction / execution-summary diagnostics / no market-data PIT`; Required IDs `R-BATCH-RUNNER-SIZE-COVERAGE`, `R-BATCH-RUNNER-DIAGNOSTIC-AUDIT`): Codex review on 2026-06-09 found the new batch runner slice is not safe to commit or execute before two material gaps are fixed. `R-BATCH-RUNNER-SIZE-COVERAGE`: the preregistration freezes top-500 size buckets with `minimum_size_bucket_count_for_primary_percentile = 50` and says the size coverage gate applies to cohort-forming months, but the runner only skips scoring inside a thin bucket and still forms a primary cohort when the remaining scored names are >= 10. An independent probe with one missing size bucket and four healthy buckets still formed one primary cohort, which can contaminate the `industry_size_neutral` primary decision with a residual-size subset. Required repair: add per-factor/as-of size coverage diagnostics and hard cohort gating so any primary-hypothesis month whose actual factor observations or size-neutral scored observations have any market-cap quintile below 50 is excluded from that hypothesis's primary cohort and reported; add schema/consistency requirements and adversarial tests proving a missing/thin bucket cannot form a primary cohort. `R-BATCH-RUNNER-DIAGNOSTIC-AUDIT`: the preregistration requires sorted BH-FDR primary p-values and per-factor input-coverage counts, but the summary currently records only thresholds / FDR booleans and lacks separate missing / non-positive / insufficient-TTM counters for the value factors (`book_to_circ_mv`, `cash_flow_to_circ_mv`, `sales_to_circ_mv`). Required repair: emit a schema-required sorted BH audit table (factor_id, primary_cell_id, p_value, rank, q=0.10 / q=0.05 thresholds, survives flags) and per-factor raw-input exclusion / availability counters for all nine factors, with adversarial schema/internal-consistency tests. Do not commit this runner slice, run signal search, spend the singleton ledger, fetch data, change the frozen factor/q/universe/threshold design after seeing results, or claim alpha / production / ship-gate / full-size / DataHub / broker readiness before these receive independent review PASS. Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-09; Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-09; pending Claude `提交`): `R-BATCH-RUNNER-SIZE-COVERAGE` — added a per-factor/per-as_of market-cap-quintile coverage pass in `monthly_cohort_rows` (counts each factor's RAW observations per quintile; a month with zero observations OR any quintile below `minimum_size_bucket_count_for_primary_percentile = 50` is recorded as a no-observation / incomplete-coverage month for that factor) and a HARD cohort gate: `cohort_excess_by_as_of` regained an `excluded_as_ofs` parameter and `summarize_results` excludes each factor's flagged months from its size-dependent views (`industry_size_neutral`, `size_neutral`); `validate_pipeline_result_sanity` now raises if any excluded month leaks into a primary cohort; a new `size_coverage_audit` summary section (per-factor observation / no-observation / incomplete / excluded month counts + lists + primary-cohort month count) is schema-required and consistency-checked. Independent re-probe basis: a month with one absent/thin quintile can no longer form that factor's primary cohort. `R-BATCH-RUNNER-DIAGNOSTIC-AUDIT` — `batch_factor_values` now records a per-factor raw-input outcome (`available` / `missing_input` / `non_positive` / `insufficient_ttm` / `insufficient_window`) for all nine factors (value factors get separate missing vs non-positive vs insufficient-TTM counts), surfaced as a schema-required `factor_input_coverage` section; and a schema-required `fdr_audit` section emits the sorted BH-FDR primary table (rank, factor_id, primary_cell_id, p_value, q=0.10 / q=0.05 BH critical values, survives flags) whose survival flags are consistency-checked against `factor_results`. Optional addressed: the composite primary score is now the family-equal-weight blend of already-neutral percentiles written directly (no re-neutralization). Verification: batch runner + schema tests 62/62 (was 52; +10 coverage/audit/leak/input tests incl. adversarial schema rejects of dropped coverage rows, count mismatches, dropped input-coverage factor, FDR survival disagreement, duplicate rank); a monkeypatched `build_summary`→schema structure test still validates the full emitted summary (now incl. the three new sections); full suite 1193/1193 (was 1183); route-doc guard 11/11; schema regenerated from the runner so the new-section pins match. After commit `e6aa705`, the amended materialization runner passed shape with 23,718 endpoint results, 3 new Tushare calls, 23,715 reused payloads, and all 14 table rollups passed. After commit `bf16a7d`, the amended full audit passed with 12/12 self-tests, six check results pass/characterized, and zero network calls. After commit `1623761`, the pre-run ROE / veto / exit-policy repair passed review and was committed. After commit `498ab99`, the gate-reader repair passed review and was committed. After commit `2214648`, the first valid frozen signal search executed and wrote `research/results/a_long_signal_search_20260604/execution_summary.json` with `no_alpha_found_under_frozen_rules`, candidate count 0, 32 cells, 417,407 evaluated rows, clean hygiene, and full restatement-exclusion application. The ledger is spent as failed threshold.
- Accepted calibration: the fixed-panel route proof, old full raw materialization, and old repaired full audit are real historical data-readiness evidence, but they are not alpha evidence and no longer certify the amended signal runner. The 1,504 ambiguous same-ann-date groups must still be excluded from future signal inputs through `restatement_ambiguous_exclusions.csv`; the future signal runner must abort if the CSV is missing or unapplied. The total-return probe proves the existing account cannot support same-anchor total-return benchmark open, but it did prove close-only total-return rows for the preferred CSI series in the small probe. Raw stock price returns are not acceptable because split / bonus / transfer / rights events create signal-correlated discontinuities. Derived total-return-open construction and price-index fallback remain forbidden. Monthly long-horizon cohort count is not independent N, YTD cumulative fundamentals cannot be mixed across quarter lengths for earnings stability, and any selection-time ST / delisting-name veto must be PIT `namechange` based.
- Required next action: independent review of the real signal-search result summary, singleton ledger update, and post-execution test update. Do not rerun or rescue this failed A-long search. Any future A-long signal search requires a new reviewed preregistration and ledger update with no silent changes to families, thresholds, horizons, universe, cost model, benchmark policy, or industry policy.
- Closure requirement: the original data-route blocker closure condition is satisfied by the valid research-only summary plus singleton ledger update; after review, close this entry as a failed frozen alpha test, not as production / ship-gate / full-size evidence.

### SR-RESOURCE-001 - DataHub broad jobs lack code-level local resource budget enforcement

- Severity: P2
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 7c DataHub / local execution stability
- Evidence: `docs/datahub_design.md` accepts a future shared DataHub for four subsystems, but before this reviewed slice there was no contract requiring slice-first local execution, partitioned reads, lazy materialization, incremental cache reuse, checkpoint / resume, or explicit user approval before all-market / all-lane / full-refresh jobs.
- Accepted calibration: this is not proof that the user's machine cannot run the system. The intended operating mode does not require all four systems to run at once. The risk is future implementation drift: a DataHub or runner slice could accidentally make full-system refresh the default and overload local execution or make review / debugging unstable.
- Mitigation evidence: reviewed slices add `schemas/datahub_local_resource_budget.schema.json`, `docs/datahub_local_resource_budget_contract_20260602.json`, `schemas/datahub_job_spec.schema.json`, and `schemas/examples/datahub_job_spec.example.json`, and route them through `docs/datahub_design.md`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/CURRENT.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and the Phase 7 handoff. The contracts require default `single_slice_incremental` behavior, a declared job spec, market/lane consistency (`A` only with `a_*`, `US` only with `us_*`), reviewed `max_concurrent_lanes <= 2`, and blocks default all-system / all-market / all-lane / full-refresh runs.
- Closure evidence: `engine/datahub/job_spec_contract.py` now provides the code-level validation boundary for future DataHub / runner / report job specs. It validates the job spec against `schemas/datahub_job_spec.schema.json`, validates the referenced resource-budget contract against `schemas/datahub_local_resource_budget.schema.json`, checks the selected budget profile against the resource-budget artifact, enforces market/lane consistency, validates `as_of_date` inside a bounded date window, rejects `single_as_of` windows that are not exactly one day, requires reviewed executable jobs to have no blocking approval gates, keeps heavy profiles tied to explicit approval, and rejects provider calls, DataHub implementation authorization, runner changes, production-consumption claims, ship-gate claims, full-market refresh, all-lane runs, raw payload writes, and other scope creep.
- Required next action: no active `SR-RESOURCE-001` action. Every future Phase 7c DataHub table, adapter, runner, report job, or executable job spec must call `validate_datahub_job_spec_contract` / `validate_datahub_job_spec_file` before execution; if a future schema version intentionally authorizes broader work, update this validator and tests in the same reviewed slice.
- Verification: `tests.schema.test_datahub_local_resource_budget_schema` validates the resource-budget schema / artifact, default slice-first behavior, `max_concurrent_lanes <= 2`, budget profiles, implementation gates, and scope-creep rejection. `tests.schema.test_datahub_job_spec_schema` validates the job-spec schema / example, one-market / one-lane partitioning, market/lane mismatch rejection, resource estimates, execution policy locks, approval gates, and scope-creep rejection. `tests.schema.test_datahub_job_spec_contract` validates the runtime helper, reviewed executable job behavior, heavy approval checks, profile / resource-budget matching, date-window invariants, blocking-gate rejection, no-scope-creep enforcement, resource-budget lane-concurrency bounds, and non-mutation.

### SR-EXEC-001 - Historical weekly screening can contaminate official outputs

- Severity: P0
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short operation / Phase 6b maintenance
- Evidence: `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` calls `A-EGS/egs_main.py --as-of $AsOf` without `--l3-mode`; `A-EGS/egs_main.py` defaults `--l3-mode` to `today` and writes official `result/a_short/<trade_date>/` outputs by default.
- Accepted calibration: `pit` lookup itself uses the effective as-of date after `set_asof`; the risk is the weekly script defaulting to `today` for historical `-AsOf` and allowing official-output overwrite.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` so historical `-AsOf` runs must pass `-L3Mode pit` or `-L3Mode neutralize`, reject `-L3Mode today`, pass `--l3-pit-strict` for PIT mode, and refuse to overwrite existing `result/a_short/<AsOf>/` or `A-EGS/Result/egs_*_<AsOf>` official outputs without `-AllowHistoricalOverwrite`.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_weekly_screening_guardrails` covers missing historical L3 mode, rejected historical `today` mode, existing official-output overwrite refusal, and strict PIT argument wiring.
